Used by `orderSvc.js` (guest menus, `itemOffAt()` for slots and order placement), `routes/orders.js` (422 on save) and `OrderingSettings.jsx` (live check).
- `admin/src/components/website-builder/blockTree.js` — page-builder block tree helpers. `SLOT_FIELD` names each container block's slot list (`columns` → `data.columns`, `tabs` → `data.tabs`); every helper goes through `slotsOf()`. `tabKey()` / `parentKey()` build drop ids (`tab:` / `col:`). Tabs block: `editors/TabsEditor.jsx`, `TabsCanvas` in `canvas/blockCanvas.jsx`, `views/site/blocks/tabs.eta`.
- `shared/headingBlock.js` (repo root) — the website Heading block's styles: `headingLayout()`, `styleString()`, `styleObject()`, `HEADING_DEFAULTS`. Used by `views/site/blocks/heading.eta` (as `it.siteBlocks`, added to every site render by `renderSite()` in `siteRenderer.js`) and `HeadingCanvas` in `canvas/blockCanvas.jsx`, so the builder preview and the site share one implementation.
- `shared/fonts.js` (repo root) — the admin's Google Fonts list (`FONT_OPTIONS`), per-font weights (`FONT_WEIGHTS`), `fontStack()` and `googleFontsUrl()`. Used by the website builder (`FontPicker.jsx`, `Website.jsx`, `SiteBlockEditors.jsx`, `ReservationsWidget.jsx`) and the menu designer/print (`menuLayout.js`, `menus.js` Zod enums). The API-side website renderers (`head.eta`, `siteDataSvc.js`, `scrolling_text.eta`, `reservations_widget.eta`, `canvas/siteBlocks.jsx`) still keep their own weight tables.
- `src/services/orderSvc.js` — Web ordering (migration 122): the one place orders are priced (via `shared/orderPricing.js`), created, moved through statuses (`TRANSITIONS`), paid (`applyPaymentResult()`, idempotent, refunds a duplicate payment automatically), refunded (`refundOrder()`) and expired (`expireStaleOrders()`, run by the hold sweep in `jobs/queues.js`). Also collection slots (`computeSlots()`, computed never stored) and venue-local time helpers (`zonedToUtc()`/`localParts()`). `routes/orderApi.js` (public, `/order-api`) and `routes/orders.js` (admin, `/api/orders`) only call into it.
- `src/services/paymentGateways/` — pluggable payment gateways for web ordering. `index.js` documents the interface (`start`, `confirmFromClient`, `parseWebhook`, `refund`, `cancel`, `isAvailable`) and holds the registry; ships `pay_at_venue` and `demo` (pre-prod pretend card payment). Adding Stripe = one file + one registry line + one `paymentPanel()` branch in `views/site/shared/ordering.eta`. Webhooks: `POST /webhooks/payments/:gateway` (`paymentWebhookRoutes` in `orderApi.js`, raw body).
- `src/routes/orderApi.js` — public web-ordering API (`/order-api`): venue menu + settings, slots, quote, place order, order status by public token, payment retry/confirm; also serves `shared/orderPricing.js` as `/order-api/pricing.js` for the guest page.
- `src/routes/orders.js` — admin web-ordering API (`/api/orders`): board list, detail, status/promised/mark-paid/refund, settings, pause, sold out (`ordering_item_availability`), report, transactions (`GET /transactions[.csv]`, from `services/onlineSalesSvc.js`). Modules `web_orders` / `web_ordering_setup`.
- `src/services/onlineSalesSvc.js` — the online sales transactions report: `onlineTransactions()` (own orders + `order_payments` and `gloriafood_orders` for a venue and range, each with a tender key: `ownTenderKey()` / `gfTenderKey()`, GloriaFood on the link's trading day via `tradingDay()`, totals by tender and source) and `transactionsCsv()`. Admin: `components/orders/TransactionsReport.jsx` (Online orders → Transactions).
- `src/jobs/orderEmailWorker.js` — order emails (confirmation with VAT breakdown, ready, cancelled), `order_email` jobs on `notificationQueue`, same venue provider as bookings via `emailCredentials()` in `emailSvc.js`.
- `src/views/site/shared/ordering.eta` — the guest ordering page (menu, dish options, basket, checkout, payment step) and order status page, vanilla JS module; each template's `order.eta` wraps it with the site header/footer. Site routes `/order`, `/locations/:slug/order`, `/order/status/:token` in `siteRenderer.js`.
- `src/services/platformHost.js` — `platformHostKind(host)` (`apex` | `office` | `ops` | `pos` | `kitchen` | `kiosk` | `kds` | `screens` | `installer` | null), `officeOrigin()`, `opsOrigin()`, `installerOrigin()`, `deviceOrigin(kind)` (the address a till / kitchen screen / kiosk opens), `DEVICE_KIND_OF_HOST`: the platform's own hosts on `PUBLIC_ROOT_DOMAIN`. Admin mirror: `admin/src/lib/hosts.js` (`IS_OPS_HOST`, `IS_OFFICE_HOST`, `mobileHref()`, `standardHref()`).
- `src/routes/platformSite.js` — the apex (macaroonie.com): global `onRequest` hook (`fastify-plugin`, registered before every route plugin) serving the platform page (`views/platform/landing.eta`), a self-removing `/sw.js`, and 301-ing any other GET to office.; API paths pass through. Also `POST /api/platform-interest` (public register-interest form, migration 138). Platform-admin list/update/delete is in `routes/platform.js` (`/api/platform/interest`), UI `components/platform/InterestPanel.jsx` on the Platform page.
- `scripts/nginx-custom-domain.sh <domain> [other names]` — switches a tenant's custom domain on: writes `sites-available/macaroonie-domain-<domain>` (everything proxied to the API), certificate via `certbot certonly --webroot` (HTTP-only block left on if it fails; https kept for the main name when only an added name fails), other names redirect, `REMOVE=1` (+ `DELETE_CERT=1`) / `DRY_RUN=1`, one run at a time (flock). The admin reads that file for `server_status` (`domainServerStatus()` in `routes/website.js`, also `cleanCustomDomain()` and `platformIps()`).
- `scripts/custom-domains-sync.sh` + `scripts/install-domain-sync.sh` — the domain sync: installed by every deploy as `/usr/local/sbin/macaroonie-domains-sync` (+ the domain script as `/usr/local/sbin/macaroonie-domain`), systemd `macaroonie-domains.timer` (every minute) and `.path` (runs when the API writes `/var/lib/macaroonie-domains/kick`). Switches on verified domains (DNS precheck, www / bare twin, backoff), removes blocks nobody has saved, writes `<domain>.json` + `last-run`. API side: `domainSyncInfo()` / `kickDomainSync()` / `domainServerFields()` in `routes/website.js` (`server_auto`, `server_state`); admin `DomainServerNotice` in `Website.jsx`.
- `scripts/ensure-nginx-ai-timeout.sh` — run by every deploy: puts `location ^~ /api/ai/` (proxy timeouts 300 s, buffering off, the block's own proxy_pass and limit_req) in front of each `location /api/` block, so a Pepper turn over a minute isn't cut off with a 504; `nginx -t` or restore. It and `ensure-nginx-media-upload.sh` look for their own block since the previous `/api/` block, not in a fixed window.
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — on every pull request and push to main: migrates an empty Postgres 16 (no pg_cron) from 001, again (expects up to date), starts the API and waits for `/api/health`; plus `node --test` of `shared/test` and `shared/pos/test`; plus job `api-tests` (its own `macaroonie_test` database, `npm test` in `api/`). Separate from the deploy workflow.
- `api/test/` — the API tests (`npm test` in `api/`, Node's test runner, one file at a time, against `buildApp()` with `app.inject()`; they empty the database, so `DATABASE_URL` must name one with `test` in it). `support/register.mjs` + `loader.mjs` swap `src/middleware/auth.js` for `support/auth-stub.mjs` (`Bearer test:<sub>` + `X-Tenant-Id`, the real permission code); `support/app.mjs` (`getApp()`, `as(sub, tenantId)`, `ok()`, `closeApp()`); `support/seed.mjs` (`resetAndSeed()`: two tenants through `POST /api/platform/tenants`, people per role); `support/fixtures.mjs` (`fillTenant(sub, tenantId, { mark, slug })`: data in every area through the API, returns `{ ids, failed, writes }`); `support/fake-claude.mjs` (stand-in Claude API, streamed or JSON). `tenant-sweep.test.mjs`: the cross-tenant sweep (every route with params as tenant B with A's ids and B's mixed in; every write A made sent again as B, also with B's ids swapped into the address; no row of B's may hold an id of A's). `ai-chat.test.mjs`: Pepper's chat loop. `system-paths.test.mjs`: every path that finds its tenant itself (system mode, migration 176). `kitchen-flows.test.mjs`, `gloriafood-kitchen.test.mjs`. The tests' database must belong to a role that isn't a superuser (superusers skip forced RLS). See Docs.jsx → Deployment → Testing.
- `src/middleware/venue.js` — `requireOwnVenue(param)` (a venue id in the URL must be the caller's) and `refuseOtherTenantsVenues` (added to every route by the `onRoute` hook in `app.js`, last among its preHandlers: any `venue_id` / `*_venue_id` / `venue_ids` key in the query or JSON body, at any depth, naming another tenant's venue = 404).
- `scripts/ensure-nginx-http2.sh` — run by every deploy (with `ensure-nginx-manage.sh` and `ensure-nginx-spa-cache.sh`): adds `http2` to `listen ... ssl` lines that lack it on an address where another block already has it, so nginx stops warning "protocol options redefined"; `nginx -t` or restore, then reload.
- `scripts/nginx-office-ops.sh` / `scripts/nginx-apex-landing.sh` — one-off server steps: office. + ops. server blocks with HTTPS (reuses a certificate covering both names, e.g. the wildcard, else `certbot certonly --webroot`; certbot never edits the file), then switching the apex `location /` to the API. See Docs.jsx → Deployment → Moving to office / ops.
- `src/routes/publicSite.js` — Public JSON: `GET /api/site/:slug` bundles config + gallery + pages + menus + hours + allergens + venue. Also serves `sitemap.xml` / `robots.txt`.
- `src/routes/siteRenderer.js` — Subdomain + custom-domain SSR. `resolveSiteHost(host)` returns `{ slug, customDomain }` or null. Renders `views/site/templates/{template_key}/{view}.eta`. Reserved subdomains (api, www, mail, …) bypass.
- `src/services/siteDataSvc.js` — `loadSiteBundle({ slug, customDomain })`. Global lookup → `withTenant(tenant_id, …)` for scoped rows. Checks `is_published` AND `custom_domain_verified` on the custom-domain path.
- `src/services/storageSvc.js` — Pluggable uploads. `getStorage().put(tenantId, kind, ext, mimetype, buffer)`. Drivers: `LocalStorage` (writes to `UPLOAD_DIR`, served at `/uploads/*` by `@fastify/static`), `S3Storage` (lazy-imports `@aws-sdk/client-s3`, compatible with AWS S3, DO Spaces via `S3_ENDPOINT`, Cloudflare R2). Selected via `STORAGE_DRIVER` env.
- `src/views/site/shared/head.eta` — Central theme renderer. Converts `website_config.theme` JSONB into CSS custom properties (`--c-*` for colours, `--f-*` for fonts, `--r-*` for radii, etc) and auto-loads Google Fonts. Every template includes it.
- `src/views/site/templates/classic/`, `src/views/site/templates/modern/` — the two shipping public-site templates. Each has `index.eta`, `menu.eta`, `page.eta`, `partials/{header,footer}.eta`.
- `src/routes/slots.js` — Calls `get_available_slots()` PG function. Thin wrapper.
- `src/routes/emailTemplates.js` — Email template CRUD + venue email settings + merge-fields endpoint + preview renderer + delivery log query + `POST /send-test` (sends a real email through the venue's configured provider against sample merge fields, used by the Widget Test page to verify setup without creating a booking). Mounted at `/api/email-templates`.
- `src/routes/widgetApi.js` — Public booking-widget API (`/widget-api/*`). No auth, CORS-allow-all, scoped per `venueId`. Mirrors `/api/bookings` but resolves tenant from venue lookup (`resolveTenant(venueId)` returns `{ id, tenant_id, ... }`). Free-booking only for v1 — deposit-required venues throw 422 on confirm. Rate limit shared with the global `@fastify/rate-limit` config.
- `src/views/site/widget.eta` — Standalone iframe-friendly booking widget page. Vanilla JS, ~400 lines, 5-step flow (covers/date/slot/details/confirmed). Reads `?theme=light|dark&accent=hex` URL params. Posts `macaroonie:booking-confirmed` message to parent on success. Uses `X-Frame-Options: ALLOWALL` + CSP `frame-ancestors *` so any tenant site can embed.
- `src/routes/media.js` — Per-tenant media library API. Mounted at `/api/media`. Routes: `/categories` CRUD; `/items` (list with `category_id`/`scope`/`search` filters), `/items/scopes` (DISTINCT scope values for dropdown), `/items/check-duplicate` (filename + hash within scope), `/items/upload` (multipart, validates MIME, takes `.ico` favicons through `utils/icon.js` like `/website/upload` does, hashes content, optionally extracts dimensions via `sharp`), `/items/:id/replace` (multipart, **swaps file behind an existing item** — used by the image editor's "Replace original" path; keeps row id, updates url/storage_key/bytes/dimensions/hash, deletes old file best-effort), `/items/:id` PATCH/DELETE, `/items/bulk` (delete / move-category / move-scope). Storage goes through existing `storageSvc` with kind `'media'`. `sharp` is lazy-loaded — listed as `optionalDependencies` so a broken native build doesn't crash the API; missing it just means no width/height capture.
- `src/routes/emailMonitoring.js` — SendGrid monitoring API. `GET /summary` returns stats + suppression lists + local email_log totals in one round-trip. `GET /stats` daily breakdown. `GET /suppressions/:type` (bounces / blocks / spam_reports / invalid_emails). `DELETE /suppressions/:type/:email` (owner-only — clears a specific entry so retries can flow). `GET /log` paginates local email_log. Uses `loadSendgridKey(tenantId, venueId)` to resolve the per-venue key with env fallback. Mounted at `/api/email-monitoring`.
- `src/services/sendgridSvc.js` — SendGrid Web API wrapper. `getStats({ apiKey, startDate, endDate })`, `getSuppressions({ apiKey, type, limit })`, `removeSuppression({ apiKey, type, email })`, `pingApiKey({ apiKey })`. All raise `Error.status` from the HTTP response so callers can distinguish auth failures from rate limits.
- `src/routes/manageBooking.js` — Public guest-facing booking management. SSR page at `/manage/{token}`. No auth — manage_token UUID IS the auth. GET shows details, POST /modify changes date/time/covers, POST /cancel cancels. Queues modification/cancellation emails.
- `src/services/guestBookingSvc.js` — `rescheduleBooking(booking, { date, time, covers }, { staff, overrideLimits, dryRun })` and `cancelBooking()`, the one implementation of moving / cancelling an existing booking (party size, cutoff for guests only, `get_available_slots()` slot check, keep the table if it fits else `allocateBestFit` without displacement). `overrideLimits` (only with `staff`) goes past the party-size range and the slot's covers limit, falling back to the Unallocated row; `dryRun` returns `{ limits, unallocated, table }` without writing (the staff AI's Confirm card); `partySizeLimit()` / `coversLimit()` word the limits. Used by `manageBooking.js`, the guest AI tools and the staff AI `change_booking`. Also `loadBookingByToken()` / `loadBookingById()`, `canModify()` / `canCancel()` / `cancelBlockReason()`.
- `src/ai/` — AI booking by chat and MCP (migration 148). `context.js`: `makeContext()`, `callApi()` (in-process `app.inject` with the caller's Authorization + `X-Tenant-Id` and `INTERNAL_CALL_HEADER`), `requireStaffPermission()`, `audit()` (ai_actions), `addUsage()` (ai_usage + the person's day in ai_usage_people, in units from `usageUnits()`: tokens priced per model (`PRICES`, $4 per million units), migration 174). `tools/guest.js` + `tools/staff.js` (bookings; `newBookingPlan()` for `override_limits`) + `tools/hs.js` (staff health & safety: `hs_status`, `food_safety_day`, log fridge/hold/cooking/delivery checks, `add_corrective_action`, checklists due/get/tick, H&S action log; `pickCheckTime()`) + `tools/purchasing.js` (staff purchasing: inventory, supplier prices, suppliers, order lists and pars, `import_price_list`, order form, place / send / change / cancel purchase orders, `receive_delivery`; one plan function per change tool shared by `describe()` and `run()`, e.g. `planOrder()`) + `tools/menus.js` (staff menus: `list_menus`, `get_menu`, `find_dishes`, create / change / copy menus, add / change / remove dishes and sections, `change_prices`; every change edits `menuSaveBody()` output and PATCHes the whole tree) + `tools/rota.js` (staff, rota, pay, tips; `rotaPlan()` builds the whole-week PUT) + `tools/cash.js` (Cash Recon day / week, figures, expenses, submit, wages paid, fill from Loyverse / GloriaFood; `recordPlan()` / `wagesPlan()` rebuild whole-day / whole-week bodies) + `tools/orders.js` (online orders, refunds, pause, report, sold out, till day / checks, kitchen report; `findOrder()` by day number or name) + `tools/venue.js` (customers, opening times, closures, tables, booking rules) + `tools/site.js` (website status / publish / banner, promotions, reviews, menu screens, booking emails, issues, feature requests) + `tools/general.js` (`open_page`: a link from `ROUTE_CATALOG` in `routes/nav.js`, only pages the person can view; `how_do_i`: Help passages from `searchHelp()` in `ai/helpIndex.js`, a BM25 index of `admin/src/pages/Help.jsx` rebuilt when the file changes, anchors from `helpSlug()` in `shared/help.js`) + `tools/briefing.js` (`day_briefing`: the reading tools in `PARTS` run as the person, 403 parts listed in `not_included`) + `tools/util.js` (`pickByName()`: id, exact, starts with, contains, every word; `venueFor()`, `venueToday()`, `mondayOf()`, `shortDate()`, `money()`, `audited()`) + `tools/index.js` (registry, Zod -> JSON Schema; every staff tool has a `section` from `SECTIONS`; `CORE_TOOLS` load in every staff chat request, the rest through tool search in `chat.js`; start-up throws on an unknown section, a duplicate name or a missing core tool). The staff assistant is **Pepper**: the name is `ASSISTANT_NAME` in `shared/assistant.js` (prompt, MCP metadata, admin); the guest chat stays neutral. `staffPermissionLevel()` in `context.js` for tools that read across modules. `codes.js` (emailed 6-digit code -> 30-minute access key for guest change/cancel). `chat.js` (`runTurn()` model loop, every call streamed through `callModel()`, events to `emit` (`streamEvents()`); confirm cards `confirmAction()` / `cancelAction()`, `displayItems()`; `withHold()`: one writer per chat across processes via `ai_conversations.running_until`, migration 174; `TURN_MS` / `STREAM_TURN_MS` turn budgets; model routing (migration 175): `startTier()`, `modelOf()`, `upgradeChat()`, `HEAVY_TOOLS`, `LONG_CHAT_TOKENS`; a refused message is withdrawn, `NOTICES`; `nowNote()` + `pageNote()` → `contextNote()`: the date and time now at the venues and the page a drawer message was sent from, stored as one hidden `_context` text block before every staff message, `_page` on older ones). `sse.js` (`wantsStream()`, `eventStream()` (opens on the first event, heartbeat, `X-Accel-Buffering: no`), `errorEvent()`). `settings.js` (ai_settings, monthly usage, `chatUnitsLeft()`, `dailyShare()` / `personUnitsLeft()` (each person's daily share), `peopleUsage()`). `erase.js` (migration 174: `eraseFromAi()`, a customer's GDPR erasure from chats, cards and the audit, closing those chats; `cleanUpAi()`, the hourly `ai-cleanup` job: chat retention, audit details after a year). `prompts.js` (`guestInstructions()`, `staffInstructions()`). `apiKey.js` (migration 149: the Anthropic key, saved on the Platform page in `platform_secrets` else `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`; `activeKey()`, async `chatConfigured()`, `checkKey()`, `saveKey()`, `removeKey()`, `keyInfo()`). `attachments.js` (migration 150: files attached in staff chats; `saveUpload()` (kind from the first bytes, size limits), `claimAttachments()` (own unsent files, per-message / per-conversation limits), `attachmentBlocks()` (stored reference, no data), `loadAttachmentData()` + `withFileData()` (bytes filled in per request), `releaseAttachments()`).
- `src/routes/mcp.js` — MCP connectors, Streamable HTTP, stateless: `POST /mcp` (guests, platform wide) and `POST /mcp/staff/:tenantSlug` (access token or Auth0 JWT, route config `allowAccessToken`; lists only the tools the person can use, `toolAllowed()` + `SECTION_MODULES` in `ai/tools/index.js` with `permissionLevels()` from `auth.js`; a read-only token gets and runs only reading tools, migration 179), plus `/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource/mcp/staff/:slug` (pointing at the connector's own origin).
- `src/routes/mcpOauth.js` — the staff connector's OAuth sign-in (migration 181) for apps that can only connect with OAuth (Claude's custom connectors, ChatGPT): authorization-server metadata, `POST /oauth/register` (dynamic client registration, public clients, `ai_oauth_clients`), `GET /oauth/authorize` (an `ai_oauth_grants` row, then the admin's Connect page), `POST /oauth/token` (code + S256 PKCE -> an ordinary `ai_access_tokens` row), and the Connect page's `/api/ai/oauth/requests/:id[/allow|/deny]`; `publicOrigin()` (also used by `mcp.js`). Admin: `pages/ConnectAi.jsx` (`/connect-ai`, outside the app shell); `main.jsx`'s `RequireAuth` / `onRedirectCallback` bring a login back to the page it started on.
- `src/services/vault.js` — stored integration keys sealed (migration 179): `seal()` / `unseal()` (AES-256-GCM, `SECRETS_KEY`, values `v1:...`), `SEALED` (every sealed column), `checkVault()` (production refuses to start without a valid key), `sealStoredSecrets()` (start-up, seals keys saved before 179). `scripts/ensure-secrets-key.sh` (every deploy: adds `SECRETS_KEY` to `api/.env` when missing). Docs.jsx → Stored keys.
- `src/routes/ai.js` — `/api/ai`: status, conversations, chat, confirm/cancel cards, settings, access tokens, activity, platform limits. `src/routes/aiApi.js` — `/ai-api`: the website chat (public, conversation id + guest_key).
- `src/services/emailSvc.js` — Pluggable email delivery: `sendEmail({ provider, credentials, from, to, subject, html })` with 5 drivers (sendgrid/postmark/mailgun/ses/smtp). Also exports `renderTemplate()` (mustache-style {{field}} replacer with HTML escaping), `buildMergeFields()` (booking+venue+customer→fields object), and `MERGE_FIELDS` (16 available fields with examples).
- `src/services/emailTemplateDefaults.js` — Built-in default HTML email templates (confirmation/reminder/modification/cancellation). Mobile-responsive, professional layout. Used when a tenant hasn't customised their own.
- `src/jobs/emailWorker.js` — BullMQ job processor for booking emails. Loads booking+venue+customer, resolves template (venue→tenant→built-in), resolves provider+credentials, renders, sends, logs to email_log, marks reminder_sent_at.
- `src/routes/platform.js` — Platform admin routes (`/api/me`, `/api/platform/tenants` CRUD + stats). `requirePlatformAdmin` guard. `/api/me` returns user profile + available tenants for org switcher.
- `src/routes/team.js` — In-app team management (`/api/team`). List members, invite (sends Auth0 invitation email), role change (syncs `app_metadata.role` to Auth0), deactivate (removes from Auth0 org), `POST /:userId/reset-password` (Auth0 change-password email). Owner-only for mutations; admin can view. Self-protection guards. Falls back to local-only invites with a warning if `AUTH0_MGMT_*` env vars are unset.
- `src/services/auth0MgmtSvc.js` — Auth0 Management API wrapper. In-memory M2M token cache (refreshes ~5min before expiry), `inviteUserToOrg`, `removeUserFromOrg`, `updateUserAppMetadata` (role sync), `sendPasswordResetEmail`, `createOrganization`, `enableOrgConnection`, `provisionTenantOrg`, `getConnectionByName`. `isConfigured()` / `canInvite()` feature checks let callers degrade gracefully when creds are missing.
- `src/routes/access.js` — Access management (`/api/access`). `GET /modules` lists all known modules merged with the tenant's `tenant_modules` rows. `PATCH /modules/:key` toggles a module on/off (owner only). `GET /roles` returns tenant_roles with permissions. `POST/PATCH/DELETE /roles[/:id]` for custom-role CRUD; built-in roles can be edited but not deleted; custom roles can't be deleted while users reference them.
- `src/config/modules.js` — Single source of truth for the module registry (key, label, description, per-built-in default permissions). Modules include bookings, venues, tables, schedule, rules, customers, website, email_templates, cash_recon, team, settings, dashboard, widget_test, documentation, issue_log, feature_requests, changelog, and (migration 106) staff, rota, rota_pay, rota_dashboard, plus rota_tips (migration 118), plus web_orders and web_ordering_setup (migration 122, group `web_ordering`), plus promotions (migration 131, same group). Export `MODULES`, `MODULE_KEYS`, `PERMISSION_LEVELS`, `permissionAtLeast()`.
- `src/routes/reviews.js` — Customer reviews CRUD. Tenant-scoped (RLS). Three ingestion paths: Apify scraper (`POST /scrape` → BullMQ), manual entry, CSV upload. Moderation via `is_approved` + `is_featured`. Mounted at `/api/reviews`.
- `src/routes/backlog.js` — Platform-admin-only dev backlog. No RLS (global table). Kanban-column `PATCH /:id/move`. Mounted at `/api/backlog`.
- `src/routes/issueLog.js` — Tenant-scoped ITIL issue log. Auto-derives P1–P4 priority from impact × urgency. Platform admin can update status and promote to backlog. Mounted at `/api/issues`.
- `src/routes/featureRequests.js` — Cross-tenant feature request wishlist with upvote toggle (`feature_request_upvotes`). Platform admin sets lifecycle status and can promote. Mounted at `/api/feature-requests`.
- `src/routes/changelog.js` — Changelog entries. Tenants see published only. Platform admin CRUD + publish/unpublish. Mounted at `/api/changelog`.
- `src/routes/inventory.js` / `src/routes/suppliers.js` / `src/routes/purchasing.js` — Purchasing (migration 153, replaced order sheets). `/api/inventory`: categories, items (no price column), supplier quotes (pack + price per pack ex VAT, several per supplier), venue prices, preferred quote (company star / per venue), price history, CSV/Excel import with dry run. `/api/suppliers`: contact, send method (email | print | excel | pdf), cutoff, lead days, delivery days, min order, per-venue overrides. `/api/purchasing`: order lists (mixed suppliers, pars per venue in base units), restaurant orders (draft, autosaved lines, submit splits into one purchase order per supplier; migration 180: request / return / withdraw / split for approval, `orderActions()` puts `can_*` flags on every order, `assertEditable()`, `orderLines()`, `emailNew()`), purchase orders (send by email / mark sent / file pdf|xlsx|html / change quantities and add the supplier's other products while to_send (`PUT /pos/:id/lines` `add`: a product already on the order tops its line up; `GET /venues/:id/catalog?supplier_id=` for the picker) / split for another day / cancel / receive, which upserts `fs_delivery_checks` by `purchase_order_id`), `GET /meta` (`can_order`, `can_approve`). Modules `purchasing` (fill in, send, receive), `purchasing_approve` (place; change quantities, split, cancel) and `purchasing_setup` (catalogue, suppliers, lists; view also shows prices to ordering staff), group `purchasing`. See Docs.jsx → Purchasing (Approval, Splitting).
- `src/services/purchasingSvc.js` — quote loading/choice per venue (`loadItemQuotes()`, `loadPreferred()`, `resolveQuotes()`, `quoteView()`), suppliers with next delivery date (`loadSuppliersForVenue()`), PO numbering (`nextPoNumber()`, advisory lock) and creation (`createPurchaseOrder()`, copies pack/price/VAT onto lines), PO documents (`poHtml()`, `poXlsx()`, `poPdf()`, `poFile()`), `emailPo()` (venue email provider, cc, PDF/Excel attachment, records `send_error`), `seedInventoryCategories()`. `src/services/pdfDoc.js` — small dependency-free PDF writer (Helvetica, WinAnsi). `sendEmail()` in `emailSvc.js` takes `cc` and `attachments` for every provider.
- `shared/procurement.js` (repo root) — purchasing maths for API and admin: `UNITS`, `packBaseQty()`, `packLabel()`, `formatBase()`, `unitPrice()`, `quotePrice()`, `chooseQuote()`, `fitQty()`, `qtyProblem()`, `packsToPar()`, `lineAmounts()`, `orderTotals()`, `supplierForVenue()`, `nextDeliveryDate()`, `minutesToCutoff()`, `deliveryTempOk()`, `receiveStatusFor()`, `DELIVERY_TEMP_MAX`, `poNumberLabel()`.
- `src/routes/rota.js` — Staff & Rota API (migration 106), mounted at `/api/rota`. Tenant-wide setup (`GET /setup`, `PATCH /settings`, shifts + roles CRUD/reorder), per-venue staff CRUD/reorder (moved here from `cashRecon.js`'s old `/config/staff*`), weekly rota (`GET/PUT .../weeks/:week[/entries]`, `POST .../copy`; the PUT's `removed_staff_ids` takes people off one week, migration 155), leaving dates and off-rota periods (`PUT .../staff/:id/leaving`, `POST`/`DELETE .../staff/:id/off-periods`, `loadOffDays()`, migration 164), and pay/tips (`GET .../pay`, per-person pay override, pay adjustments (`.../pay-adjustments`, migration 137: extra, deduction, cash advance with an automatic next-week repayment), zero-sum `move-points` (points) and `move-tips` (£, migration 109), `reset-moves`, `fill-wages` into `cash_wage_entries`). Gated by `staff`, `rota`, `rota_pay` (hours and pay, fill wages) and `rota_tips` (pots, shares, moves; migration 118) modules; the pay payload is redacted per caller by `redactWeek()`. See Docs.jsx's Staff & Rota section.
- `src/services/openDays.js` — `resolveOpenDaysForWeek(tx, tenantId, venueId, dates)`: which dates a venue is open (exception → date override → weekly template). Shared by Cash Recon's week-detail and the Rota's `GET .../weeks/:week` (`open_dates`).
- `src/routes/hsSettings.js` — tenant-wide H&S settings (`/api/hs-settings`, migration 124): `GET ?venue_id=&date=` → `{ follow_opening_days, closed }`, `PATCH { follow_opening_days }` (owner/admin). With the setting on, `openDays.js`'s `hsClosedDates()` drives `checklists.js` `/due` (`not_required` on daily templates) and `dashboardTiles.js` `computeHsStatus()` (closed venue-days expect no daily checklists or fridge/hold/cooking checks; status `closed`).
- `src/services/rotaCalc.js` — `computeRotaWeek()`: the one implementation of rota hours, pay, tip points and tip shares (pure, no DB). Both `GET .../pay` and `POST .../fill-wages` call it.
- `src/routes/testData.js` — Pre-prod QA tooling. `POST /:venueId/test-data/seed` generates synthetic bookings ("Test Guest N") across a date range at a target per-sitting occupancy %, using the same exception→override→template sitting-resolution cascade as `schedules.js`; bypasses holds/Stripe/slot-cap checks like `admin-override`. `POST /:venueId/test-data/clear` bulk-deletes bookings by date range or the whole venue (`dry_run: true` previews the count; `confirm: true` required to actually delete; clears dependent `payments` rows first since that FK is `ON DELETE RESTRICT`). Mounted at `/api/venues`. Gated by the `test_data` module (owner/admin only by default).
- `src/services/hsTestDataSvc.js` — test data for H&S (migration 139): `generateHsData()` fills a past date range for one venue (fridge/freezer temps per check time, hold checks, cooking checks with random published-menu dishes, past purchase orders from the venue's order lists on each supplier's delivery days, one delivery check per received purchase order matched by `purchase_order_id`, checklists/audits completed with every task ticked) and `clearGenerated()` deletes only `is_generated` rows. Routes `POST /api/venues/:venueId/test-data/hs/generate|clear` in `routes/testData.js`; UI in `TestData.jsx`.
- `src/jobs/reviewScrapeWorker.js` — BullMQ worker for Apify Google review scraping jobs. Polls Apify dataset, upserts reviews, marks job complete.
- `src/services/apifySvc.js` — Apify HTTP API wrapper. `startRun(actorId, input)`, `getDataset(datasetId)`, `waitForRun(runId)`. Requires `APIFY_API_TOKEN` env var.
- `src/config/ws.js` — WebSocket server. Rooms keyed by venue_id. Auth via JWT query param.
- `src/services/broadcastSvc.js` — Call `broadcastBooking(type, booking)` after any booking mutation.
- `src/jobs/queues.js` — BullMQ queues. `notificationQueue` for emails, `holdSweepQueue` for hold cleanup.
- `src/routes/foodSafety.js` — SFBB food-safety logging (migrations 076, 077, 090, 142). Equipment (fridge/freezer) temp checks + capture times, deliveries, hot/cold hold checks + stations + capture times, cooking/reheat checks + sessions, cooking items (`/cooking-settings`, `/cooking-items`, picker `GET /cooking/items`), plus `GET /defaults` for built-in target/min/max per equipment type.
- `src/services/cookingItems.js` — `loadCookingPicker(tx, tenantId, venueId)`: the cooking-check dish tabs from published menus, the venue's own list (`fs_cooking_items`) or both (`fs_cooking_settings.item_source`). Used by `GET /food-safety/cooking/items` and `hsTestDataSvc.js`. Mounted at `/api/food-safety`, gated by the `food_safety` module.
- `src/routes/checklists.js` — Operator-defined recurring checklists (migration 083): template CRUD + item CRUD/reorder, `GET /due` (what's due for a venue/date), `GET`/`PUT /instance` (lazy-created tick state per template per date; status `completed` exactly when every active task is ticked, no separate complete step). Mounted at `/api/checklists`, gated by the `checklists` module.
- `src/services/legacyImportSvc.js` — one-off import of the legacy CAFE_MANAGER workbook (checklist sheets incl. the "Monthly Audit" H&S sheet, fridge/freezer, hot food, deliveries) into existing checklist templates and food-safety logs. Used by `routes/legacyImport.js` (Legacy data import page) and `scripts/import-legacy-checklists.js`. Never creates templates; see Docs.jsx's Checklists section for the sheet-to-table map.
- `src/routes/hsDashboards.js` — Customisable dashboards (migrations 086–088, 101, 152): dashboard CRUD + reorder, widget CRUD + reorder, per-widget `col_span`/`height_px` resize. Registered four times in `app.js` — `/api/hs-dashboards` (`kind: 'hs'`, `hs_dashboard`), `/api/cash-dashboards` (`'cash'`, `cash_dashboard`), `/api/rota-dashboards` (`'rota'`, `rota_dashboard`) and `/api/custom-dashboards` (`'custom'`, `custom_dashboard` core module, the blank Dashboards page); each mount filters every query by `hs_dashboards.kind`. Since migration 152 every mount accepts every widget type (`WIDGET_TYPES`, keys of `WIDGET_MODULE`, which maps each type to the module whose data it shows); adding a widget needs view on that module (`permissionLevel()`). A widget is a thin pointer (`widget_type` + optional `checklist_template_id`) at the food-safety/checklist data — no separate per-widget data model. Mounted at `/api/hs-dashboards`, gated by the `hs_dashboard` module.
- `src/routes/hsActionLog.js` — H&S Action Log (migration 092): a general facilities/compliance to-do list per venue, migrated from the legacy spreadsheet's "ActionLog" tab. Category CRUD + reorder (`hs_action_categories` — tenant-managed reorderable list, same pattern as `fs_hold_stations`, not a fixed enum), entry CRUD, dedicated `PATCH /entries/:id/complete` that sets `completed_by`/`completed_at` server-side from the caller's JWT email. Mounted at `/api/hs-action-log`, gated by the `hs_action_log` module.
- `src/routes/dashboardTiles.js` — Overview page tile system (migration 093). `TILE_CATALOG` (static, same "only real known tiles" idea as nav.js's ROUTE_CATALOG) + CRUD + `PATCH /reorder` on `dashboard_tiles`, gated by `requirePermission('dashboard', …)`. `GET /hs-status?from=&to=&today=` returns the combined Checklists + Food safety status per venue per day for the `hs_today_status`/`hs_week_status` tiles, computed by `computeHsStatus()` in `src/services/hsStatus.js` (shared with the staff AI's `hs_status` tool) — see Docs.jsx's Overview Tiles section for the full expected/completed/unresolved derivation and the status-colour rules.
- `src/config/defaultDashboardTiles.js` — `DEFAULT_DASHBOARD_TILES` + `seedDefaultDashboardTiles(tx, tenantId)`, used for brand-new tenants (`POST /api/platform/tenants`), same "one seed function, called from two places" pattern as `defaultNav.js`.
- `src/utils/checklistPeriod.js` — `periodStartFor()`/`mondayOf()`/`monthStartOf()`, factored out of `checklists.js` so `dashboardTiles.js`'s hs-status aggregation can never drift from what `checklists.js` itself considers a template's current period.
- `src/routes/nav.js` — Admin-configurable navigation (migration 091). `ROUTE_CATALOG` (static, so the designer can only point items at real pages) + CRUD on `nav_items` + `PATCH /items/:id/reorder` (siblings) + `PATCH /items/:id/reparent` (indent/outdent — moves to a new parent at an index, re-sequencing both the old and new sibling lists) + `POST /reset` (re-seed via `defaultNav.js`) + `PATCH /style` (tenant-wide `sidebar`/`launcher` toggle). Mounted at `/api/nav`, gated by the `nav_designer` module (owner-only `manage` by default).
- `src/config/defaultNav.js` — `DEFAULT_NAV_TREE` + `seedDefaultNav(tx, tenantId)`. Used for brand-new tenants (`POST /api/platform/tenants`) and the nav designer's "reset to defaults". Migration 091 seeds the same tree once in raw SQL for tenants that already existed — that seed never re-runs, so this file is the only copy that matters going forward; keep them in sync by hand if the default tree changes.

### Admin portal
- `src/components/PageErrorBoundary.jsx` — wraps the page outlet in `AppShell` and `MobileShell` (keyed by path): a page that throws while rendering shows "This page stopped working" with the error and a Reload button instead of blanking the admin.
- `src/hooks/usePermission.js` — `usePermission(moduleKey)` → `{ level, canView, canManage, isOwnerOrAdmin, isOwner, loaded }` from `/me`; `src/components/ReadOnlyNotice.jsx` — `ReadOnlyNotice` (the blue View only bar) and `ReadOnlyFieldset` (native `<fieldset disabled>` around a form). Every module screen uses them to hide or disable changes for View.
- `src/lib/api.js` — `useApi()` hook. Injects Auth0 token automatically. All API calls go through here. `download(path, filename)` method fetches with auth and triggers browser Save dialog.
- `src/hooks/useRealtimeBookings.js` — WS hook. Connects to `/ws?venue=&token=`. Invalidates TanStack Query on push.
- `src/components/TenantGate.jsx` — sits inside `RequireAuth`, after Auth0 identity login. Auto-selects a single-tenant user's only tenant (no picker flash — the loading state is held until the auto-select effect fires); silently re-enters a returning multi-tenant user's last-picked tenant if it's still valid (`localStorage` key `maca_selected_tenant`, checked against `/me`'s `current_tenant`); shows the mandatory `TenantSwitcherModal` (no close/dismiss) only for a genuine "which one?" case — multiple tenants, no valid stored pick.
- `src/components/TenantSwitcherModal.jsx` — shared tenant-picker modal. `TenantGate` renders it mandatory (no `onClose`) for first-choice; `AppShell`'s sidebar header renders it dismissible for voluntary switching. Replaced the old sidebar `<select>` dropdown.
- `src/components/MobileSuggestModal.jsx` — mounted at the bottom of `AppShell` (desktop shell only, never rendered under `MobileShell`). Detects a touch device in portrait at ≤700px (`IS_TOUCH && matchMedia('(max-width: 700px) and (orientation: portrait)')`, checked on mount and on an `orientationchange`-driven media-query listener so a mid-session rotation into portrait also triggers it) and offers a big modal pointing at `/mobile`. Dismissing either button (Open mobile view / Stay on this page) sets `maca_mobile_prompt_dismissed` in localStorage — offered once per browser, never nags again.
- `src/components/MobileViewToggle.jsx` — persistent floating pill button, bottom-left, shown any time the viewport matches `IS_TOUCH && matchMedia('(max-width: 700px)')` (no portrait requirement, no dismissal — unlike `MobileSuggestModal` above, this is an always-on toggle rather than a one-time nudge). Takes a `target` prop (`'mobile'` or `'standard'`) and navigates to `/mobile` or `/` respectively. Mounted twice: `<MobileViewToggle target="mobile" />` in `AppShell.jsx` (next to `MobileSuggestModal`), `<MobileViewToggle target="standard" />` in `MobileShell.jsx` (after `<Outlet />`) — the latter is the only way to get from `/mobile/*` back to the full desktop admin without editing the URL by hand.
- `src/pages/Dashboard.jsx` — "Overview" (home page, `/` route). Tile-based layout (migration 093) — quick_access / stats_today / upcoming_bookings / venues_status / hs_today_status / hs_week_status, rendered via a shared `TileCard` shell mirroring `HSDashboard.jsx`'s `WidgetCard` (header with icon/title, edit-mode reorder/resize/remove controls, scrollable body). Non-managers see `me.dashboard_tiles` (server-filtered by role); an owner/admin's "Customise layout" mode fetches the full unfiltered list from `GET /api/dashboard-tiles` and can additionally set per-tile `hidden_role_ids` via a small "Visible to" modal. The `Shortcuts` component (per-user quick-access picker, `maca_dashboard_shortcuts` localStorage) is unchanged — it's nested inside the `quick_access` tile, a separate customisation axis from tile layout.
- `src/pages/Timeline.jsx` — Gantt view. @dnd-kit for drag. Booking cards positioned by pixel-accurate time offset. Current-time red line indicator (today only, updates every 30 s).
- `src/pages/Schedule.jsx` — 7-day grid + sitting editor + slot caps grid.
- `src/pages/Tables.jsx` — table list with drag-to-reorder (grip handles always visible), combinations, disallowed pairs section.
- `src/pages/Rules.jsx` — booking rules + smart allocation toggles (allow_cross_section_combo, allow_non_adjacent_combo). enable_reconfirmed_status toggle (re-confirmed status for operator phone-call workflow).
- `src/pages/Bookings.jsx` — Guestplan-style time-grouped list. Stats bar (reservations/tables/guests — active only). Inline status dropdown. Phone visible. Permanent resizable right panel (280–700 px). BookingDrawer in inlineMode.
- `src/pages/Customers.jsx` — Customer list + resizable detail panel. GDPR anonymise (double-confirm inline) + export (JSON download via api.download()).
- `src/pages/Website.jsx` — Website builder admin page (`/website` route). Left-rail nav is grouped by job, not table — `TENANT_NAV` (tenant-site level: Home, Pages & modals, Header, Footer, Locations index, Reservations widget, Look & layout (was Brand & theme: the brand itself is on `/brand`, `pages/Brand.jsx`, since migration 196), Domain, SEO, Legal & cookies, Analytics, Emergency banner) and `VENUE_NAV` (per-location: Location page, Pages & modals, Opening hours, Find us, Contact, Gallery, PDF menus, Allergens, Booking, Online ordering, Delivery); `SINGLE_VENUE_NAV` collapses the two for one-venue tenants. Above the menu, `PublishButton` (last published / scheduled time, plus a Staging link beside it that opens `staging-{slug}` in a new tab) opens `PublishModal` (staging link, publish now, schedule, override staging); Domain keeps the address and the Site is live switch. There is no per-venue Identity page any more (removed) — `tenant-brand` renders `BrandSection`, which shows a "Default"-plus-per-venue-pill selector (multi-venue tenants only) over `BrandIdentitySection` + `BrandThemeSection` + `BrandLayoutSection`, each taking an optional `venueId` prop (see the Brand & theme override Completed item). Note `Website.jsx` also has an unreferenced, dead `ThemeSection({config})` function from an earlier refactor, unrelated to any of the above — don't confuse the two when adding a theme field (see Common mistakes). Onboarding card for tenants with no `website_config`/`tenant_site` row yet. Shared primitives: `SectionCard`, `FormRow`, `Toggle`, `SaveBar`, `FileUpload`, `ImageField`, `useConfigFields()` helper. Theme sections save the full `theme` JSONB in one PATCH; each content section saves its own subset.
- `src/pages/Docs.jsx` — in-app technical documentation (auto-synced with codebase).
- `src/pages/Help.jsx` — operator user guide.
- `src/pages/Settings.jsx` — theme colour picker (live CSS var update) + timeline view defaults.
- `src/contexts/SettingsContext.jsx` — `themeHex` state + `hexToHsl`/`fgForHex`/`applyTheme` helpers. Default `#630812`. Persisted to `maca_settings` localStorage key.
- `src/contexts/TimelineSettingsContext.jsx` — `hideInactive`, `groupBySections`, `panelMode`, `venueId`, `refetchTrigger`. All three toggles persisted to `maca_timeline_prefs` localStorage.
- `src/components/bookings/BookingDrawer.jsx` — booking detail side-panel. Save button in header (contextual per edit mode). Table override: individual checkboxes only, pre-populated from member_table_ids; **single-table → `PATCH /relocate`** (cascade displacement), **multi-table → `PATCH /tables`** (direct), **unallocated → `PATCH /tables`**; "Unallocated" checkbox at top of picker (orange); unallocated bookings show orange badge in view mode. **Capacity warning** (amber) shown permanently in table section when `booking.covers` exceeds table/combination max. **End time editor**: "End time" button in Date & time section; `PATCH /bookings/:id/duration`; midnight crossover handled.
- `src/components/bookings/NewBookingModal.jsx` — admin new booking. Touch-optimised: 48px cover buttons, date-as-button (OS picker), tel input for phone, custom numeric keypad for covers on touch devices. Accepts `openManual`/`prefillTime`/`prefillTableId`. **Canvas click flow** opens ManualAllocModal directly. **Manual allocation** button bypasses slot resolver. Customer search: debounced suggestions panel (desktop: side panel; mobile: inline below phone field with × dismiss). Walk In button. autoFocus suppressed on touch. **Slot warnings**: amber when booking end > `sitting_closes_at`; red when > `sitting_doors_close`.
- `src/components/widget/BookingWidget.jsx` — Self-contained widget component. This is the reference implementation for the Ember.js widget.
- `src/pages/EmailTemplates.jsx` — Email template admin page (`/email-templates` route). 3 tabs: Templates (per-type editor with merge field pills, preview iframe, reset-to-default), Settings (provider picker, credentials, sender identity, reminder toggle + hours slider, guest modify/cancel permissions), Sent emails (audit log). Venue selector in header. TipTap visual WYSIWYG editor with toolbar + HTML mode toggle.
- `src/pages/Media.jsx` — Standalone media library page (`/media` route). Renders `MediaLibraryModal` with `inline` + `manager` mode, so it fills the page (no backdrop, close, fullscreen or footer). Browse/organise/upload tenant assets without being mid-task.
- `src/components/media/MediaLibrary.jsx` — Reusable per-tenant media library modal (or, with `inline`, a page: the Media page). Two modes: `picker` (returns URL via `onPick` callback, "Insert selected" footer button) and `manager` (no insert action). Features: grid + list views, search, category sidebar, scope (form) filter, upload (button + drag-drop), duplicate detection (filename + SHA-256 hash), upload progress chips, single + multi select, single-item detail panel (preview, rename, change category, change scope, delete, **edit**), bulk actions (move category, move scope, delete), resizable detail panel, ESC to close, fullscreen toggle.
- `src/components/media/ImageEditor.jsx` — Crop / rotate / flip editor. Uses `react-easy-crop` for the crop UI; rotation (90° increments) and flip (H/V) applied via `<canvas>` at export. Aspect-ratio presets (Free, 1:1, 4:3, 3:2, 16:9, 9:16). Two save modes: **Save as new** (POST `/media/items/upload` with the cropped Blob → fresh media_items row) or **Replace original** (POST `/media/items/:id/replace` → keeps the row id, swaps file content + url + storage_key + dimensions + hash, deletes old file best-effort). Output MIME matches input for JPEG/WebP, falls back to PNG for everything else. `crossOrigin='anonymous'` on the source `<Image>` so canvas isn't tainted (works for same-origin and CORS-enabled S3 URLs).
- `src/pages/EmailMonitoring.jsx` — SendGrid monitoring (`/email-monitoring` route). Two stacked sections: **What went out** (4 stat cards from SendGrid + 4 from local log + daily breakdown table + recent sends from email_log) and **What's in (suppressions)** (4 cards — bounces/blocks/spam_reports/invalid_emails — with inline removal). Venue picker in header (uses that venue's SendGrid API key). Date range selector (7/30/90 days). Falls back to a clear "this venue is using {provider}" banner when the venue isn't on SendGrid; falls back to a "no API key" banner when the key is missing/invalid. Owner-only on suppression removal.
- `src/pages/Team.jsx` — Team management page (`/team` route). Invite users, set roles (inline dropdown), deactivate/reactivate. RBAC reference table. Owner-only mutations.
- `src/pages/Platform.jsx` — Platform admin dashboard (`/platform` route). Tenant list with venue/user counts, create/edit tenant cards, active/inactive sections, stats overview. Platform admin only.
- `src/pages/Access.jsx` — Access management (`/access` route). Two tabs: **Modules** (master on/off toggles per module, owner-only) and **Roles** (list of tenant_roles + inline permission matrix editor with `none/view/manage` toggle pills per module). Built-in roles show a "Lock" badge, can be edited but not deleted. Custom roles can be added/edited/deleted (only when no user references them). Available to anyone for viewing, mutations are owner-only (API-enforced).
- `src/pages/Reviews.jsx` — Reviews management page (`/reviews` route). Scrape/import/manual-add reviews. Filter, approve, feature. Stats bar.
- `src/pages/Menus.jsx` — Structured menu builder (`/menus` route). Sections (collapsible, ↑/↓ reorder) containing dishes (drag-reorder by grip within their own section: one `DndContext` per `SectionEditor`, `useSortable` in `ItemRow`); per-dish price, calories (optional kcal), description, photo, variant groups, dietary tags. Copy a dish / a section with its dishes (`copyOfDish()` / `copyOfSection()`, in the draft) and Copy options from another dish in the dish panel. The dish panel's option groups (`AttachedGroups` / `AttachedGroupCard`) drag into order by their grips and start closed. A dish's own options (`menu_item_variants`) go back in the save payload as they are and show in `OwnOptionsCard` (Turn into an option group: `sameAsOwnOptions()` / `ownOptionPrices()` / `withOwnOptionsAsGroup()`; Remove). Below md the dish and section panels are full-screen sheets (`SIDE_PANEL`); top bar buttons are `TOP_BTN`. `SectionImagePicker` gives each section a small category icon (deliberately compact — the image always renders no bigger than the section heading font on the live site). The save payload builder whitelists fields explicitly rather than spreading the item/section object — a new field must be added there or it silently never saves.
- `src/pages/MenuAllergenMatrix.jsx` + `src/components/menus/AllergenMatrix.jsx` — Allergen matrix (`/menus/allergens`, migration 141): `AllergenMatrixEditor` (dish rows x allergen then dietary tag columns; an allergen cell cycles No / Contains / May contain / Can be removed, a dietary cell toggles; explicit Save of changed dishes via `PUT /api/menus/:id/allergens` with `levels`) and `AllergenLookup` (read-only search + has / free-from tag filter in a pop-up grid, `TagFilterModal`, with the filters in use as removable chips, levels shown apart; the H&S Dashboard `allergen_lookup` widget and the Overview `allergen_lookup` tile). `LevelChip` draws a tag at its level (also used by the Menus dish panel). Data from `loadAllergenMatrix()` in `routes/menus.js` (`GET /api/menus/:id/allergens`).
- `admin/src/components/website-builder/canvas/BlockInserter.jsx` — the page builder's "+" block picker, plus `BlockAvailabilityContext` / `useBlockAvailability()` (what the page being edited can use: `hidden` block keys, `pdfMenus` `{ venueId, on }`), provided by `PageBuilder.jsx`.
- `shared/menuTags.js` (repo root) — menu tag kinds and allergen levels (migration 145): `STANDARD_ALLERGENS` (the 14), `ALLERGEN_LEVELS`, `LEVEL_LABELS`, `REMOVABLE_NOTE`, `levelOf()`, `isRemovable()`, plus `menuTags()` / `hasRemovable()` for tags hidden on menus (migration 146) and the allergen notice helpers `DEFAULT_NOTICE`, `noticeOf()`, `allergenKey()` (migration 147). Used by `routes/menus.js`, `services/menuSeeds.js` (`ensureStandardAllergens()`), `shared/menuLayout.js`, `siteRenderer.js` (`it.siteBlocks.allergenKey`), the ordering page (served as `/order-api/menuTags.js`) and the admin. `components/menus/DietaryTagsManager.jsx` is the Allergens & dietary page (two drag-sortable lists, type, merge, add missing standard allergens, and the `AllergenNoticeCard` builder).
- `src/pages/MenuDesigner.jsx` — Menu print designer (`/menus/:id/design`, opened from the menu editor's "Design print"). Snap-grid pages; left tray of block types + sections/dishes not placed yet (`placementSummary()`), drag (pointer events) or tap + to add; select then drag to move, corner handle to resize; right panel for position steppers, "Fit height to content", "Show on every page" (layout `master`), per-type options; Page setup modal (paper, orientation, margin, grid 6/12/24 cols, 2.5/5/10mm rows, text size, `regrid()` keeps blocks in place). Explicit Save (`PUT /menus/:id/print-layout`). `Menus.jsx` now reads `?edit=<id>` to open a menu directly (the designer's back link).
- `src/pages/Orders.jsx` — Online orders (`/orders`, `web_orders`): live board per venue + day (New / In progress / Ready / Done), sold out, pause, report tab, transactions tab (`components/orders/TransactionsReport.jsx`). `src/pages/OrderingSettings.jsx` (`/orders/settings`, `web_ordering_setup`): per-venue ordering settings, one Save. `src/pages/mobile/MobileOrders.jsx` (`/mobile/orders`). All built on `src/components/orders/shared.jsx` (OrderBoard, OrderDetailModal, AvailabilityModal, PauseButton, `useRealtimeOrders`, `printTicket`).
- `src/pages/Backlog.jsx` — Kanban backlog (`/backlog` route, platform admin only). 5-column board with @dnd-kit drag between columns.
- `src/pages/IssueLog.jsx` — ITIL issue tracker (`/issues` route). Filter by priority/status/category. Inline detail panel. Platform admin can update status and promote.
- `src/pages/FeatureRequests.jsx` — Feature request wishlist (`/feature-requests` route). Upvote toggle. Platform admin lifecycle controls and promotion to backlog.
- `src/pages/Changelog.jsx` — Changelog timeline (`/changelog` route). Platform admin editor with publish/unpublish. Tenants see published entries only.
- `src/pages/Inventory.jsx` (`/inventory`), `src/pages/Suppliers.jsx` (`/suppliers`), `src/pages/OrderLists.jsx` (`/purchasing/lists`) — purchasing setup (`purchasing_setup`). `src/pages/Purchasing.jsx` (`/purchasing`) and `src/pages/mobile/MobilePurchasing.jsx` (`/mobile/purchasing`) wrap `PurchasingApp` from `src/components/purchasing/ordering.jsx` (Order / Deliveries tabs, `OrderForm`, `PoDetail` with `AddPoItemsModal`, `ReceiveForm`; view in the URL `?order=` / `?po=` / `?tab=deliveries`). Shared UI: `src/components/purchasing/shared.jsx` (`NumInput`, `QtyStepper`, `DayPicker`, `DateButton`, `Drawer`, `Card`, `printHtml()`).
- `src/pages/Staff.jsx`, `StaffRoles.jsx`, `RotaSetup.jsx`, `Rota.jsx`, `RotaDashboard.jsx` — Staff & Rota pages (`/staff`, `/staff/roles`, `/rota/setup`, `/rota`, `/rota-dashboard`). `RotaDashboard.jsx` is a third `DashboardPage` config (`kind: 'rota'`, `/api/rota-dashboards`).
- `src/components/staff/shared.jsx` — shared staff-page primitives: `useVenueChoice` (`maca_staff_venue`), `useRotaSetup` (`['rota-setup']`), `SortableRows`, `ConfirmDelete`, `Modal`, `Segmented`, `TimeSelect`, time helpers.
- `src/components/staff/TipPotsSetup.jsx` — Rota setup's Tip pots section: sortable pot list, pot editor (name, distribution, Cash Recon SC sources grouped by venue, manual lines). Sources come from `GET /api/rota/sc-sources`.
- `src/components/staff/rotaExport.js` — rota Print / PDF (hidden-iframe `print()` of `rotaHtml()`, A4 landscape) and image export (`drawRotaCanvas()` JPEG at 0.92, share sheet on touch devices), both from one `buildRotaSheet()` model drawn like the grid (a tile per shift, hatched LEFT / AWAY, hourly periods in tinted boxes; no totals, roles or buttons; migration 194). No libraries. Also `ENTRY_STATUSES` (rota entry status codes, names, `solid` / `soft` colours matching `STATUS_CLS` / `STATUS_SOFT` in `rota.jsx`) and `statusOf()`.
- `src/components/staff/rota.jsx` — `RotaGrid` (draft + Save/Discard + copy week; day-parts ON/OFF cells or hourly `PeriodEditor`), `RotaDayList`, `RotaPayTable`, `RotaTipsTable`, `useRotaPerms()`. Used unchanged by the Rota page and the Rota dashboard widgets.
- `src/pages/TestData.jsx` — Pre-prod QA tooling (`/test-data` route, owner/admin only). "Generate dummy bookings" (date range + occupancy % + status) and "Clear bookings" (date range or all, typed-`DELETE`-to-confirm with a live dry-run count preview). Not for use once real tenants onboard.
- `src/pages/FoodSafety.jsx` — SFBB food-safety logs (`/food-safety` route), recording only. Tabs: Today (fridge/freezer grid + counts), Deliveries (entry form + current-week list, click any item to load it back into the form for editing), Holds (hot/cold hold grid), Cooking (till-style category tabs + dish buttons; `bg-muted/60` fill so tiles read as distinct from the page background). "Corrective actions" button cycles every out-of-range reading for the day still missing a note. All setup lives in H&S settings (`components/hs/HsSetup.jsx`).
- `src/pages/Checklists.jsx` — Operator-defined recurring checklists (`/checklists` route). Two tabs: Today (tick off what's due) and Checklists (template builder — name, department, frequency daily/weekly/monthly, ordered tasks).
- `src/pages/HSDashboard.jsx` — Customisable H&S dashboards (`/hs-dashboard` route). Any number of named dashboards (tabs), each holding any number of widgets (checklist / temp_checks / delivery_checks / hold_checks / cooking_checks) behind one date navigator. "Edit layout" adds/removes/reorders/resizes (width in grid columns, height in px) widgets. Checklist widget's "Complete" action lives in the widget's own card header. Container is full width (`w-full`; it was `w-[90%] mx-auto` until it left a visible empty strip on tablets). The grid is always exactly `column_count` columns (`repeat(column_count, minmax(0, 1fr))`), shrinking to fit a narrower screen so the operator's layout is kept; wide content scrolls inside its own card. Full screen uses the Fullscreen API (webkit-prefixed on iPad) and falls back to a `fixed inset-0 z-50` pseudo full screen where the API is missing (iPad home-screen apps). Date navigator: fixed-width label (doesn't reflow between "Today" and a full date string), 44px outlined prev/next arrows, native `input[type=date]` overlay for the calendar picker. The dashboard-name tabs render in the same row as the date navigator (`flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap`; from `sm` up the tabs strip is `flex-1 min-w-0 overflow-x-auto`, so it scrolls sideways rather than wrapping below — the earlier `flex-wrap` version wrapped onto a second line on an iPad) rather than on a separate row below — the tabs' JSX lives once, in that combined row; the `dashboards.length === 0` empty-state branch doesn't render it at all (guarded by `dashboards.length > 0`), and the `editing`-mode dashboard-management strip (rename/reorder/columns) still renders on its own row below, since it's edit-mode-only chrome not part of the everyday navigation row.
- `src/pages/CashDashboard.jsx` — Cash Recon Dashboard (`/cash-dashboard`, `cash_dashboard` module). Just a config (`CASH_DASHBOARD_CONFIG`) for `DashboardPage`, the generic dashboard engine exported from `HSDashboard.jsx` (tabs, edit layout, widget CRUD, row-spanning grid, full screen). `HSDashboard.jsx`'s own default export is the same engine with `HS_DASHBOARD_CONFIG`.
- `src/components/dashboards/widgetTypes.jsx` — the one widget catalog for every dashboard (migration 152): `WIDGET_TYPES` (H&S + Menus types here, `CASH_WIDGET_TYPES` from `cashRecon/widgets.jsx`, `ROTA_WIDGET_TYPES` from `staff/rotaWidgets.jsx`), each with `group` (`WIDGET_GROUPS`), `module` and `nav`; `renderWidget()`, `canUseWidget(meta, me)`, `hasHsDayWidget()`, `NoAccess`. `src/components/dashboards/nav.jsx` — `useDashboardNav()` (one selected day; `ctx = { date, weekStart, selectedDay, setSelectedDay }`), `navModeFor()` (`day` / `week` / `both`), `DashboardNav`. `src/pages/Dashboards.jsx` — blank dashboards (`/dashboards`, `CUSTOM_DASHBOARD_CONFIG`). `src/pages/mobile/MobileDashboard.jsx` — `MobileDashboardPage` (any kind, read-only layout); default export is `/mobile/dashboards`, `MobileHSDashboard.jsx` wraps it for the H&S kind.
- `src/components/cashRecon/widgets.jsx` — `CASH_WIDGET_TYPES`, `useWeekNav()` (week + selected day context) and `renderCashWidget()` for the ten cash widgets (day tiles, day balance, week balance, reconciliation grid, week summary grid, week staff list, wages paid, petty cash, week expenses, tips to pay out). Figures come only from `reconCalc()`/`useReconWeek()` and `SpreadsheetView` (both exported from `CashRecon.jsx`), `PettyCashPanel` (exported from `MobileExpenses.jsx`), the wages paid endpoint, and for tips the rota pay payload (`useRotaPay()`, `tipRows()`, `TipMoneyCell` from `components/staff/rota.jsx`).
- `src/lib/dashboardGrid.js` — shared row-spanning grid for the H&S Dashboard, Cash Dashboard and Overview tiles: fixed `ROW_UNIT` rows, `grid-auto-flow: row dense`, `rowSpanFor(height_px)`/`heightForRows(rows)`.
- `src/components/foodSafety/shared.jsx` — Shared panels reused by both `FoodSafety.jsx` tabs and `HSDashboard.jsx` widgets: `TempChecksTable`, `DeliveryChecksPanel`/`DeliveryChecksBoard`, `HoldChecksTable`, `CookingChecksPanel`, `EndOfDayReview`. One implementation of each check type, never two. `TempKeypadModal` is the shared IS_TOUCH-gated custom keypad used by `TempCell`, `HoldCell`, and `CookingEntryModal`'s core-temp field in place of the native keyboard.
- `src/pages/HSActionLog.jsx` — Standalone H&S Action Log page (`/hs-action-log` route) — venue picker + `HSActionLogPanel`.
- `src/components/hsActionLog/shared.jsx` — `HSActionLogPanel`, reused by `HSActionLog.jsx` and the H&S Dashboard's `action_log` widget. Open/Completed/All status tabs, category filter, add/edit modal (task, category, priority, assigned-to, due date, notes, photo attachment via the Media library picker), category-management modal (drag-reorder, same pattern as Food Safety's hold-stations manager).
- `src/components/hs/HsSetup.jsx` — all food safety setup as H&S settings sections (`SETUP_SECTIONS`, `HsSetupSection`): Fridges & freezers and Hold stations (drag-sortable), Fridge check times (`/capture-times`) and Hold check times (`/hold-capture-times`, kept separate), Cooking sessions (`CookingSessionsSettings` from `foodSafety/shared.jsx`, drag-sortable via `PATCH /food-safety/cooking-sessions/reorder`), Cooking items (`CookingItemsSettings`: item source menus/own/both with explicit Save, own-item list drag-sortable). Inline confirm on remove; invalidates every `fs-*` query after a change.
- `src/components/checklists/shared.jsx` — `ChecklistRunPanel`, the tick-list UI shared by `Checklists.jsx` and the H&S Dashboard's checklist widget (Tick all button). Accepts `hideHeader` + `onStateChange` (`{ isCompleted, isPending, hasItems, tickAll, untickAll }`) so the host renders `ChecklistHeaderAction` (Tick all, then All done with a confirmed Untick all) in its own header.
- `src/components/hs/HsSettings.jsx` — `HsSettingsButton({ venueId, section, label })`: the H&S settings window, General (Follow restaurant opening days switch, owner/admin, explicit Save) plus the food safety setup sections from `HsSetup.jsx` (food_safety manage), with its own venue picker; and `ClosedDayNotice` (banner when `/hs-settings` says the venue is closed that date). Used by Food safety, Checklists, the H&S Dashboard (`DashboardPage` config `HeaderExtra` / `Notice`, both optional) and `MobileHSDashboard.jsx`.
- `src/pages/NavDesigner.jsx` — Navigation designer (`/nav-designer` route, gated on `nav_designer` module). Outline-editor tree (move up/down/indent/outdent buttons, not drag-and-drop reparenting) over `nav_items`; add section/link with route picked from `GET /api/nav/route-catalog`; per-item module gate, per-role hide (`hidden_role_ids`, checkboxes sourced from `/api/access/roles`), "Show as a quick-access tile" toggle; typed-confirmation "Reset to defaults"; tenant-wide Sidebar/Launcher style toggle.
- `src/pages/Launcher.jsx` — Quick-access tile grid (`/launcher` route) — the alternative to the sidebar when `tenants.nav_style = 'launcher'`. Tiles come from `/api/me`'s `launcher_tiles` (admin-defined via Nav Designer); each viewer can drag-reorder (`@dnd-kit`, `rectSortingStrategy`) and hide tiles for themselves, persisted to `localStorage` (`maca_launcher_prefs_{tenantId}`) — never a DB table. "Reset to admin defaults" clears the override. Empty state explains where tiles come from and offers a "Switch back to sidebar" button when the tenant is stuck in launcher mode with nothing flagged yet.
- `src/components/mobile/MobileShell.jsx` — layout for the `/mobile/*` route tree (a sibling of `AppShell`'s tree in `main.jsx`, both inside `RequireAuth`/`TenantGate`). Minimal safe-area-aware header (back arrow / title / sign out), no sidebar. `useMobileManifest()` swaps `<link rel="manifest">`, `<link rel="apple-touch-icon">`, `<meta name="theme-color">`, and `<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-title">` to the mobile-only "Macaroonie Ops" identity on mount and restores them on unmount. Also exports `useHideMobileHeader()` — a hook a nested full-page-detail view (e.g. `MobileCashUp.jsx`'s day declaration) calls to suppress the shell's own header for as long as it's mounted, when it needs to render one unified header of its own instead of stacking a second header underneath the shell's — and `MobileLogoutButton`, the same sign-out button the shell's header uses, for that replacement header to fold sign-out into itself. Both are backed by a small context (`HeaderVisibilityContext`) internal to this file; the visibility toggle is a plain boolean (mount sets it, unmount clears it) — no page content is passed through context, avoiding any render-loop risk from passing JSX/objects through it.
- `src/mobile/registry.js` — `MOBILE_MODULES` array (key/label/description/icon/path), the single source of truth for tiles on the `/mobile` hub. Add an entry here + a route in `main.jsx` to ship a new mobile-optimised module.
- `src/pages/mobile/MobileHub.jsx` — `/mobile` index route; renders one tile per `MOBILE_MODULES` entry, two to a row.
- `src/pages/mobile/MobileHSDashboard.jsx` — phone-portrait, single-column H&S Dashboard for logging checks: same dashboards (as `DashboardTabs`, a framed strip with scroll arrows), widget types, `/api/hs-dashboards/*` reads and shared body components. No layout editing on the phone (done on the desktop page). Ignores `col_span`/`height_px` and renders full-width/natural-height so the page is the only scroll region. Imports `WIDGET_TYPE_BY_KEY` from `HSDashboard.jsx`.
- `src/pages/mobile/MobileExpenses.jsx` — second mobile module (`/mobile/expenses`). Venue picker + week navigator; `WeekExpensesPanel` shows Mon-Sun from `useReconWeek()` (week-detail) with week totals and a per-day Add; `useExpenseActions()` is shared with the single-day `PettyCashPanel` (Cash Dashboard widget). Tap-to-edit expense list, add/edit modal with category chips + camera-capture receipt photo. Reuses the exact same `/venues/:id/cash-recon/*` endpoints as `CashRecon.jsx`'s `ExpensesSection` — no new backend surface for this feature beyond the `POST /expenses` bug fix (see Common mistakes).
- `src/pages/mobile/MobileCashUp.jsx` — fourth mobile module (`/mobile/cash-up`). Venue picker + week nav over a plain vertical list of the week's 7 days (day name/date, `StatusBadge`, income, variance), each row tapping into a day-detail view for the complete daily declaration — same "list, then full-page detail" shape as `MobileOrderSheets.jsx`. A "Week total" tile below the list sums income/takings/variance across days that have a report. Reuses `GET /venues/:id/cash-recon/week/:week_start` (the same endpoint `WeekView`'s day cards use) — no new backend surface. The day-detail view is `MobileDayDeclaration` (module-local): it calls `useHideMobileHeader()` (from `MobileShell.jsx`) to suppress the shell's own header, then renders one unified header of its own — back arrow (to the day list), `EEE, dd MMM` date (e.g. "Mon, 21 Sep") + `SaveIndicator`, a Submit/Unsubmit button, and `MobileLogoutButton` (also from `MobileShell.jsx`) — followed by `DayView` (exported from `CashRecon.jsx`) rendered with `hideHeader` + `onStateChange={setDayState}`, so DayView's own sticky header is skipped and its status/save/submit state flows up into the page's single header instead. No `StatusBadge` in this header — the Submit/Unsubmit button's own label/colour already conveys submitted-vs-not, so the separate status pill was redundant here (unlike the week-list view above, which keeps `StatusBadge` since there's no per-day submit button to read the state off). `fmt`/`getMonday`/`isoWeekDates`/`StatusBadge`/`SaveIndicator`/`DayView` are exported from `CashRecon.jsx` for this.
- `src/pages/mobile/MobileAllergens.jsx` — `/mobile/allergens`: `AllergenLookup` (from `components/menus/AllergenMatrix.jsx`) with `phone` (sticky menu, search and the Allergens & diet filter button, `maca_allergen_lookup_mobile`). Read-only, no new API.
- `src/pages/mobile/MobileWages.jsx` — fifth mobile module (`/mobile/wages`): venue picker + week navigator over `WeekWagesEditor` (`layout="cards"`), the same wages list as the desktop Wages page (see `components/cashRecon/WagesTable.jsx`). Its earlier Paid checkbox and submitted-report lock went with wage submission (migration 137).
- `src/routes/cashRecon.js` — wages are written only by the whole-list `PUT /:venueId/cash-recon/wages/:week_start` (keeps entry ids the week already had) plus `POST .../set-default`. The per-entry `/paid` and `/paid-date` PATCHes and wage submit/unsubmit were removed in migration 137 (wages are never submitted or locked).
- `docs/POS.md` — the POS design (decisions, architecture with a local hub per venue, events and sync, devices and PINs, printing, payments adapter, data model, phases). `hub/` is the venue hub: `src/escpos.js` (ticket to ESC/POS bytes, code page 1252), `src/printer.js` (TCP 9100 queue with retries, `printerStatus()` via DLE EOT), `bin/test-print.js` (Trial 1: sample kitchen ticket + receipt to a printer IP, `--preview`), `test/` (`npm test` in `hub/`, fake network printer). `shared/pos/tickets.js` — `kitchenTicket()`, `receipt()`, `billSubtotal()`, `toText()`: tickets as print operations, shared by the hub and future previews.
- Device addresses (18 Oct 2026; Docs.jsx → Till (POS) → Hosting): one `pos/` build on `pos.` (tills), `kitchen.` (kitchen screens, `kds.` forwards) and `kiosk.` (kiosks), served by `routes/posSite.js` (manifest named per host); `pos/src/lib/appHost.js` (`HOST_APP`, `addressFor()`, `belongsHere()`, `takePairCode()`, `moveDevice()`, `tellDisconnect()`); `POST /pos-api/pair` with `app` (a code for another kind: 422 `wrong_address`), `POST /pos-api/disconnect`, `POST /pos-api/move` (one-time code, `#pair=`), `POST /screen-api/disconnect`; `pos/src/components/MoveNotice.jsx` (`MoveNotice`, `MovedAway`); Disconnect in `TillSettings.jsx`, the kitchen `SettingsPanel` and the kiosk `StaffPanel`; the screen player's own settings (`openSettings()` in `screen/player.js`, five taps or OK presses). Tests: `api/test/device-addresses.test.mjs`.
- Installer app + link codes (migration 184; Docs.jsx → Installer app & link codes): `pos_pairings` (codes a till / kitchen screen / kiosk shows, `app` = the address's kind), `services/linkCodes.js` (`newLinkCode()`, `linkCodeHash()`, `linkUrl()`, `linkCodeTaken()`: never live in `pos_pairings` and `screen_pairings` at once, `findLinkCode()`), `POST /pos-api/pairings` + `GET /pos-api/pairings/:id` (`Pairing <secret>`), `POST /api/pos/venues/:id/link` (`posSetup.js`), `routes/installer.js` (`/api/installer`: `GET /venues/:id` overview, `POST /lookup`), `routes/installerSite.js` (admin build on `installer.<domain>`, `installer.webmanifest`), `installerOrigin()` in `platformHost.js`. QR codes: `shared/qr.js` (`qrMatrix()`, `qrSvg()`, no dependencies; `shared/test/qr.test.mjs`), drawn by `pos/src/screens/Pair.jsx` (`useLinkCode()`; `requestLinkCode()` / `checkLinkCode()` in `pos/src/lib/store.js`) and `screen/player.js` (`showCode()`, `qr.js` in `PLAYER_LIBS`). Admin: `components/installer/InstallerShell.jsx` + `QrScanner.jsx`, `pages/installer/` (`InstallerHome`, `InstallerLink`, `InstallerAdd`, `InstallerDevice`, `shared.jsx`), routes `/install/*` and `/link/:code` in `main.jsx`, `IS_INSTALLER_HOST` / `installerHref()` in `lib/hosts.js`, `ShownCodeField` + `LinkDeviceModal` / `LinkDeviceButton` + `linkShownCode()` + `InstallerLine` in `components/pos/TillsSection.jsx` (used by the Tills, Kitchen screens and Kiosks tabs). Tests: `api/test/installer.test.mjs`; every API test adds devices with `linkDevice()` in `api/test/support/devices.mjs`.
- New location with Pepper (migration 186; Docs.jsx → New locations (store openings)): `shared/storeOpening.js` (`OPENING_AREAS`: 17 areas with their modules and modes copy | new | skip; `normalisePlan(raw, overrides, { templates, today })` (the only way a plan is made: AI answer + the person's overrides), `planProblems()`, `previewPlan()`, `hoursText()` / `tablesText()`, `buildFollowups()`, `AREA_LINKS`, `MAX_ROUNDS`). `services/storeOpeningAi.js` (`askPepper()`: steps scope / answers / deep_dive / change, structured output `ANSWER_SCHEMA`, `SYSTEM_PROMPT`, counted with `addUsage()`). `services/storeSetup.js` (`createStore()`: one transaction, `AREAS` per area copy / new in `AREA_ORDER`; `copyTable()` copies rows with new ids and remaps every uuid, uuid array and jsonb uuid to the copies, dropping ids the template venue owns but nothing copied (`OWNED_TABLES`); `venueSummary()`, `tenantContext()`, `freeSlug()`). `routes/storeOpenings.js` (`/api/ai/store-openings`: list, get, start, scope, answers, deep-dive, change, PATCH overrides, create, follow-ups, delete draft; `busy_until` lease, `view()`). Pepper: `ai/tools/locations.js` (`plan_new_location`, `answer_location_questions`, `change_location_plan`, `location_openings`, `create_location`; section `locations`, module `venues`). Admin: `pages/NewLocation.jsx` (`/new-location[/:id]`), link on `Venues.jsx`. Tests: `api/test/store-openings.test.mjs`.
- Print designs (migration 190; Docs.jsx → Till (POS) → Print designs): `shared/pos/ticketLayout.js` (`BLOCK_TYPES` per kind kitchen | receipt, `defaultLayout()`, `normaliseLayout()`, `REQUIRED_BLOCKS` / `canRemove()`, `normaliseVariation()`, `applyVariation()`, `PRINT_USES` kitchen | receipt | kiosk_receipt, `SAMPLES` / `sampleTicket()`, `blockSummary()`), `kitchenTicket(send, layout)` / `receipt(bill, layout)` in `shared/pos/tickets.js`, `opsHtml()` + `previewHtml(ops, cols)` in `shared/pos/ticketHtml.js` (the till's browser print and the designer's column-exact preview). API: `routes/printDesigns.js` (`/api/pos/print-designs[...]`, `/api/pos/print-design-uses`), `services/printDesignSvc.js` (`printLayouts()`, `loadUses()`, `resolveUses()`): `tillConfig()` `print_layouts`, the sequencer's `printConfig()` `layouts`, `kioskConfig()` `print_layouts.receipt`; `billOps(check, cfg, { layout })`, `jobsForEvent()` uses `kiosk_receipt` for kiosk devices. Admin: `components/pos/PrintingSection.jsx` (Till setup → Printing), `pages/PrintDesigner.jsx` (`/pos/printing/designs/:id`). Tests: `shared/pos/test/tickets.test.mjs`, `api/test/print-designs.test.mjs`.
- Kitchen printers and who prints (migration 191; Docs.jsx → Till (POS) → Printing): `shared/pos/kitchenPrint.js` (`kitchenPrintJobs(orders, cfg, at)`: a station of kind `printer` with `settings.printer_id` prints each dish once when it reaches it, HOLD / FIRE / VOID, `item.printed` / `hold_printed` / `void_printed`; `printsDue()`, `hasPrinters()`), run by the sequencer after every kitchen change and for `kitchen.print_due` (`kitchenPrintsDue()` in `services/kitchenSvc.js`, from the hold sweep). `pos_printers.connection` hub | device (+ `device_id`, `status`, `status_at`); `printsHere()` / `receiptHere()` in `shared/pos/printing.js`. API: `POST /pos-api/print-jobs/claim` (hub: network printers; till / kitchen screen: its own; stale `claimed_at` taken back), `POST /pos-api/hub/status`, `hubConfig()` in `services/posSvc.js`, hub devices (`HUB_ROUTES` in `routes/posApi.js`, `app: 'hub'` link codes), `POST /api/pos/print-jobs/:id/again`. Hub: `hub/bin/hub.js`, `hub/src/cloud.js`, `hub/src/service.js`. Device printing: `pos/src/lib/devicePrint.js` (till and kitchen screen stores, `pos.print` push). Admin: `components/pos/PrintersSection.jsx` (connection, state, Tickets, Venue hub), the `printer` kind in `KitchenSection.jsx` and `KitchenFlow.jsx`. Tests: `shared/pos/test/kitchenPrint.test.mjs`, `api/test/kitchen-printers.test.mjs`, `hub/test/service.test.mjs`.
- Shared kitchen flows and Sent from (migration 192; Docs.jsx → Kitchen screens → Shared flows): `kds_flows.venue_id` NULL = shared by every location, `boxes` [{ id, name, kind }] + `box_links` [{ id, from, to, targets }] (not `kds_flow_edges`). `shared/pos/kitchen.js`: `resolveSharedFlow(flow, place)` (boxes to the venue's stations by `flowName()`, stream / device filters by name, links to missing boxes left out; `{ links, matches, missing, differs }`), `shareFlow()` (own to shared), `boxProblems()`, `NAMED_TARGET_KINDS`, link filter kind `device` (RANK 2, the `lines.sent` event's `device_id` via `itemFromLine()`). `services/kitchenSvc.js`: `loadFlows()` (own then shared), `flowPlace()`, `loadFlow(tx, tenantId, flow, stations, { venueId, place })`, `pinActiveFlows()`. `routes/posSetup.js`: `flowView()`, `sharedPlaces()` (setup's `kds_locations` with `active_flow_id`, `kds_shared_names`), `flowNameTaken()`, `everyVenueChanged()`, `cleanTargets()`, `PUT /api/pos/kds-flows/shared/order`, the shared branch of `PUT /api/pos/kds-flows/:id`, copies both ways in `POST .../kds-flows`. Admin: `KitchenFlow.jsx` (`seedDraft()` for boxes, `AddBoxMenu`, `PlacesNote`, `useAddStations()`, the box part of `NodePanel`, Sent from chips in `LinkPanel` and `CheckPanel`), `pages/Screens.jsx` (`useScreensPlace()`, `mergeOrder()`: the Location picker on Screens and Walls). Bench: `spec.device_id` in `shared/pos/kitchenBench.js`, `bench-device` in `Bench.jsx`. New locations keep a shared flow in use (`storeSetup.js`). Tests: `shared/pos/test/kitchenShared.test.mjs`, `api/test/shared-flows.test.mjs`, the last test in `store-openings.test.mjs`.
- Kitchen flow Sleeper (migration 200; Docs.jsx → Kitchen screens → The kitchen flow): kind `sleeper` with `settings.delay_mins` (`SLEEP_MINS_MAX` 240); `flowTimes().wakes()`, `itemSleeps()`, `workSince()`, `item.slept` in `shared/pos/kitchen.js`; `StationModal` / station row in `components/pos/KitchenSection.jsx`; box style, `flow-sleeper-note` and Check a dish in `KitchenFlow.jsx`; `kds-sleeps` and the ticket clock in `pos/src/kitchen/KitchenApp.jsx`; `wakeTime()` in `Bench.jsx`.
