## Database — critical patterns

### Slot generation
Slots are **never stored**. They are computed at request time by the `get_available_slots(venue_id, date, covers)` PG function (current definition in `020_slot_start_filter.sql`). The function:
1. Checks for named schedule exceptions (highest priority) → date override → weekly template
2. Iterates sittings, generates candidate times at `slot_interval_mins` while `slot_time < closes_at`
3. **Slot is generated as long as `slot_time < closes_at`** — the booking may run past `closes_at`. Contrast with the old rule which required `slot_time + duration ≤ closes_at`.
4. Looks up per-slot cover cap (sparse — only stored if different from sitting default)
5. Subtracts active bookings + non-expired holds from the cap
6. Applies `zero_cap_display` logic (hidden vs unavailable)
7. Returns `sitting_closes_at` and `sitting_doors_close` per slot for frontend warnings

### Double-booking prevention
Two layers:
1. `UNIQUE (table_id, starts_at)` on `booking_holds` — DB-level race condition guard
2. `confirm_hold(hold_id, tenant_id)` PG function uses `FOR UPDATE NOWAIT` at booking confirm time

### Hold lifecycle
```
Guest presses Book
  → POST /bookings/holds → INSERT booking_holds (TTL = hold_ttl_secs, default 300)
  → Widget shows countdown timer

Guest cancels
  → DELETE /bookings/holds/:id → slot immediately free
  → Also cancels Stripe PI if one was attached

Payment received (Stripe webhook)
  → confirm_hold() → INSERT bookings → DELETE hold
  → broadcastBooking() → WS push to timeline

Hold expires (no action)
  → pg_cron sweep or BullMQ holdSweepQueue deletes expired rows
```

- ✅ **Timeline background colour** — `timelineBg` state in `SettingsContext` (default `#ffffff`); applied as `backgroundColor` on the Timeline rows wrapper div; 6 predefined swatches + `ColourPickerRow` in Settings
- ✅ **Closed/unavailable area colour** — `greyColour` state in `SettingsContext` (default `#8c8c8c`); `hexToRgba(greyColour, 0.38)` used in `greyBackground` useMemo gradient stops; 6 grey swatches + `ColourPickerRow` in Settings
- ✅ **Tile modes (compact / extensive)** — `tileMode` + `compactFontSize` in `TimelineSettingsContext`; persisted to `maca_timeline_prefs`; `ROW_HEIGHT_MAP = { compact: { sm:36, md:44, lg:52 }, extensive: 72 }`; `BookingCard` renders single-row (compact) or 3-line flex-col (extensive); extensive shows phone + table allocation via `tableById` Map; `Detail` toggle button in AppShell sidebar; default tile mode + compact size configurable in Settings
- ✅ **Wide time columns** — `wideColumns` boolean in `TimelineSettingsContext` (default `false`); `const hourWidth = wideColumns ? 120 : HOUR_WIDTH`; all three utility functions (`timeToX`, `sittingTimeToX`, `durationToWidth`) accept optional `hw` param defaulting to `HOUR_WIDTH`; all resize/drag/canvas-click/grey-strip calculations use `hourWidth`; `TableRow`, `BookingCard`, `TimelineHeader` all accept `hourWidth` prop; toggle in Settings → Timeline defaults
- ✅ **ColourPickerRow sync fix** — `useEffect(() => setHexInput(value), [value])` added so local picker state syncs when an external swatch button updates the context value (previously only colour wheel/hex input worked; swatches appeared to do nothing)
- ✅ **Resize drag no-drawer-open** — `wasResizingRef` boolean ref set to `true` in `handleUp` after resize mutation fires; `handleBookingClick` checks ref and returns early (suppressing drawer open) then resets ref; prevents the `click` event that fires after `pointerup` from opening the booking drawer
- ✅ **Timeline TDZ fix** — `hourWidth`, `totalWidth`, `rowHeight` declarations (and their `tileMode`/`compactFontSize`/`wideColumns` destructure) moved to before the `nowX` useMemo in `Timeline.jsx`. Previously they were declared ~50 lines after `nowX`, causing a `ReferenceError: Cannot access '...' before initialization` crash (Temporal Dead Zone) that made the entire Timeline render blank. Also added `hourWidth, totalWidth` to the `nowX` dep array.
- ✅ **Compact tile vertical padding** — `py-[3px]` added to the compact tile content div (`flex items-center h-full ...`) so there is a small top/bottom gap around text inside each booking tile in compact mode.
- ✅ **Opening hour line** — `showStartLine` (boolean, default `true`) and `startLineColour` (hex, default `#630812`) added to `SettingsContext`; `firstOpenX` useMemo in Timeline computes x-position of first sitting's `opens_at` for the selected date; a `position:absolute` 3px-wide full-height overlay div is rendered inside the Timeline wrapper at `LABEL_WIDTH + firstOpenX` (z=2, `pointer-events:none`) when `showStartLine` is true; Settings → Appearance shows toggle + colour picker + 6 swatches (only visible when line is enabled).
- ✅ **Shade header row** — `headerBgStrips` boolean (default `false`) added to `SettingsContext`; when true the same `backgroundStyle` object (grey strips + diagonal stripes) is applied as inline style to the `TimelineHeader` outer div, replacing `bg-background`; sticky label cell retains `bg-background` regardless; toggle in Settings → Appearance.
- ✅ **Last order time inclusive** — migration `022_slot_inclusive_last_order.sql` changes `WHILE v_slot_time < v_sitting.closes_at` to `<=` so the last-order slot itself is bookable (e.g. 22:00 last orders → 22:00 slot offered). Uses `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION` so no type drop needed.
- ✅ **Section headers in grouped view (bug fix)** — grouped-section `TableRow` call was missing `rowHeight`, `tileMode`, `compactFontSize`, `tableById`, `hourWidth` props; now matches flat-view `TableRow` exactly.
- ✅ **Timeline hour range control** — `timelineStart` (default 9) and `timelineEnd` (default 24) added to `TimelineSettingsContext`; persisted to `maca_timeline_prefs`; derived `startHour`, `endHour`, `totalHours` in Timeline component; `timeToX` and `sittingTimeToX` accept optional `sh = START_HOUR` third param; `startHour` threaded to `TableRow`, `BookingCard`, `TimelineHeader`, all useMemos and drag/resize/canvas-click handlers; Settings → Timeline defaults shows two hour dropdowns.
- ✅ **Drawer table override uses /relocate** — single-table selection in `BookingDrawer` now calls `PATCH /bookings/:id/relocate` (triggers cascade displacement), multi-table still calls `PATCH /bookings/:id/tables`.
- ✅ **Overlap detection (stop sign)** — `overlappingIds` useMemo O(n²) pairwise check on active bookings for shared tables and overlapping time windows; overlapping `BookingCard` tiles get red `ring-2 ring-red-500 ring-inset` border and `⛔` badge top-right.
- ✅ **Date display as full word** — Timeline top bar replaced with styled-button + invisible `<input type="date">` overlay (matching Bookings pattern); both Timeline and Bookings now show `EEEE d MMMM yyyy` format (e.g. "Monday 22 March 2026"); "Today" shown when on current date; Today button only visible when not on today.
- ✅ **Opening hour line per sitting** — `firstOpenX` (single value) replaced with `sessionStartXs` (array); one 3px line rendered per sitting so both lunch and dinner get their own line; `TimelineHeader` receives `sessionStartXs`/`showStartLine`/`startLineColour` props and renders lines inside its canvas when `headerBgStrips` is on.
- ✅ **Header shade toggle fix** — `TimelineHeader` outer div was missing `bg-background` class so it appeared shaded by default (parent wrapper's `backgroundStyle` bled through); `bg-background` added to the `className` — `backgroundStyle` inline style overrides it when `headerBgStrips` is on.
- ✅ **Session names on sittings** — migration `023_sitting_names.sql` adds `name text` (nullable) to `venue_sittings`, `override_sittings`, `exception_sittings`; `SittingBody` Zod schema updated; `name` returned in `sittings-for-date` and all schedule GET endpoints; Schedule.jsx sitting editor has a "Session name" text input (placeholder: e.g. Lunch, Dinner); name displayed next to time range in sitting list.
- ✅ **Per-sitting stats bar** — `sittingStats` useMemo in Timeline maps active bookings to sittings by HH:MM comparison; shows total bk/covers + per-sitting breakdown in Timeline and Bookings top bars (`hidden sm:flex`); uses sitting `name` if set, falls back to `opens_at–closes_at` time range.
- ✅ **Capacity warning always visible in drawer** — `hasCapacityIssue = booking.covers > (combination_max_covers ?? table_max_covers)` computed on drawer open; amber warning shown permanently in table section (not just when editMode === 'table').
- ✅ **Sidebar expanded-by-default setting** — `sidebarExpandedDefault` boolean (default `true`) added to `SettingsContext`; AppShell uses it to initialise the `open` state on desktop (mobile always starts collapsed); Settings → Interface toggle.
- ✅ **Zero cap greying fix** — `staleTime: 0` added to `slots-overlay` query; `if (slots.length > 1)` changed to `>= 1` with `intervalMs` defaulting to 15 min so single-slot venues now get cap=0 greying.
- ✅ **Unallocated option in drawer** — `tables` query made non-lazy (always fetches); `unallocatedTable` derived from `tables.find(t => t.is_unallocated)`; "Unallocated" checkbox added at top of table picker (orange); currently-unallocated bookings show orange "Unallocated" badge in view mode; `handleTableSave` routes unallocated selection to `PATCH /tables` (not `/relocate`); `toggleTable` clears unallocated when a real table is picked.
- ✅ **Table lock** — `table_locked boolean DEFAULT false` on `bookings` (migration 024); `PATCH /bookings/:id/lock` toggles the flag; `/relocate` conflict scan now selects `table_locked` and throws 422 before displacing if any conflicting booking is locked; `BookingDrawer` shows a lock/unlock icon button inline next to the table pill (amber ring when locked, muted when unlocked); `Lock`/`Unlock` icons from lucide-react.
- ✅ **Tenant Website Builder / CMS — one site per tenant + location pages** *(migrations 025 + 026 + 027 + 041 + 042 + 043)*. Each master franchisee has ONE site at `{tenant_site.subdomain_slug}.macaroonie.com` (or a verified custom domain). Each venue under the tenant becomes a `/locations/{venue.slug}` page driven by `website_config` + the venue row, plus a `/locations` index. Booking widget gained a tenant mode (`/widget/tenant/:tenantId`) with a location-picker step 0, and a `?venue=<id>` deep-link param. Migration 043 (May 2026) flipped the architecture from one-site-per-venue to this model — the `tenant_brand_defaults` table was renamed to `tenant_site` and absorbed `subdomain_slug`, `custom_domain[_verified]`, `home_blocks`, `is_published`, `meta_title/description`, `default_widget_venue_id`, locations-index settings (`hide_locations_index`, `locations_heading`, `locations_intro`); `website_config.home_blocks` → `page_blocks` (drives the location page layout); `website_pages` got a nullable `venue_id` (NULL = tenant-level page, non-NULL = location page). **Pre-flip background:** Venue sites inherit brand defaults and override per-location (address, hours, gallery, menus, hero, about). New tables: `website_config` (per-venue, UNIQUE venue_id), `website_opening_hours`, `website_gallery_images`, `website_pages`, `website_menu_documents`, `website_allergen_info` (migration 025). Migration 026 adds `custom_domain`, `custom_domain_verified`, `template_key` ('classic' | 'modern'), and `theme` JSONB. Migration 027 shifts from UNIQUE tenant_id to UNIQUE venue_id, adds `tenant_brand_defaults` with emergency banner. Pluggable storage service (`STORAGE_DRIVER=local|s3`). Subdomain + custom-domain SSR renderer with `@fastify/view` + Eta. Two shipping templates (classic, modern) sharing theme CSS variables. Admin page at `/website` with venue picker + brand defaults mode (4 sections: identity, theme, analytics, emergency banner) + per-venue mode (18 sections: setup, template, theme overrides, branding, hero, about, gallery with @dnd-kit reorder, PDF menus, allergens, opening hours, find us, contact + socials, online ordering, delivery providers, booking widget, custom pages, SEO, analytics). Deployment pieces (wildcard Nginx, DNS, SSL) pending — see Outstanding items.
- ✅ **Cash recon — SC included_in_income flag** *(migrations 033 + 034)* — independent `included_in_income` boolean on `cash_sc_sources`. Works alongside existing `included_in_takings` with XOR variance adjustment: both on = no change (SC in income AND till); income only = subtract (SC bundled in income but not in till); takings only = add (SC not in income but in till); both off = no change. Toggle in admin SC source editor. Summary shows signed "SC adjustment" line. Weekly grid variance also uses XOR logic.
- ✅ **Booking email system** *(migration 035)* — end-to-end: `manage_token` UUID on bookings (guest auth for self-service). Pluggable email service (`emailSvc.js`) with 4 providers (SendGrid, Mailgun, SES, SMTP) selectable per venue. Default HTML email templates (confirmation, reminder, modification, cancellation) with 16 `{{merge_fields}}`. Email worker (`emailWorker.js`) processes BullMQ jobs: loads booking + venue + template, renders fields, sends, logs to `email_log`. Reminder scheduling via delayed BullMQ jobs (configurable hours-before per venue). Guest manage page at `/manage/{token}` (SSR, themed, modify date/time/covers + cancel with double-confirm). Triggers wired into bookings.js (confirmation on create, cancellation on status change, reminder on confirm). Admin page at `/email-templates` with 3 tabs: template editor (per-type, merge field pills, preview iframe, reset-to-default), settings (provider, credentials, sender, reminders, guest permissions), sent emails log.
- ✅ **Auto-deploy migrations** — `api/scripts/migrate.js` runner with `schema_migrations` tracking table. Runs automatically in GitHub Actions workflow + deploy.sh. Auto-baseline (`AUTO_BASELINE_UP_TO=024` env) for servers whose schema predates the runner. Manual baseline workflow (`.github/workflows/db-baseline.yml`).
- ✅ **CLAUDE.md session protocols** — adopted SOS Checklist, EOS Protocol, Standard Design Rules, Doc maintenance section from the COGS template. TOC added. Gotchas section marked append-only.
- ✅ **Visual email editor** — TipTap WYSIWYG with toolbar (bold/italic/underline, headings, lists, alignment, link, image, divider, colour picker, CTA button insert). Visual / HTML mode toggle. Merge field pills insert at cursor in visual mode. `<a class="btn">` preserved via extended Link extension with class attribute round-trip. URL sanitisation (`isSafeUrl`) prevents `javascript:`/`data:` injection.
- ✅ **Platform admin + tenant/user management** *(migration 036)* — `platform_admins` table (global, no RLS). Auth middleware detects platform admins and bypasses all role gates. `GET /api/me` returns user profile + available tenants (for org switcher). Platform admin routes: `GET/POST/PATCH /api/platform/tenants` + stats. Team routes: `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/team` for invite, role change, deactivate (owner-only, self-protected). Org switcher in AppShell sidebar (re-authenticates with different Auth0 org). `VITE_AUTH0_ORG_ID` now optional. Team page at `/team`. Platform page at `/platform`.
- ✅ **Auth0 Management API integration** — full team lifecycle in-app, no Auth0 dashboard required for tenant operators. New service `api/src/services/auth0MgmtSvc.js` (token caching via client-credentials grant, `inviteUserToOrg`, `removeUserFromOrg`, `updateUserAppMetadata`, `sendPasswordResetEmail`). `POST /api/team/invite` now sends an Auth0 organization invitation email and rolls back the local row if Auth0 fails. `PATCH /api/team/:userId` syncs role to Auth0 `app_metadata.role` whenever role changes. `DELETE /api/team/:userId` removes the user from the Auth0 org membership in addition to setting `is_active=false` locally. New endpoint `POST /api/team/:userId/reset-password` (owner-only) sends an Auth0 password change email — no need to touch the Auth0 dashboard. Auth middleware reconciles Auth0 sub ↔ local user on first login (links by email when invited row has `auth0_user_id IS NULL`), uses local `users.role` as source of truth for `req.user.role`, and rejects auth when local `is_active=false`. New env vars `AUTH0_MGMT_CLIENT_ID`, `AUTH0_MGMT_CLIENT_SECRET`, `AUTH0_INVITE_CLIENT_ID` (all optional — when missing, system degrades to local-only invites with a warning banner in the Team page). Migration 037 seeds the initial platform admin (Michal) so `/platform` works on first deploy.
- ✅ **Auto-provision Auth0 organisations on tenant creation** — `POST /api/platform/tenants` with `auto_provision: true` (UI default) creates the Auth0 organisation via `provisionTenantOrg()` and enables Username-Password + Google connections with `assign_membership_on_login: true` baked in. New helpers in `auth0MgmtSvc.js`: `createOrganization`, `getConnectionByName`, `enableOrgConnection`, `provisionTenantOrg`. The tenant row is inserted in the DB FIRST (with the provisioned `auth0_org_id`), so a partial Auth0 failure leaves the platform admin a clear edit path. Response includes `auth0_provisioning: { attempted, org_created, enabled_connections, error }` so the UI can surface partial failures with an inline warning. Requires extra M2M scopes (`create:organizations`, `read:connections`, `create:organization_connections`) on top of the team-invite scopes — listed in `api/.env.example`.
- ✅ **Configurable RBAC: tenant module switches + custom roles** *(migration 038)* — extends RBAC beyond the booking-focused enum. Two new tables: `tenant_modules` (per-tenant on/off switch per module — disabled modules are hidden from nav AND rejected at the API) and `tenant_roles` (custom roles per tenant with a `permissions` JSONB mapping `module_key → 'manage' | 'view' | 'none'`). 4 built-in roles seeded (owner/admin/operator/viewer); built-in roles can have permissions edited but not deleted. New `users.custom_role_id` (nullable) takes precedence over the legacy `users.role` enum when set. New module registry at `api/src/config/modules.js` lists all 14 modules + descriptions + per-built-in defaults — single source of truth for migrations, API, UI.
  - **Module groups** (`MODULE_GROUPS` in modules.js) bundle tightly-coupled modules under one master switch. Current groups: `bookings` (bookings, venues, tables, schedule, rules, customers, widget_test), `email_templates`, `website`, `cash_recon`. Core modules (dashboard, team, settings, documentation) are `core: true` with no master switch. The tenant Modules tab in `/access` toggles GROUPS, not individual modules — disabling Bookings hides every booking-related nav entry at once.
  - **Role permissions stay per-module** so a role can have `bookings: manage` + `rules: view` even though both belong to the same group.
  - **Routes**: `/api/access/modules` (per-module list/patch — internal), `/api/access/module-groups` (group-level toggles — what the UI uses), `/api/access/roles` (custom-role CRUD).
  - New `requirePermission(moduleKey, level)` middleware for route gating. `/api/me` extended with `enabled_modules: string[]`, `permissions: { module: level }`, `effective_role`. AppShell filters nav by `me.permissions[item.module]`. New admin page at `/access` with Modules (group toggles) + Roles tabs.
- ✅ **Reservations widget — full rebuild** *(migrations 050 + 051 + 052, May 2026)*. The legacy `widget.eta` + `booking_widget` block are gone — replaced with a clean ground-up rebuild at `views/site/reservations_widget.eta` and `views/site/blocks/reservations_widget.eta`. Routes renamed: `/widget/:id` → `/reservations/:id`, `/widget/tenant/:id` → `/reservations/tenant/:id`. Admin page renamed: `/widget-settings` → `/reservations-widget` with a Live preview iframe + Share & embed card (Direct URL + iframe code). Sidebar nav entry "Reservations widget". The old `widget-api/*` API endpoints are unchanged (still the source of truth for slots/holds/confirms). Migration 052 rewrites existing live `booking_widget` block instances to `reservations_widget` in-place. The old `widget.eta` had a broken slot filter (`s.available_seats` field that doesn't exist), buggy date input, and dim-cell calendar — all rebuilt fresh.
- ✅ **Widget customisation — full control surface**. Tenant-level defaults at `/reservations-widget` and per-block overrides in the page builder, both with parity:
  - **Header** controls: tri-state show/hide, custom header text, sub-header text
  - **Button**: theme-role bg + fg colour pickers, corner radius slider
  - **Borders + radii**: theme-role border colour, card/chip radius slider
  - **Three independent typography tracks** — Body / Calendar / Time slots, each with its own FontPicker + size slider (px). Each can be a different Google Font; the widget builds a single deduped Google Fonts URL covering all picked fonts with per-font weight specs (no v2 400s)
  - **Calendar day colours** — Open day (bg/fg/border) and Closed day (bg/fg/border), all theme-role pickers
  - **Per-block anchor ID** — set `data.anchor_id` to anything URL-fragment-safe; section renders `<section id="<your-anchor>">` so any `<a href="#book-now">` link works
  - **Debug toggle** — tri-state on the block, boolean on the tenant defaults; surfaces a fixed black bar at top of widget showing live state + last action + last API URL/status. Also accepts `?debug=1` / `?debug=0` URL override
  - **Messages** — "Larger party" copy override (set to a single space to hide entirely)
  - All colour fields store theme role names (`'primary' | 'accent' | 'background' | 'surface' | 'text' | 'muted' | 'border'`) via `ThemeColourPicker`. SSR resolves role → hex against the tenant theme before reaching the widget. URL params carry hex directly.
- ✅ **`ThemeColourPicker` component** (`admin/src/components/website-builder/ThemeColourPicker.jsx`) — pill-strip showing the 7 theme roles as swatches rendered with their actual hex colours from `tenant_site.theme.colors`. Stores the role name (not hex). Optional "Inherit" swatch. Includes `resolveRole(value, theme)` helper that maps name → hex. Used by all colour controls on widget settings + block editor.
- ✅ **Reservations widget calendar — month-view date picker**. Replaced the native `<input type="date">` with a full month-grid calendar. Prev/next month arrows, today highlighted with an inset accent ring, day-of-week headers (Mon-first ordering, `(getDay() + 6) % 7`). Click a date → fires `pickDate(iso)` → calls `loadSlots()` → advances to slots step. "Jump to date" native input below the grid for fast multi-month navigation. Calendar pre-fetches a `GET /widget-api/venues/:id/schedule-summary` (returns `openDaysOfWeek: number[]` from the venue's weekly template) and greys out closed days with diagonal hatching — operators can't waste clicks on closed days.
- ✅ **Scrolling text block** — clean replacement for the broken `ticker` block. Loads its own Google Font directly via a self-contained `<link>` in the block markup (NOT relying on head.eta's font collector) and applies it with `!important` so no parent style can override. No legacy `font_style: 'sans'` branch. Default font Caveat. Six font sizes (sm to 3xl as multipliers of `--fs-base`). Theme-role bg picker. Optional text-colour role override. Per-instance unique class scoping. Migration 051 rewrites `ticker` → `scrolling_text` in existing live data.
- ✅ **Gallery block — Media library integration**. Replaced the disconnected `website_gallery_images`-based gallery with a `media_items`-driven block. Two source modes: "By category" (with `category_id`, blank = all images) and "Pick specific images" (chip-style multi-select preserving order). Three layouts: Grid (configurable columns + aspect ratio), Masonry (CSS multi-column flow), Horizontal (snap scroll). Per-block aspect ratio (1:1 / 4:3 / 16:9 / natural), gap, max-images cap, captions toggle, lightbox toggle. Built-in vanilla-JS lightbox with prev/next + ESC/click-out close, scoped per block. New backend resolver `loadGalleryItemsForBlocks(tenantId, blocks)` in `siteDataSvc.js` walks all gallery blocks pre-render, batches one DB query, and returns `{ [blockId]: items[] }`. Editor shows a live thumbnail preview of the resolved set.
- ✅ **Dynamic menu (`menu_inline`) block — full control surface**. Section filter (chip-style multi-select of menu sections, blank = all), specific dish filter (chip-style grouped by section), hide-prices toggle, column count (1-4 with Auto = use menu's print_columns), flow direction (Columns / Snake CSS-multi-column vs Rows / Wrap CSS-grid), show/hide section headers toggle, subheader text override with show/hide toggle. Section deletion / hide-prices changes preserve variant labels (joined with " · ").
- ✅ **Pre-prod block cleanup**. Dropped legacy blocks entirely: `ticker` (→ `scrolling_text`), `two_column` (→ `story_with_stamp`), `booking_widget` (→ `reservations_widget`). Each has an idempotent data migration that rewrites JSONB in `tenant_site.home_blocks` and `website_config.page_blocks`. PageBuilder templates updated. Block partial files deleted from disk. Total block roster: 22.
- ✅ **Per-tenant Widget settings JSONB column** *(migration 050)* — `tenant_site.widget_settings jsonb` stores tenant-level booking widget defaults. PATCH on `/website/tenant-site` accepts it; widget renderer fetches it and merges into `tenantWidgetDefaults` before rendering. Single column keeps the field set malleable.
- ✅ **Per-font Google Fonts weight map**. Earlier the Google Fonts URL builder requested every font with `wght@300;400;500;600;700;800` which v2 rejected as 400 the moment any picky font (Caveat 400-700, DM Serif Display 400-only, Libre Baskerville 400/700) ended up in the URL. Now both `head.eta` and `FontPicker.jsx` maintain matching `FONT_WEIGHTS` maps that specify the actual supported weight set per font. Widget partial has its own copy of the same map (so the embed widget can build its own URL deduplicated across body/calendar/slots tracks).
- ✅ **Columns block 1-4** — was 2-4. Default still 2.
- ✅ **Mobile responsive blocks**. Added scoped `_blockId` classes + `@media` collapse rules to `menu_inline`, `dish_list`, `reviews_band`, `story_with_stamp`. Header block now has per-element mobile visibility flags (`mobile_show_logo`, `mobile_show_brand`, `mobile_show_subtitle`, `mobile_show_links`, `mobile_show_cta`, `mobile_breakpoint`). `head.eta` body has `overflow-x: hidden` defensive guard.
- ✅ **Theme-only colour picker for all blocks**. Replaced hex colour inputs across `BookingWidgetEditor` → `ReservationsWidgetEditor`, `ScrollingTextEditor`, `WidgetSettings.jsx` → `ReservationsWidget.jsx` with `ThemeColourPicker`. Operators can no longer pick "off-palette" hex values; only the 7 theme roles. Backwards-compat: legacy hex values in existing data are detected (regex `^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$`) and passed through unchanged at render time.
- ✅ **SEO + AI discoverability** *(migration 055)*. Schema.org `Restaurant` JSON-LD on every tenant site page (address, geo, phone, hours, menu link, `servesCuisine`, `priceRange`); `BreadcrumbList` on location pages. Open Graph + Twitter Card meta. Canonical URL per page. `llms.txt` at root of every tenant domain (generated from published data; referenced in `robots.txt`). `cuisine` + `price_range` columns on `website_config`; both wired to the admin Website builder SEO section and to the JSON-LD emit.
- ✅ **Widget combo-to-combo displacement**. `tryDisplaceCombo` in `widgetApi.js` now tries free existing combinations as displacement destinations after individual tables fail. Slot-availability SQL subquery extended to match. Fixes false-positive availability and "Conflict" errors when conflicts can only be moved to a combo destination (e.g. T8+T9) rather than individual tables.
- ✅ **Admin relocate chain-displacement (second cascade level)**. `/relocate` step 6c: when a conflict has no free single table and no fully-free combo, the system tries combos whose every occupant can be moved to a free individual table — all in the same atomic transaction. Covers scenarios where e.g. T8+T9 is needed for a 4-person but T8 has a 2-person who can move to T11 once the primary booking vacates it.
- ✅ **Pre-configured combos bypass non-adjacent rule in `/relocate`**. Step 3b (find explicit combo containing target table) no longer applies the adjacency check. An operator-configured combination is always eligible as a relocation target regardless of sort-order gaps. The adjacency rule still governs step 3c (automatic expansion). Fixes 422 errors on venues with T3A/T3B-style split tables.
- ✅ **Timeline Manual Mode**. Amber `Hand` toggle in the timeline sidebar. When on, `/relocate` is called with `manual_mode: true`: allocation logic still runs (picks right table/combo) but conflict detection and cascade are skipped — booking placed directly, overlaps allowed. Persisted to `maca_timeline_prefs`. Overlap badges still show.
- ✅ **Long-press to access stacked booking** (5 s hold). `BookingCard` merges a 5 s pointer timer alongside dnd-kit's drag listener. If the finger drifts > 8 px, timer cancels and drag takes over. On fire, opens the drawer for the overlapping peer booking. `overlapPeerMap` computed in the `overlappingIds` useMemo; bridged via `overlapPeerMapRef` to avoid TDZ crash (memo is ~600 lines below the callback).
- ✅ **Widget confirmation page customization** — configurable heading text, rich-body HTML (TipTap WYSIWYG in the block editor), and up to N CTA buttons each with label + URL. Stored as `confirmHeading`, `confirmBodyHtml`, `cta_buttons[]` on the block/tenant-defaults data shape. SSR renders from `_confirmHeading`/`_confirmBodyHtml`/`_ctaButtons` vars resolved via `_pickStr()` (block → tenant-default → empty string). `CONFIRM_HEADING` / `CONFIRM_BODY_HTML` / `CONFIRM_CTAS` vars baked into the widget JS at render time.
- ✅ **Widget booking hold fixes** *(migrations 053 + 054)*. Migration 053 makes `booking_holds.table_id` nullable — the public widget creates holds at venue-level (no per-table selection) so the NOT NULL constraint was rejecting every widget hold. Migration 054 rewrites `confirm_hold()` to fix two bugs: (1) status check now excludes `no_show` + `checked_out` matching the slot resolver; (2) for combination holds the function now checks every member table is free, not just the first.
- ✅ **Reviews system** *(migration 056)*. `reviews` table with RLS (tenant_id). Three ingestion paths: Apify Google scraper (`POST /api/reviews/scrape` enqueues a BullMQ job via `reviewScrapeWorker.js` + `apifySvc.js`), manual entry (`POST /api/reviews`), CSV import (`POST /api/reviews/import-csv`). Moderation flags: `is_approved` + `is_featured`. Admin page at `/reviews` (sidebar nav). Stats summary (`GET /api/reviews/stats`). `google_place_id` column added to `venues` to tell the scraper what to fetch. New `reviews_band` block updated for media-library integration (images from actual reviews). Requires `APIFY_API_TOKEN` env var for scraping.
- ✅ **Dev tools suite** *(migration 057)*. Four platform/tenant tools:
  - **Backlog** (`/backlog`, platform-admin only) — 5-column Kanban (backlog/todo/in_progress/in_review/done). @dnd-kit drag between columns. Item types: epic/story/task/bug/spike. Priority + story points. Items can be promoted from issue log or feature requests.
  - **Issue log** (`/issues`, tenant-facing) — ITIL-style tickets. Impact × urgency matrix auto-derives P1–P4 priority. Categories: incident/problem/change_request/service_request. Platform admin can update status, add resolution notes, promote to backlog.
  - **Feature requests** (`/feature-requests`, tenant-facing) — upvote toggle (cross-tenant `feature_request_upvotes` table). Platform admin can set status (submitted/under_review/planned/in_progress/shipped/declined) and promote to backlog.
  - **Changelog** (`/changelog`, all users) — timeline list of published entries. Platform admin editor with publish/unpublish toggle. Tenants see only published entries.
  - New modules: `issue_log` (group: support), `feature_requests` (group: support), `changelog` (core: true). `support` group added to `MODULE_GROUPS`.
- ✅ **Curly-quote contamination fix in `reservations_widget.eta`** — the Edit tool introduced 153 U+2018/U+2019 typographic quotes into the template during confirmation-page development. Four of these were inside the `<% %>` server-side JS block (lines 105, 106, 115, 116), causing Eta to pass invalid token characters to V8 → "Invalid or unexpected token" 500 on every widget request. The remaining 149 were in the `<script>` HTML section where they would produce client-side JS errors. Fixed via Python byte-level global replacement (`\xe2\x80\x98`/`\xe2\x80\x99` → `\x27`). Diagnostic: `python3 -c "data=open('f','rb').read(); [(print(i,l)) for i,l in enumerate(data.split(b'\n'),1) if b'\xe2\x80\x98' in l or b'\xe2\x80\x99' in l]"`.
- ✅ **Venue auto-select fix (`EmailMonitoring.jsx`)** — `useQuery`'s `onSuccess` callback was removed in `@tanstack/react-query` v5, so the auto-select-first-venue logic silently never ran; `venueId` stayed `''` forever while the native `<select>` (no blank option) visually defaulted to the first venue, producing a dropdown/body-text mismatch. Fixed by deriving the default venue at render time (`selectedVenueId || venues[0]?.id || ''`), matching the existing `useVenueId` pattern in `Rules.jsx`. `WidgetTest.jsx` had the same dead `onSuccess` but was left as manual-select-only since it has an intentional blank "Select venue…" state.
- ✅ **SES Secret Access Key field added (`EmailTemplates.jsx`)** — the AWS SES provider settings only exposed an Access Key ID (mislabelled "API key") and AWS region; there was no input for the Secret Access Key the backend already expected in `provider_domain`, so per-venue SES credentials could never be fully configured. Added the missing field and relabelled the shared key input to "Access key ID" for SES.
- ✅ **Test data tool** *(migration 067)* — `/test-data` admin page (owner/admin only) for pre-prod QA: generate synthetic bookings across a date range at a target per-sitting occupancy %, or bulk-clear bookings by date range / venue-wide with a typed-`DELETE` confirmation and a dry-run count preview. New `test_data` module (group: `bookings`). See `src/routes/testData.js` in Key files.
- ✅ **Food safety logs** *(migrations 076, 077, 090)* — SFBB-style due-diligence logging per venue/day: equipment (fridge/freezer) temp checks, deliveries, hot/cold hold checks, cooking/reheat checks. Equipment and hold stations are drag-reorderable named entities (migration 090), not fixed enums. `/food-safety` admin page, `food_safety` module. See `src/routes/foodSafety.js` + `src/pages/FoodSafety.jsx` + `src/components/foodSafety/shared.jsx` in Key files.
- ✅ **Checklists** *(migration 083)* — fully operator-defined recurring checklists (opening/closing/cleaning/anything), any number of tasks, daily/weekly/monthly due schedule. `/checklists` admin page, `checklists` module. See `src/routes/checklists.js` + `src/pages/Checklists.jsx` + `src/components/checklists/shared.jsx` in Key files.
- ✅ **H&S Dashboard** *(migrations 086–088)* — customisable dashboards combining checklist + food-safety check widgets behind one date navigator; a widget is a thin pointer at the existing check data, not a separate model. `/hs-dashboard` admin page, `hs_dashboard` module. See `src/pages/HSDashboard.jsx` in Key files.
- ✅ **H&S Action Log** *(migration 092)* — general facilities/compliance to-do list per venue (repairs, records, training, cleaning), migrated field-for-field from the legacy spreadsheet's "ActionLog" tab. Categories are a tenant-managed reorderable list (seeded with the spreadsheet's four: Repairs, Records, Training, Cleaning), not a fixed enum. Not date-scoped — an entry stays open across days until ticked complete (records who/when, same convention as checklists). Photo attachments reuse the existing Media library rather than a new upload path. Also available as an `action_log` widget on the H&S Dashboard. `/hs-action-log` admin page, `hs_action_log` module. See `src/routes/hsActionLog.js` + `src/pages/HSActionLog.jsx` + `src/components/hsActionLog/shared.jsx` in Key files.
- ✅ **Dashboard → Overview tile system** *(migration 093)* — the home page ("Dashboard") is renamed "Overview" and rebuilt as a tile layout, same layout-control pattern as `hs_dashboard_widgets` (`col_span`/`height_px`/`sort_order`) but simpler — one implicit layout per tenant (Overview is tenant-wide, not per-venue, so no multiple-named-dashboards concept), plus per-role visibility (`hidden_role_ids`) mirroring `nav_items`. The four elements that used to be fixed page sections (quick access shortcuts, today's stats, upcoming bookings, venues status) are now tiles, in their original order, for every existing tenant. Two new tile types ship (not seeded by default — an owner/admin adds them via "Customise layout"): `hs_today_status` (combined Checklists + Food safety status for today, across venues, red/amber/green/grey) and `hs_week_status` (same, as a Monday–Sunday 7-day strip). Gated by the existing `dashboard` module (relabelled "Overview") — no new module. See `src/routes/dashboardTiles.js` + `src/config/defaultDashboardTiles.js` + `src/pages/Dashboard.jsx` in Key files.
- ✅ **Food safety / checklists polish** — drag-to-reorder added to the Equipment and Hold-stations management lists; the H&S Dashboard checklist widget's "Complete" action moved into the widget's own card header (was a full-width button at the bottom); Deliveries tab redesigned to an inline entry-form + current-week list with click-to-edit; H&S Dashboard container widened to `w-[90%] mx-auto` (was a fixed `max-w-6xl` cap, inconsistent with other wide admin pages); Cooking tab's category tabs and dish buttons restyled like till/POS buttons (fixed height, tighter width, `bg-muted/60` fill so tiles read as distinct tappable tiles instead of blending into the page background); H&S Dashboard date navigator made touch-friendly (fixed-width label so the toolbar doesn't reflow between "Today" and a full date string, 44px outlined prev/next arrows, confirmed the native `input[type=date]` overlay still opens the OS calendar picker).
- ✅ **Navigation designer + quick-access launcher** *(migration 091)* — replaces the previously-hardcoded `NAV_SECTIONS` array in `AppShell.jsx` with an admin-configurable, arbitrary-depth nav tree per tenant (`nav_items`, self-referencing via `parent_id`), with per-role visibility (`hidden_role_ids`, on top of the existing module-permission gating) and a tenant-wide `tenants.nav_style` toggle (`sidebar` / `launcher`). `/nav-designer` admin page is an outline editor (move up/down/indent/outdent buttons — not free-form drag-and-drop reparenting, which is much more fragile to build correctly for arbitrary depth). `GET /api/me` now returns `nav_tree` (pruned/filtered for the caller's effective role) and `launcher_tiles` (flat, filtered, sorted); `AppShell.jsx` renders these directly instead of a static array. Launcher mode (`/launcher`) replaces the whole sidebar with a tile grid built from links flagged "Show as a quick-access tile"; per-user tile order/visibility is personalised via `localStorage` (`maca_launcher_prefs_{tenantId}`), never a DB table, matching the existing `maca_settings`/`maca_timeline_prefs` pattern. New `nav_designer` core module (owner-only `manage` by default). See `src/routes/nav.js` + `src/config/defaultNav.js` + `src/pages/NavDesigner.jsx` + `src/pages/Launcher.jsx` in Key files.
- ✅ **Dashboard quick-access shortcuts kept in sync** — the Dashboard page's own `SHORTCUT_OPTIONS` picker (a separate, older feature from the Navigation designer above — per-user shortcuts from a fixed catalog, always available regardless of `nav_style`) had never been updated to include Food safety, Checklists, or H&S Dashboard since those modules shipped. Added all three.
- ✅ **Wages UX overhaul + cash-only net balance** *(migration 093)* — five changes to `/cash-recon`'s Wages page: (1) each staff entry redesigned from 4 stacked blocks into a single compact grid row (Staff / Type / Hours / Rate / Total / Cash paid / Notes / Remove); (2) new `cash_wage_defaults` table (`tenant_id, venue_id, staff_id, entry_type, sort_order`, `UNIQUE(venue_id, staff_id)`) lets an operator "Set as default" on the current week's staff list (who's included + Fixed/Hourly) so future weeks with no saved entries auto-populate from that instead of the full active-staff roster — falls back to the full roster if no default has been set yet; ad-hoc (staff_id-less) entries are never included in the default since there's no stable identity to match in a future week; (3) removed the "Load template" button and its `window.confirm()` popup (Standard Design Rule #4 violation) — replaced by the non-destructive "Set as default" action; (4) cash recon's net-position calculation (`weekNetPosition()` in `SpreadsheetView`, and the Wages summary card in `WeekView`) now subtracts only `wages_cash_total` (the `cash_amount` column sum) rather than `wages_total` (the full wage cost) — a wage paid partly or fully by bank transfer no longer drags down the cash net position; (5) an amber `AlertTriangle` flag appears wherever wages are shown in the recon view whenever a week's `total_wages !== total_cash_wages`. New route `POST /:venueId/cash-recon/wages/:week_start/set-default`; `GET /:venueId/cash-recon/config` now also returns `wage_defaults` (joined with staff name/rate); both wage-total queries in `cashRecon.js` now select `cash_amount` sums alongside `total`.
- ✅ **Mobile PWA — /mobile section + H&S Dashboard** — a phone-portrait area of the same admin SPA at `/mobile/*`, sharing the existing Auth0 session/API/deploy pipeline (no new Nginx/DNS/Auth0 app). `main.jsx` mounts a second top-level route tree (`<Route path="mobile" element={<MobileShell />}>`) alongside the existing `<AppShell />` tree, both inside the same `RequireAuth`/`TenantGate`. `src/components/mobile/MobileShell.jsx` is a minimal header-only layout (no sidebar) that swaps the page's PWA identity to "Macaroonie Ops" (its own `admin/public/mobile.webmanifest`, `mobile-pwa-192/512.png`, `mobile-apple-touch-icon.png`, teal theme colour) while a `/mobile` route is mounted, by rewriting the existing `<link rel="manifest">` / `<link rel="apple-touch-icon">` / `<meta name="theme-color">` / `<meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-title">` tags on mount and restoring them on unmount — no second `vite-plugin-pwa` instance, since there's only one `index.html` to inject into. `src/mobile/registry.js` (`MOBILE_MODULES`) drives the `/mobile` hub screen (`MobileHub.jsx`) — add an entry + a route to ship a new mobile module, no shell changes needed. First module: `src/pages/mobile/MobileHSDashboard.jsx`, a full-functionality single-column rebuild of `HSDashboard.jsx` (same dashboards-as-tabs, same add/remove/reorder-widget mutations, same six widget types, same `/api/hs-dashboards/*` endpoints, same shared body components) that deliberately ignores `col_span`/`height_px` and renders one full-width column at natural height so the page itself is the only scroll region. `DashboardModal`, `AddWidgetModal`, `WIDGET_TYPE_BY_KEY` are exported from `HSDashboard.jsx` and reused rather than duplicated.
- ✅ **Wages default-list bug fix** — a brand-new week (never saved before) failed to auto-populate from the venue's default staff list, because the frontend's `useEffect` guard (`if (!wagesData || !config) return`) treated a resolved `null` (the API's "confirmed — no wage report yet" response) the same as `undefined` ("query still loading"). Fixed by checking `wagesData === undefined` specifically.
- ✅ **Website theme — tenant-customisable boxed-inset margin steps** — the 5 boxed-inset steps (previously a hardcoded `{1:16, 2:24, 3:40, 4:64, 5:96}` px scale shared verbatim across `head.eta` and all 19 `api/src/views/site/blocks/*.eta` partials) are now redefinable per tenant via `theme.spacing.boxed_steps` — exactly 5 `{ value, unit: 'px' | '%' }` entries, mixing units freely (e.g. step 1 = 20px for mobile portrait, step 3 = 5% for everything else). The step *numbers* (1-5) referenced everywhere — theme default (`boxed_step`), the existing mobile-portrait-only override (`boxed_step_mobile`), and every block's own per-block override — are unchanged; only what each number resolves to is now configurable. Falls back to the standard scale when unset. Admin canvas parity via `boxedLayout.js`'s `setBoxedSteps()`, called once per render by `themeResolver.js`. New "Boxed inset — step values" editor in `BrandLayoutFields.jsx` (Website → Brand & theme → Layout); the existing default-step and mobile-override pickers now show each step's live value. See Website CMS section in Docs.jsx for the full wiring.
- ✅ **Admin portal — website brand accent for section header/footer bars** — `GET /api/me` now also returns `site_theme` (the tenant's public-website brand colours, `tenant_site.theme.colors`, resolved with `head.eta`'s own default fallbacks). `SettingsContext.applySiteTheme()` writes `--site-primary` / `--site-accent` / etc CSS variables — a namespace deliberately separate from the admin's own `--primary` (operator's Settings colour pick) and `--accent` (neutral hover-state grey used everywhere via `hover:bg-accent`). `CashRecon.jsx`'s `SectionCard` (Income, Service Charges & Tips, Takings, Wages, Expenses) now has a coloured header bar plus a new optional `footer` prop rendering a matching coloured footer bar, both using `--site-accent`, so each section reads as a clearly bounded card instead of blending into a flat grey wall of inputs.
- ✅ **Menu inline canvas preview — variant visibility parity fix** — `MenuInlineCanvas` (the page-builder's live preview for the Menu (inline) block, in `dataBlocks.jsx`) ignored a menu's `hide_zero_priced_variants` / `hide_unpriced_variants` toggles, which `menu_inline.eta` (the actual SSR partial) already applied correctly — so an operator editing a page saw variants/prices the published site actually hid. Ported the same filtering + price-formatting logic into the canvas component. `dish_list.eta` (the other menu-shaped block) is unrelated — it's a free-form curated dish list with plain string prices, not backed by the structured menu/variant data model.
- ✅ **Food safety — temp reading pre-fill + touch keypad + cooking-check edit** — three related changes to `foodSafety/shared.jsx`: (1) `TempCell`/`HoldCell` pre-fill an unlogged reading with `min_temp_c` (lower end of that equipment/station's configured range) instead of a blank field with only a ghost placeholder — display-only, nothing saves until the cell is actually touched; (2) new `TempKeypadModal` — a full-screen, IS_TOUCH-gated custom on-screen keypad (digits, one decimal place, a `±` sign toggle for negative freezer readings) replacing the native `<input>` on touch devices, since at least one Linux/Chrome tablet never surfaces its own on-screen keyboard for this input type (a platform limitation `inputMode="decimal"` can't fix — same reasoning as the existing covers-entry custom keypad in `NewBookingModal.jsx`). Applied to `TempCell`, `HoldCell`, and `CookingEntryModal`'s core-temp field; (3) each row in the Cooking tab's "Today's checks" list is now a button — tapping it reopens `CookingEntryModal` pre-filled from that check (`target.existingCheck`) so an already-logged core temp can be corrected; `persist()` already routed to `onUpdate` whenever `checkId` was set, so this only needed the state-initialisation path.
- ✅ **Menu item calories + section category image** *(migrations 094 + 095)* — two additive, nullable fields on the structured menu builder: `menu_items.calories` (int, kcal) shown next to the price wherever price shows; `menu_sections.image_url` (a small category icon/image next to the section heading, deliberately capped at `1.3em`/`1.6em` so it never renders bigger than the heading font regardless of the uploaded image's actual resolution — `SectionImagePicker` in `Menus.jsx` is a compact variant of the existing dish `ItemImagePicker`). Wired through all the places the whole-tree bulk-upsert touches: `ItemBody`/`SectionBody` Zod schemas, `loadMenuFull()`, `upsertMenuTree()`, the `/duplicate` route's re-mapping, and three render surfaces (`menu_inline.eta`, `menu_print.eta`, `MenuInlineCanvas` in `dataBlocks.jsx`) kept in parity. Neither field has a show/hide toggle — they render automatically whenever set, matching how `notes`/dish `image_url` already behave. First-ever Help.jsx/Docs.jsx sections for the Menus feature — none existed before this (a pre-existing documentation gap, not something this change caused).
- ✅ **Cooking check delete** — `DELETE /api/food-safety/cooking/:id` hard-deletes a `fs_cooking_checks` row (no `is_active` column on this log table, nothing else references its id, so a plain DELETE is correct — unlike the config-entity soft-deletes elsewhere in this file). `CookingEntryModal` shows a "Delete this reading" control (inline double-confirm, Standard Design Rule #6) only when editing an existing check (`checkId` set); wired through a new `deleteCheck` mutation in `CookingChecksPanel` that invalidates the day's query and closes the modal on success.
- ✅ **Dashboard widget height enforcement** — `HSDashboard.jsx`'s `WidgetCard` and `Dashboard.jsx`'s `TileCard` both changed their body wrapper from `style={{ maxHeight: heightPx }}` to `style={{ height: heightPx }}` (keeping `overflow-y-auto`). A `maxHeight` let a widget/tile with little content shrink below the size the operator explicitly set with the resize controls; `height` enforces that size in both directions — short content leaves blank space, long content scrolls — matching what "set a height" should mean on a customisable grid.
- ✅ **H&S today tile — full breakdown** — `computeHsStatus()` in `dashboardTiles.js` now returns, per venue per day, `checklists: [{id, name, frequency, completed}]` (one entry per active template — checklists have no "unresolved" concept, just done/not-done) and `categories: [{key, label, expected, completed, unresolved, status}]` (one aggregate entry each for equipment/hold/cooking checks, included only when that category has anything configured for the venue). Computed from data already loaded for the existing expected/completed sums — no new queries. `Dashboard.jsx`'s `HsTodayStatusBody` renders this as a real breakdown list (grouped by venue when there's more than one) instead of the old venue-only rollup that only showed when >1 venue.
- ✅ **Checklist reopen in dashboard widgets** — the H&S Dashboard's checklist widget (and its mobile counterpart) could mark a checklist complete but never reopen it — the full-page `ChecklistRunPanel` already had a working double-confirm reopen flow, it just wasn't exposed in `hideHeader` mode (the mode the widget cards use). Fixed by adding `reopen: () => save.mutate({ markComplete: false })` to the `onStateChange` payload; `HSDashboard.jsx`'s `WidgetCard` and `MobileHSDashboard.jsx`'s `MobileWidgetCard` each hold a local `confirmReopen` boolean and render the same inline double-confirm in their compact header (tap the green "Complete" badge → "Yes, reopen"/"Cancel").
- ✅ **Cash Recon SectionCard — header-only accent, plain footer, spacing fix** — reverted the footer's coloured band back to plain (`bg-muted/30`, no accent) per feedback that two accent bars per card read as too heavy; the header keeps its `--site-accent` colouring. Also fixed zero-visible-gap between stacked section cards on the daily-declaration page: `SectionCard`'s own `mb-4` and the page wrapper's `space-y-0` were two competing spacing mechanisms, and the effective gap was zero — removed `mb-4` from the component, changed both `space-y-0` wrappers to `space-y-4` so the page's own layout container is the single source of spacing.
- ✅ **H&S Dashboard widget headers — brand accent colour** — `WidgetCard` (desktop) and `MobileWidgetCard` (mobile) headers now use the same `--site-accent-soft`/`--site-accent` theming as Cash Recon's `SectionCard`, instead of a generic `bg-muted/30`. Required `MobileShell.jsx` to fetch `GET /me` and call `applySiteTheme()` itself — `/mobile` never mounts `AppShell`, so without this the site-accent CSS variables were never set for a session opening straight into `/mobile` (e.g. the installed PWA's own start URL), and the new accent header would have silently shown the hardcoded fallback colour.
- ✅ **Mobile Expenses module + `POST /cash-recon/expenses` bug fix** — new `/mobile/expenses` page: venue/date pickers, a day's running total, tap-to-edit expense list, and an add/edit modal with category chips + a `capture="environment"` camera input for the receipt photo. Building it surfaced that `POST /:venueId/cash-recon/expenses` had required a `report_id` UUID that its only caller (`CashRecon.jsx`'s `ExpensesSection.handleAdd()`) never had in scope and never sent (it sends `date` instead) — every "Add expense" click on the desktop page has been silently 400-ing since it shipped, swallowed by an empty `catch {}`. Fixed the endpoint to take `date` and find-or-create the daily report row itself (same upsert pattern `PUT /daily/:date` uses for its header), also blocking adds to an already-submitted report. Both the desktop form and the new mobile page call this same, now-working endpoint. See Docs.jsx's Mobile App section for why this wasn't just routed through `PUT /daily/:date` instead (that endpoint replaces income/takings/SC wholesale from the request body — unsafe for a single-expense write).
- ✅ **SC sources — independent add/deduct direction per side** *(migration 096)* — replaced `cash_sc_sources.included_in_takings`/`included_in_income` (booleans, fixed direction each — see the superseded Common-mistakes entry above) with `takings_effect`/`income_effect`, each `'none' | 'add' | 'subtract'`. Lets an operator deduct a service charge from Takings (or add one to Income) — combinations the old fixed-XOR model couldn't express. `scEffectAmount(effect, amount)` in `CashRecon.jsx` is the one place the sign mapping lives; both `variance(date)` (week grid) and the `scAdjustment` useMemo (day view) call it identically, so there's still only one implementation of the adjustment math. Admin editor: two selects ("Effect on Takings" / "Effect on Income") replace the two toggles; the week-grid source row shows a `↳ takings +`/`↳ income −`-style badge per active effect instead of a bare `↳ takings`/`↳ income` tag. Migration maps old booleans onto the new columns so every existing source computes an identical adjustment number before and after — see Docs.jsx for the exact mapping and why it looks asymmetric.
- ✅ **Cash Reconciliation variance sign fixed** — variance was computed as Income − Takings everywhere (`variance(date)` in the week-grid `SpreadsheetView`, the day-view `const variance`, and a third, independent recomputation in `WeekView`'s 7-day cards that additionally referenced a dead field, `d.total_sc_in_takings`, never present in the `/week/:week_start` response). That sign reads backwards from the standard accounting convention (Actual − Expected): taking in MORE than declared showed as a NEGATIVE number. Fixed by flipping to `variance = Takings − (Income + scAdjustment)` in both `SpreadsheetView` and the day view, and by making `WeekView`'s day cards consume the `/week/:week_start` endpoint's own `variance` field directly (`total_takings − total_income`, which was already correctly signed — only the client-side recompute was wrong) instead of recomputing with the dead field. The existing amber/red colour comparisons (`variance > 0` amber, `< 0` red) needed no changes — flipping the underlying number's sign alone makes shortfalls (the more serious case) land on red and surpluses on amber, which they hadn't reliably done before.
- ✅ **Net Cash / Cash to bank — cash-only channels** *(migration 097)* — **Superseded: Net Cash and Cash to bank were removed (see "Variance includes till payouts").** `cash_payment_channels.counts_as_cash` (boolean, defaults to `true` only for `type = 'cash'`, backfilled from existing `type` values) lets an operator flag which Takings channels actually become physical cash in the till. Card, voucher and online-link channels default off. `Net Cash` and the renamed `Cash to bank` row (see below) in both `SpreadsheetView` (week grid) and the day view now sum only `counts_as_cash` channels (`cashTakingsTotal(date)`/`cashChannels` in `CashRecon.jsx`) — `Total Takings` and `Variance` are unaffected and still sum every active channel. A "Non-cash" badge renders next to any such channel wherever it's listed (week-grid row label, day-view Takings list). Toggle lives in Cash Recon settings → Payment channels, defaulting to the channel's `type` when the operator hasn't touched it yet.
- ✅ **"Net Position" renamed to "Cash to bank"** — **Superseded: Net Cash and Cash to bank were removed (see "Variance includes till payouts").** the week-grid's final wage-adjusted row (`weekNetPosition()`, unchanged formula: cash takings − expenses − cash wages) now reads "Cash to bank" in the UI. The day-view's per-day "Net Cash Position" summary line (no wages, wages are weekly-only) is unrenamed — it's a different, simpler figure and keeping distinct names avoids implying it's the same "money ready to bank" total.
- ✅ **Boxed inset — per-step mobile values** *(spacing.boxed_steps entries)* — each of the 5 tenant-configurable boxed-inset steps can now carry an optional `mobile_value`/`mobile_unit`, applied only on phone portrait (≤600px). Implemented by resolving every consumer — the theme default (`--boxed-pad`) AND every per-block `boxed_step` override — through 5 shared CSS variables (`--boxed-step-1` … `--boxed-step-5`) emitted once in `head.eta`'s `:root`, with a single `@media (max-width: 600px) and (orientation: portrait)` block overriding whichever steps have a `mobile_value` set. This is a one-source-of-truth improvement over the previous design, where only the theme-level default could differ on mobile (via `boxed_step_mobile`, choosing a different step INDEX) and every per-block override was "fixed at all sizes" — per-block overrides now automatically inherit mobile-responsiveness for free since they resolve through the same vars. `boxed_step_mobile` (swap to a different step index on mobile) still works, composing on top of any per-step mobile values. Mirrored in the admin canvas (`boxedLayout.js`'s `boxedStepVarCss`/`boxedStepVarsCss`/`boxedStepMobileVarsCss`, `themeResolver.js`, `ThemeFrame.jsx`) for canvas/SSR parity, though the canvas's "Approximate mobile preview" toggle narrows a div rather than the real viewport, so it still won't retrigger the media query — only a genuinely narrow browser window does (pre-existing limitation, unrelated to this change). Editor: `BrandLayoutFields.jsx`'s per-step cards gained a "Mobile" checkbox that reveals a second value/unit input, defaulting to the step's own desktop value when first ticked.
- ✅ **Website theme — background image** — new `theme.colors.background_image` (nullable URL, alongside the existing `colors.background` hex). Renders on `html, body` as `background-image` with `cover`/`center`/`no-repeat`/`fixed`, sitting behind the flat background colour. Editable via `ImageField` in `BrandThemeSection`'s Colours card (the actually-rendered tenant-brand theme editor — NOT the same-named but unreferenced `ThemeSection` function earlier in `Website.jsx`, which is dead code left over from a prior refactor). Mirrored in `themeResolver.js`/`ThemeFrame.jsx` for canvas parity. Column-overwrite semantics apply as with the rest of `theme` — see the existing "theme JSONB is column-overwrite" gotcha.
- ✅ **Venue "Hero" nav entry renamed to "Identity"** — `PAGES_VENUE`'s `branding` nav item was labelled "Hero" but its component (`BrandingSection`) has only ever configured site name, tagline, logo and favicon — never hero image/overlay content, which lives entirely in the Hero block and the theme's Hero settings (`theme.hero.*`). The "Hero" label was a stale leftover from before the block-based redesign; relabelled to match what it actually does. The section itself was kept (removing it would have deleted the only way to edit a venue's per-location site name/tagline/logo/favicon). **Superseded the same day** — see the next entry; this section was removed entirely once it turned out (checking the actual render path) that its site_name/tagline fields were dead and its logo/favicon fields are better served by the new full override below.
- ✅ **Brand & theme — per-venue site overrides, replacing the sparse inheritance model + the venue "Identity" page** *(migration 098)* — a multi-venue tenant can now give an individual venue its own complete Brand & theme (identity, colours, typography, layout), separate from a single, simpler "everyone shares the tenant default" state for single-venue tenants. Investigating the user's "why do we need Identity if we have Brand & theme" question surfaced that the venue-level Identity page's `site_name`/`tagline` fields were saved but **never actually read by any renderer** — `mergeLocationConfig()` only ever inherited `logo_url`/`favicon_url` per venue (`BRAND_INHERITABLE`), and separately `brandTheme.js`'s `mergeThemes()` forced tenant `colors`/`typography` to always win over a venue's own theme regardless, specifically to paper over an earlier bug where a stale venue theme silently overrode a template's fonts/colours on the live site. Replaced both mechanisms with one explicit boolean, `website_config.use_brand_override` (default `false`): off, the venue's brand/theme fields (`BRAND_OVERRIDE_FIELDS` in `siteDataSvc.js` — site_name, tagline, logo_url, favicon_url, primary_colour, secondary_colour, font_family, template_key, theme) are ignored entirely and the tenant's apply as-is; on, the venue's own values apply wholesale, no blending. `mergeThemes()`/`brandTheme.js` deleted (zero remaining callers). Admin UI: `BrandSection` (`Website.jsx`, rendered under `tenant-brand`) shows a pill selector — "Default" + one pill per venue with an active override — only when the tenant has more than one venue; **+ Add site override** picks a venue, `POST`s to ensure its `website_config` row exists, then `PATCH`es a snapshot-copy of the tenant's current values plus `use_brand_override: true` (a copy, not a live link — later tenant-default edits don't retroactively change an existing override); **Remove override** (inline double-confirm) just flips the flag back off, leaving the venue's stored values in place in case it's re-added later. `BrandIdentitySection`/`BrandThemeSection`/`BrandLayoutSection` all take an optional `venueId` prop (`null` = tenant default via `/website/brand-defaults`, a venue id = that venue's `website_config` via `/website/config?venue_id=X`) — same form fields either way. The standalone per-venue "Identity" page (`PAGES_VENUE`'s `branding` key, `BrandingSection` component) is deleted — it's fully superseded, and covered strictly less than the new override (no theme/typography/layout, and its site_name/tagline never worked as described above).
- ✅ **Expense VAT amount** *(migration 099)* — `cash_expenses.vat_amount` (numeric, default 0) alongside the existing (gross) `amount`, purely informational: never added to or subtracted from anything in reconciliation — `amount` already covers VAT everywhere it's used (`total_expenses`, `Net Cash`/`Cash to bank`). All three write paths accept it (`ExpenseEntrySchema` in the whole-day `PUT /daily/:date`, plus the individual `POST`/`PUT /expenses[/:id]` routes). `CashRecon.jsx`'s `ExpensesSection` and `MobileExpenses.jsx`'s `ExpenseModal` both gained a second "VAT amount" input next to "Gross amount" (relabelled from plain "Amount"), and show a small "VAT £x" line under the gross figure wherever an expense is listed.
- ✅ **Expense paid by card** *(migration 100)* — `cash_expenses.paid_by_card` (boolean, default false). A card-paid petty cash expense is still recorded (receipt, VAT, category) but excluded from Total Expenses, Net Cash and Cash to bank, since the money never left the till. API: all three expense write paths accept the flag; week-detail returns `total_expenses` (cash only) plus `total_card_expenses`. UI: `PaidByCardToggle` + `CardBadge` exported from `CashRecon.jsx`, used by the desktop `ExpensesSection` and `MobileExpenses.jsx`; day summary, week grid and mobile day total show card spend on a separate "Paid by card (not in recon)" line.
- ✅ **Week-grid day headers — visible "Open" affordance** — `SpreadsheetView`'s day column headers (`Tue 22 Sep` etc) were already a full click target (`onClick={() => onSelectDay(date)}`, hover/active background, `cursor-pointer`) but nothing in the cell visually signalled it was clickable beyond the hover state, which touch devices never see. Added a small always-visible "Open ›" line under the status badge in each header cell so the affordance doesn't depend on hover.
- ✅ **`TempChecksTable`/`HoldChecksTable` mobile layout fix** — on a phone-width viewport (the `/mobile/hs-dashboard` Fridge/Freezer and Holds widgets), the sticky Equipment/Station name column's `bg-muted/50` header background was translucent, so as the table scrolled the next header's text (Range, then the first capture-time column) visibly bled through underneath it — the reported "EquipmentRange" overlap. Fixed by (1) making the sticky header cell's background fully opaque (`bg-muted`, no `/50`), (2) hiding the Range (min…max guideline) column below the `sm:` breakpoint, same treatment already used for the Type column, and (3) letting the equipment/station name wrap (`whitespace-normal break-words max-w-[110px]`) on mobile instead of forcing a single `whitespace-nowrap` line, reverting to the old one-line/no-cap behaviour at `sm:` and above. Net effect: on mobile only the sticky name + capture-time columns render, narrow enough that the temp/hold entry column is visible without horizontal scrolling. Both tables are the single shared implementation (per the note on this file) — fixed once, applies to `FoodSafety.jsx`, desktop `HSDashboard.jsx` widgets, and `MobileHSDashboard.jsx` alike.
- ✅ **Tenant selection — login UX overhaul** — investigating the request surfaced that the codebase had already migrated login to identity-only Auth0 (no `organization` param, `TenantGate` resolving the restaurant client-side via `X-Tenant-Id`) in an earlier session that never updated CLAUDE.md's "Auth flow" section or the Docs.jsx "Authentication" section — both still described the original organisation-scoped-login design. Docs corrected (see "Auth flow" above and Docs.jsx). On top of that pre-existing foundation, three behavioural gaps were closed: (1) a single-tenant user could see a one-frame flash of the "Choose a restaurant" picker before the auto-select effect ran — fixed by computing the auto-select candidate at render time (not just inside the effect) and holding the loading state until it resolves, so the picker only ever renders for a genuine multi-tenant, no-valid-history case; (2) the full-page "Choose a restaurant" list is now a proper modal (`TenantSwitcherModal`, new shared component) rather than a bespoke full-screen layout — mandatory (no close) when it's the first-login picker, dismissible when opened voluntarily; (3) the sidebar's tenant `<select>` dropdown (a Standard Design Rule #2 hover/tap-imprecision concern on a tablet) is replaced by a "Tenant" button that opens the same modal — collapsed-sidebar mode now opens the modal directly instead of first expanding the sidebar. "Check last one and log into it" was already correct behaviour (the `maca_selected_tenant` localStorage pick persists across sessions and is re-validated against `/me` on every load) — no backend change was needed for that part.
- ✅ **Mobile Order Sheets — third `/mobile` module** *(superseded by Mobile Purchasing, migration 153)* — `admin/src/pages/mobile/MobileOrderSheets.jsx`, registered in `mobile/registry.js`. Unlike the first two mobile modules, this one needed no body-component rework: the desktop `OrderSheets.jsx` page's list/detail pattern already collapses to a single-column, full-page-detail-on-select layout at phone width (the list is `hidden md:flex` once an order is selected — the same responsive approach `Bookings.jsx`'s `inlineMode` uses), and the item table's horizontal scroll-on-overflow is a fine mobile pattern as-is. `OrderCard`/`OrderDetail`/`NewOrderModal`/`FilterModal`/`ALL_STATUSES`/`DEFAULT_STATUSES` were exported from `OrderSheets.jsx` (previously module-local) and reused verbatim — same `/api/order-sheets/*` endpoints, same autosave/status/delete mutations. Only the shell differs: no page-level title bar or `pl-14` AppShell-hamburger spacing (both irrelevant under `MobileShell`, which already supplies a back-arrow header with the module title), and the list/detail split is strict either/or on mobile (no resizable side-by-side panel, since there's no room for one on a phone). Building this surfaced that the entire Order Sheets feature (templates, categories, orders) had zero coverage anywhere in CLAUDE.md, Help.jsx, or Docs.jsx since it shipped — same "no doc gap flagged ≠ no doc gap exists" pattern as the earlier Menus gap; baseline sections added to all three docs alongside the mobile module itself.
- ✅ **Mobile-view suggestion modal** — `admin/src/components/MobileSuggestModal.jsx`, mounted in `AppShell` (desktop shell only). Detects a touch device in portrait at ≤700px on mount and on a live `matchMedia` orientation-change listener (so rotating into portrait mid-session also triggers it, not just page load), and offers a large centred modal pointing at `/mobile` — "Open mobile view" (navigates there) or "Stay on this page" (dismiss). Either choice sets `maca_mobile_prompt_dismissed` in localStorage so it only ever offers once per browser.
- ✅ **Overview page responsive fix + persistent mobile/standard toggle** — three related fixes surfaced by using the admin at phone width: (1) `Dashboard.jsx`'s page header (`px-6 h-14 border-b`) had no left-side allowance for `AppShell`'s fixed collapsed-sidebar hamburger button (`fixed top-3.5 left-3.5 ... lg:hidden`), so on any viewport below `lg` the "Overview" title/date sat directly under the hamburger icon — fixed by adding `pl-14 lg:pl-6` (the same `pl-N lg:pl-4`-style pattern `OrderSheets.jsx`'s header already used) plus `min-w-0`/`truncate` on the title block and a `hidden sm:inline`/`sm:hidden` pair shortening "Customise layout" to "Customise" on narrow screens so it never wraps against the title. (2) The tile grid was a fixed `style={{ gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, minmax(240px, 1fr))' }}` — at phone width this either forced 4 columns @ 240px+ each (960px+, horizontal scroll) or, if browsers shrank below the minmax floor, badly squeezed tiles; replaced with Tailwind `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4` plus a `SPAN_CLASSES` lookup (`{1: 'col-span-1', 2: 'col-span-1 sm:col-span-2', 3: '... lg:col-span-3', 4: '... lg:col-span-4'}`) on `TileCard` so a tile's configured width (1-4 "quarters") degrades gracefully to half-width or full-width instead of overflowing a grid that doesn't have that many columns yet at that breakpoint. Main content padding also dropped from a flat `p-6` to `p-4 sm:p-6`. (3) New `src/components/MobileViewToggle.jsx` — see Key files — gives every phone-width visitor a persistent way to jump between the desktop admin and `/mobile`, since previously `MobileSuggestModal` only offered a one-time nudge on first detection and `/mobile` itself had no way back to the full admin at all short of editing the URL.
- ✅ **Mobile Cash Up + Mobile Wages — fourth and fifth `/mobile` modules** — see `src/pages/mobile/MobileCashUp.jsx` and `src/pages/mobile/MobileWages.jsx` in Key files for the full write-up. Cash Up gives a plain day-by-day list (status/income/variance) for the current week with tap-through to the full daily declaration; Wages narrows the desktop's 8-column staff grid down to just Name / To be paid / Paid per entry, the two figures that matter when an operator is settling up away from a desk. Both reuse existing `/cash-recon/*` endpoints with no backend changes. Required exporting several previously module-private helpers from `CashRecon.jsx`: `fmt`, `parseNum`, `getMonday`, `isoWeekDates`, `StatusBadge`, `SaveIndicator`, `DayView`.
- ✅ **Sidebar header now shows the tenant name, not the "Macaroonie" wordmark** — `AppShell.jsx`'s expanded-sidebar header used to be a static `MacaroonIcon` + "Macaroonie" label, with a separate "Tenant" block underneath repeating the current tenant's name in its own bordered button. Collapsed into one row: the header now shows the current tenant's name directly next to the logo, with a small `ChevronDown` arrow only when `available_tenants.length > 1` (`canSwitchTenant`) — tapping the name or the arrow opens `TenantSwitcherModal`, same as before. A single-tenant user sees their tenant's name as a plain (non-interactive) label, since there's nothing to switch to; a user with no resolved tenant yet (`available_tenants.length === 0`, e.g. platform admin outside any tenant context) still sees "Macaroonie" as a neutral fallback. The old dedicated "TENANT" strip below the header is gone — its one remaining job, warning when `hasTenants` is true but nothing is currently selected (a rare edge case; `TenantGate`'s mandatory picker normally prevents reaching `AppShell` in this state at all), is now a single amber banner row directly under the header instead of a whole labelled section. The collapsed-sidebar (icon-rail) tenant chevron button is unchanged.
- ✅ **Fixed the fixed hamburger button being completely hidden (and unclickable) on Cash Recon at phone width** — `AppShell.jsx`'s collapsed-sidebar hamburger button was `z-10`, but `CashRecon.jsx`'s own page headers use `sticky top-0 z-10` (four views) or `z-20` (the spreadsheet view) — equal or higher than the hamburger, and a `position: sticky` header creates its own stacking context, so it painted fully over the hamburger rather than just visually overlapping it like Dashboard's title did (see the earlier, milder version of this bug). The practical effect: a phone-width user landing on Cash Recon had no way to open the sidebar at all — the fixed button was there, but invisible and un-tappable underneath the page's own header. Fixed two ways: (1) `AppShell`'s hamburger button raised to `z-40`, safely above every in-page header found anywhere in the app (the highest so far is `z-20`) and below true modals (`z-50`), so it's never coverable by page content again regardless of that page's own z-index choices; (2) all 5 of `CashRecon.jsx`'s page-level sticky headers (`SpreadsheetView`, `WeekView`, `DayView`, `WagesView`, `SettingsView`) given the same `pl-14 lg:pl-4` treatment as `OrderSheets.jsx`/`Dashboard.jsx`, so the button doesn't visually sit on top of the venue selector/title once it's actually visible. **Other pages with the same `sticky top-0 z-10`-or-higher page-level-header shape have NOT been individually audited yet** — grep `sticky top-0 z-1\|sticky top-0 z-2` across `admin/src/pages` for the remaining candidates (`Menus.jsx`, `Website.jsx`, `Timeline.jsx` are plausible page-level instances; several other hits are nested `<thead>`/list-group headers that don't sit at the literal top of the page and are not at risk). The z-40 bump makes the button clickable everywhere regardless, but those pages may still want the `pl-14` visual cleanup — see Outstanding items.
- ✅ **Tablets always get the phone-style collapsed hamburger sidebar, regardless of screen width** — `AppShell.jsx`'s sidebar previously branched on the Tailwind `lg` breakpoint (1024px) alone: below it, a collapsed sidebar became a hidden (`w-0`) overlay opened by a floating hamburger button; at or above it, a collapsed sidebar instead became a permanent `w-14` icon rail (or stayed expanded by default per `sidebarExpandedDefault`), assuming "wide screen" meant "desktop with a mouse." A landscape tablet at the host stand (an iPad landscape is ~1024px, right at that breakpoint) got this desktop treatment despite being held and tapped like a phone. New `IS_TOUCH` check (`navigator.maxTouchPoints > 0`) makes every touch device use the phone-style behaviour unconditionally: sidebar starts collapsed, collapses to `w-0` (never the icon rail), stays a `fixed` overlay (never reserves layout width via `lg:relative`), and the floating hamburger stays visible and tappable at any width. Non-touch devices (mouse-driven desktops/laptops) are unaffected. See the Common-mistakes entry for the exact class-pairing pattern to follow when adding new sidebar `lg:` classes.
- ✅ **Touch devices get a dedicated burger rail; H&S Dashboard iPad fixes** — iPad testing showed the floating hamburger (visible at every width on touch devices since the change above) covering page titles: page spacers were `pl-14 lg:pl-X`, so they switched off at `lg` even though the button was still there. On the Overview, `sm:px-6` also overrode `pl-14` from 640px up. Fixes: (1) on touch devices `AppShell` now renders an in-flow `w-14` rail holding the burger, centred in an `h-14` cell to line up with page headers, so no page has to reserve space for it; the floating button is non-touch-only (`lg:hidden`, moved to `top-2.5` so it is vertically centred on `h-14` headers). (2) New Tailwind variant `notouch:` (`:root:not([data-touch]) &`, `tailwind.config.js`) plus `<html data-touch>` set in `main.jsx`; every page spacer is now `max-lg:notouch:pl-14` (Overview, Cash Recon x5, Order Sheets, Order sheet templates/categories, Timeline, Bookings). (3) `HSDashboard.jsx`: full width instead of `w-[90%] mx-auto`; grid columns use `minmax(0, 1fr)` instead of a 240px minimum, so a 4-column dashboard shrinks to fit instead of overflowing (a first attempt that dropped to fewer columns on narrow screens broke side-by-side layouts and was reverted); the date/tabs row no longer wraps from `sm` up; full screen falls back to a pseudo full screen where the Fullscreen API is missing (iPad home-screen apps). The timeline's full-screen toggle in `AppShell` gained the webkit-prefixed API too.
- ✅ **Cash Recon Dashboard + row spanning on every dashboard** *(migration 101)* — new `/cash-dashboard` page: named dashboards per venue built from six cash widgets (days of the week tiles, day balance, week balance, the editable reconciliation grid, wages paid with Paid tick boxes, petty cash), driven by a week navigator plus a selected day that the day tiles set. No new tables: `hs_dashboards.kind` (`hs`/`cash`) and the same route plugin mounted twice. `HSDashboard.jsx` was refactored into a generic `DashboardPage({ config })` engine used by both pages. The week grid's maths moved into `reconCalc()` in `CashRecon.jsx` so the grid and the balance widgets share one implementation; `SpreadsheetView` gained `hideHeader` for embedding; `MobileExpenses.jsx`'s body became an exported `PettyCashPanel`. New `cash_dashboard` module in the `cash_recon` group (tenant switch and role permissions copied from `cash_recon`), nav link after Cash recon, route catalog and Overview shortcut. Row spanning: `lib/dashboardGrid.js` gives the H&S Dashboard, Cash Dashboard and Overview tiles fixed 120px rows with dense packing, so a tall widget spans several rows and shorter widgets stack beside it; height controls now step in rows and still store `height_px`.
- ✅ **Cash Dashboard week expenses widget** *(migration 102)* — `cash_week_expenses`: read-only list of every expense in the week, grouped by day (category, VAT, Card badge), with cash / card / VAT totals.
