Reads `detail.days[date].expenses` from `useReconWeek()`, so no new endpoint; the migration only widens the `widget_type` CHECK. Adding a widget type means three places: the migration's CHECK, `WIDGET_TYPES_BY_KIND` in `hsDashboards.js`, and `CASH_WIDGET_TYPES` + `renderCashWidget` in `widgets.jsx`.
- ✅ **Cash Dashboard week summary grid widget** *(migration 103)* — `cash_week_summary_grid`: the reconciliation grid's full row set (each income source, SC source and payment channel, section totals, expenses, variance, Net Cash, wages, Cash to bank) with only the WEEK column. Read-only, every figure from `reconCalc()` via `useReconWeek()`, so it always equals the full grid's WEEK column. `ScEffectBadge` is now exported from `CashRecon.jsx` for it.
- ✅ **Staff pay type + Cash Dashboard week staff list widget** *(migration 104)* — `cash_staff.pay_type` (`hourly` | `fixed`, default `fixed`, backfilled from `cash_wage_defaults.entry_type`) decides what `default_rate` means (£/hr vs £/week) and seeds `entry_type` whenever someone is added to a week. Settings → Staff gained a Fixed/Hourly selector, a type/rate badge per row, and a working reorder (the up/down buttons now call `PUT /config/staff/reorder`; they previously only swapped local state and snapped back on refetch). New shared helpers exported from `CashRecon.jsx` — `wageEntryForStaff()`, `defaultWageEntries()`, `PAY_TYPES`, `staffRateLabel()` — replace three hand-copied auto-populate blocks (`WagesView`, `MobileWages`, and the new widget). New `cash_week_staff` widget: draft-then-Save editor for the week's list (pay type, hours × rate or fixed amount, add/remove, copy from one of the last 8 weeks, Set as default), saving through the existing whole-tree `PUT /wages/:week_start` with `cash_amount` carried over by entry id so Paid ticks survive.
- ✅ **Per-widget dashboard options + Days of the week widget upgrades** *(migration 105)* — `hs_dashboard_widgets.settings` (jsonb, default `{}`) stores display options for any H&S/Cash dashboard widget. A widget type opts in by listing `options: [{ key, label, hint }]` in its type list; `WidgetCard` (`HSDashboard.jsx`) renders one toggle per option in Edit layout and PATCHes the whole `settings` object; the API validates only the shape (flat map, ≤20 keys) and writes it with `tx.json()`. First user: `cash_day_tiles` gets `hide_closed` and `compact`, and tapping a day now opens `DayView` in a full-screen overlay (rendered inline, so it stays inside the dashboard's full-screen element) as well as selecting it.
- ✅ **Cash widget header figures** — a widget type may set `HeaderValue` (component) in `CASH_WIDGET_TYPES`; `WidgetCard` renders it in the title bar (not in Edit layout). Week summary grid → week variance, Week expenses → cash total, Wages paid → sum of `cash_amount`. Same query keys as the bodies (`useReconWeek`, new `useWeekWages`), so no extra requests.
- ✅ **Rename dashboard widgets and Overview tiles** — new shared `components/dashboards/TitleRename.jsx` (Edit-layout Name row, explicit Save, "Use default" clears to `null`) used by `HSDashboard.jsx`'s `WidgetCard` and `Dashboard.jsx`'s `TileCard`. Both PATCH routes already accepted `title_override`; only the UI was missing. Also fixed the Overview's "Visible to" (`hidden_role_ids`) button, which never rendered: `TileCard` showed `headerExtra` only when not editing, but the caller built it only when editing.

- ✅ **Variance includes till payouts** — Cash Recon's variance adds back cash expenses (per day) and cash wages (per week), since staff pay both out of the till before counting; the summary is now just the Variance (Net Cash / Cash to bank removed from the grid, day view and dashboard widgets). The `/week` route's per-day totals were also fixed (a two-table join had been multiplying them).
- ✅ **Wages paid on day** *(migration 112)* — `cash_wage_entries.paid_date`: the day a cash wage came out of the till. Cash Recon's day variance adds back wages paid that day (`reconCalc().dayWages()`, `wages_cash_by_date` from week-detail, `wages_cash`/`unassigned_cash_wages` from `/week`), so each day balances; wages with no day still balance only in the week. `PaidDaySelect` in `CashRecon.jsx`; `PATCH .../entries/:id/paid` takes `paid_date`, new `PATCH .../entries/:id/paid-date` works on a submitted week.
- ✅ **Staff & Rota** *(migration 106)* — staff list moved out of Cash Recon settings to its own `/staff` page under a new "Staff" nav section; company-wide **Roles** (`staff_roles`, tip points multiplier); **Rota setup** (tenant-wide `rota_settings.mode` day_parts/hourly + `slot_minutes` 15/30/60, and `rota_shifts` with start/end/points); per-venue weekly **Rota** (names as rows, days as columns; day-parts mode = ON/OFF per shift column, hourly mode = any number of non-overlapping start/end periods per day), explicit Save, copy from a previous week. Pay: hourly (per-shift rates via `staff_shift_rates`, else default) or fixed per week/day/shift (`cash_staff.pay_basis`), per-week pay override, **Fill Cash Recon wages** (upserts `cash_wage_entries` by staff_id, fully-paid rows stay paid). Tips: points = shift points x fraction worked x role multiplier + zero-sum manual moves (`rota_week_staff.points_adjustment`), pot = week's service charge from sources distributed to staff (split sources reported separately), or a manual override (`rota_weeks`). Tips are display-only, never written to wages. **Rota dashboard** (`hs_dashboards.kind = 'rota'`) with rota grid / who's on / pay / tips widgets. New module group `staff` (`staff`, `rota`, `rota_pay`, `rota_dashboard`). Scope decision: roles, shifts and settings are tenant-wide ("roles shared within the same company"); staff and entries are per venue. Verified end to end against a real Postgres as a non-superuser (RLS isolation included). Print / PDF and Save (Share) image buttons on the rota grid export the week as shown (see `rotaExport.js`). Tip shares can be rounded to a tenant-set unit (`rota_settings.tip_round_to` + `tip_round_mode`, migration 107) via `roundTip()` in `rotaCalc.js` (points pots only). **Tip pots** *(migration 108)* superseded the single pot described above: tenant-wide `tip_pots` (name + distribution house | points | manual), each fed by Cash Recon SC sources via `cash_sc_sources.tip_pot_id` (replacing the dropped `cash_sc_sources.distribution` field, which only fed the old pot) plus named manual lines (`tip_pot_lines`) whose amounts are entered per venue week (`rota_week_pot_lines`); manual pots take per-person amounts per week (`rota_week_pot_manual`). `rota_weeks` (single-pot override) dropped. £ tip moves *(migration 109)*: `rota_week_staff.tip_adjustment`, zero-sum, added to each person's pot shares (`tip_share = tip_share_from_pots + tip_adjustment`, floored at 0); `POST .../move-tips`, and `reset-points` replaced by `reset-moves { kind }`. Setup UI: `components/staff/TipPotsSetup.jsx` in Rota setup; weekly UI: `RotaTipsTable` pot cards + per-pot share columns. **Rota tips permission** *(migration 118)*: `rota_tips` split from `rota_pay` so a role can see wages and tips independently; `GET .../pay` needs either (`requireAnyPermission`) and `redactWeek()` strips the half the caller lacks. **Tip adjustments** *(migration 117)*: `rota_tip_moves.action` (`move` | `add` | `remove`, `from_staff_id` only for a move) for one-sided bonuses / take-outs with a required reason; totals return `tips_added` / `tips_taken_out`. **Tip moves as transactions** *(migration 113)*: `rota_tip_moves` + `rota_tip_move_lines` (one giver, one or more recipients, split equally / by amount / by % in the UI via `splitEvenly()`/`splitByPercent()`), `POST`/`DELETE .../tip-moves`; replaced the net `points_adjustment`/`tip_adjustment` columns and the old move-points/move-tips routes. **Tip pot surcharge** *(migration 111, superseded by 116: `tip_pots.surcharges` jsonb `[{ name, pct }]`, applied in order, each on what is left after the ones before, so they compound; pot summary returns `surcharges` with each `amount`)*: `tip_pots.surcharge_name` + `surcharge_pct`; `rotaCalc.js` shares `total = gross - round2(gross x pct / 100)` and reports `gross`/`surcharge` per pot. **Hide closed days** *(migration 110)*: `rota_settings.hide_closed_on_rota` / `hide_closed_on_print`, two separate toggles in Rota setup; closed days from `services/openDays.js`, filtered by `visibleRotaDates()` in `rotaExport.js`; a closed day with entries always stays visible. **Pot line kinds** *(migration 115)*: `tip_pot_lines.kind` (`amount` | `percent`); weekly values in `rota_week_pot_lines` may be negative; `rotaCalc.js` applies % lines to max(0, sources + £ lines) and floors the gross at 0; each line returns `value` (£ effect); `PotCard` has a +/− sign button since tablet decimal keypads lack a minus key. **Tip +/- nudges** *(migration 119)*: `rota_week_staff.tip_unallocated` (net £ per person), `POST .../tip-nudge { staff_id, direction }` moves 0.50 between a person's tip total and the week's unallocated pot (`totals.tips_unallocated`, may stay above 0), `POST .../reset-unallocated` undoes all; `NudgeCells` in `rota.jsx` (the −/+ buttons and the total are separate cells, and every figure in the tips table goes through `TipNum`, which always has a second line, so all figures in a row line up). **Tips widget parts**: the Rota dashboard tips widget has `hide_pots` / `hide_shares` / `hide_moves` widget options, passed to `RotaTipsTable` as `sections`. **Hide print totals** *(migration 114; superseded by migration 194, which dropped the setting: the print and image never show totals now)*: `rota_settings.hide_totals_on_print`, one Rota setup checkbox; `buildRotaSheet({ hideTotals })` sets `sheet.showTotals = false` so the print and image drop the Hours column and the Working count row.
- ✅ **Web ordering (collection) with pluggable payment gateways** *(migration 122)* — guests order on the tenant site (`/order`, `/locations/:slug/order`): menu from the venue's chosen menus, dish options from variant groups (now with `price_mode` base/extra and `min_select`/`max_select`), basket, ASAP or a collection slot (per-venue hours windows, lead time, interval, max orders per slot, order ahead), tips, minimum order, then a payment method; a live status page at `/order/status/:token`; emails for confirmation / ready / cancelled. Staff get the Online orders board (accept with prep time, preparing, ready, collected, reject/cancel with automatic refund, retime, mark paid cash/card, partial refunds, print ticket, sold out per venue, pause, report) and Ordering setup; `/mobile/orders`. Payments go through `services/paymentGateways/` only; `pay_at_venue` and a pre-prod `demo` gateway ship, Stripe (Stage 1) is one more gateway file. Dishes gain `is_orderable` and takeaway / eat-in VAT rates; VAT follows the order type (`vatRateFor()`: collection/delivery = takeaway, table orders = eat in), with a venue default per type. Platform fee: `tenants.ordering_fee_percent` (platform admin, Platform page), stored per order as `platform_fee_pence` (subtotal x %, tips excluded), shown in the report; the Stripe gateway will take it as `application_fee_amount`. Decisions (Sep 2026): direct charges on the tenant's own Stripe account (tenant = merchant of record), one Stripe account per tenant, collection only, pay on collection allowed, manual/auto accept and tips toggles per venue. Orders table has `channel` for the future POS. Also fixed the app-wide error handler (it was registered after the routes, so no route ever used it; every error body was Fastify's default with the status text in `error`). See Docs.jsx's Web Ordering section.
- ✅ **Online ordering block options** — block data (`title_text`, `subtitle_text`, `hide_subtitle`, `basket_mode` side|modal, `basket_sticky`, `basket_bg`/`basket_text` theme roles, `show_vat`, `sticky_nav`, `sticky_offset`, `show_search` + `search_style` bar|icon_phone|icon, `highlight_favourites` + `favourite_label` + `favourite_bg`/`favourite_text` theme roles, `item_notes`) → `orderOptions` in `blocks/online_ordering.eta` → `shared/ordering.eta` (defaults there; root classes + `--mo-*` CSS vars; sticky `.mo-nav` holding search + tabs with a scroll highlight; sticky `.mo-basket-col`). Items carry `featured` (`menu_items.is_featured`) and now keep `vat_rate` in `publicItem()` (it was stripped, so the guest page's VAT was always 0). `head.eta` uses `overflow-x: clip` on `html, body` so `position: sticky` works on tenant sites at all. Dish cards in the basket get `is-in` + a `.mo-in-basket` count badge (`inBasket()` in `itemCardHtml()`). `sticky_offset` 0 = automatic: the page script measures the site's sticky header (`findTopBars()`/`measureHeader()` in `shared/ordering.eta`) and sets `--mo-offset` on `#mo-root`.
- ✅ **Promotions for online ordering** *(migration 131)* — `promotions` (tenant-wide: kind basket | item | bogo, % or pence, dish/section scope, buy/get/get_percent, min spend, required dishes, max discount, apply_mode auto | manual | code + unique case-insensitive code, max_uses, schedule in the menu-schedule shape, venue_ids, exclusive, sort_order = priority, badge_text) + `order_promotions` (copied name/code, discount) + `orders.discount_pence` / `order_items.discount_pence`. One engine, `shared/promotions.js` `applyPromotions()`, called from `priceBasket()` (server and guest page). Migration 132 adds kinds `free_item` (cheapest covered dishes free once qualified, spend excludes them), `bundle` (meal deal: `bundle` parts + `bundle_price_pence`, dearest-fit, as many deals as the basket makes) and `tiered` (`tiers` `[{ min_pence, value }]`, highest reached), with guest nudges that carry a Choose / Add button (`showPromoDish()` in `ordering.eta`). Admin `/promotions` (`Promotions.jsx`, drag = priority, copy, on/off, uses + discount given), API `/api/promotions`, module `promotions` (group web_ordering). Guest page: discount lines, tap-to-apply offers, spend-more / add-more hints, promo code box (`POST /order-api/venues/:id/promo-code`), dish badges, promo box (migration 134: `show_in_box` + `box_bg`/`box_text`/`box_image_url`, one-at-a-time card above the basket / under the title, `promoBoxHtml()`; replaced the offers strip), `?promo=CODE` / `?offer=<id>` deep links, promos kept in `maca_promos_<venueId>`. Staff: order detail, ticket, confirmation email, report (discounts + per-promotion use). **Promo block** (`promo_cta`): one promotion with a button to `/order` with the offer ready, banner or voucher card, hidden when the promotion isn't running (`site_promotions` from `sitePromotions()` in `siteRenderer.js`, live data, not snapshotted). See Docs.jsx's Web Ordering → Promotions. Promotion queries filter `tenant_id` explicitly (not only RLS), and the editor's dish choices open in a pop-up (`DishPickerModal`), with the chosen dishes shown on the page as removable chips.
- ✅ **Heading block** — `heading` page-builder block (content): eyebrow, heading (tag h1-h4), line below, size, align, font (theme heading/body or a listed Google font, loaded by the block itself), weight, italic, capitals, bar / rules decoration, theme-role colours, background, spacing. Styles in `shared/headingBlock.js`, shared by the SSR partial and the canvas through `it.siteBlocks`.
- ✅ **Minimum order quantity per dish** *(migration 133)* — `menu_items.min_order_qty` (NULL or 2-99), edited in the Menus dish panel's Online ordering box (`MinQtyInput`), badge on `ItemRow`. `loadOrderingMenu()` sends it as `min_qty`; `priceBasket()` errors (`code: 'min_qty'`) when a dish's qty over all its basket lines is below it, so both the quote and `createOrder()` reject it; the guest page shows Min N on cards and in the dish sheet, starts at the shortfall and removes a line rather than go below.
- ✅ **Online ordering menu times + scheduled menu changes** *(migration 130)* — `ordering_settings.menu_schedules` jsonb `{ menuId: { from, until, times: [{ days, start, end }] } }`, edited per chosen menu in Ordering setup (`ScheduleEditor`, `components/orders/ScheduleEditor.jsx`, shared with Promotions). Scheduled menus may not overlap each other (from today on); unscheduled menus are always on. Guests see each menu's hours; `/order-api/.../slots?items=` offers only times when every basket dish is on; `createOrder()` rechecks with `itemOffAt()`. Rules in `shared/menuSchedule.js`.
- ✅ **Rota sickness and time off + Clear rota** *(migration 129)* — `rota_entries.status` (`on | sick | pto | uto | abs`). `RotaGrid`'s per-name `ModeButton` picks what a tap puts in (ON/OFF, SICK/OFF, ...); a tap flicks a cell between OFF and that status, switching any other status to it. Hourly periods carry a status in `PeriodEditor`. `computeRotaWeek()`: only `on` counts as hours / days worked / points; `pto` is paid like working; sick/uto/abs unpaid; rows add `paid_hours`, `paid_days`, `absence_hours`, and fill-wages writes `paid_hours`. Copy from week copies `on` entries only. Codes and print colours: `ENTRY_STATUSES` in `rotaExport.js`. **Clear rota** (toolbar) sets the draft to `[]`. The print sheet has body padding as well as `@page` margins, since Chrome's Margins Minimum/None replaces `@page`.
- ✅ **Menu section panel, section VAT, printed colours, Dietary tags** *(migrations 127, 128)* — `Menus.jsx`: sections edit in `SectionDrawer` (title, subtitle, image, highlight, print options, VAT), same shape as the dish `ItemDrawer`; only one panel is open at a time; the section row shows badges; section delete is an inline confirm in the panel. `menu_sections.vat_rate_takeaway` / `vat_rate_eat_in` (127) and `vatRateFor(item, fulfilment, settings, section)`: dish, then section, then venue default. VAT inputs use `RateInput` (keeps typed text). Both print layouts set `print-color-adjust: exact` on tag chips and tinted boxes. The Dietary groups page is now Dietary tags at `/menus/dietary-tags` (`MenuDietaryTags.jsx`, `DietaryTagsManager.jsx`, add/edit `TagModal` using `PATCH /menus/dietary/:id`); 128 moves existing nav links.
- ✅ **Boxed inset mobile values save** — `ThemeSchema`'s `boxed_steps` entry in `routes/website.js` only listed `value`/`unit`, so Zod stripped every step's `mobile_value`/`mobile_unit` on save and phones kept the desktop inset; both are now in the schema. The Layout card's step inputs (`BrandLayoutFields.jsx`) now use a buffered `StepNumberInput` (clamping `Number('')` to 0 made the field impossible to clear and retype), and `BrandLayoutSection` shows a save error. Pages & modals' **Save details** is disabled until the form differs from the saved page (`detailsDirty` in `PagesSection`). The step pickers (Layout's default step and mobile override rows, block inspector's Boxed inset) show each step's phone value; the mobile override row shows only the phone value.
- ✅ **Designable ordering page + Online ordering block** *(migration 126)* — `/order` and `/locations/:slug/order` render the tenant's built-in page (`website_pages.system_key = 'order'`, seeded by the migration and `ensureOrderPage()` in `services/orderPage.js`, listed as "Order online" in Website → Pages & modals, can't be deleted/unpublished/moved) through `page.eta`; its `online_ordering` block (`blocks/online_ordering.eta`, editor `OnlineOrderingEditor.jsx`, canvas placeholder) includes `shared/ordering.eta`. The block can go on any page; `siteRenderer.js`'s `loadOrRender404()` attaches `ordering_venues` to every bundle for it. Also fixed the ordering page on phones (tab strip and cards overflowed the screen; `minmax(0, 1fr)`).
- ✅ **Online ordering GDPR consent + newsletter** *(migration 125)* — checkout's "Your privacy" section: a required order-only-use box (covers allergy notes, which are health data; `OrderBody.data_consent` must be `true`) and an optional unticked newsletter box. Wording and privacy-policy link come from `loadPrivacy()` in `orderSvc.js` (returned by `GET /order-api/venues/:id`, recorded verbatim in `orders.consent` + `orders.data_consent_at`). Newsletter status lives on `customers.marketing_opt_in` (+ `_at`, `_source`, `marketing_opt_out_at`); `POST /api/customers/:id/unsubscribe`, `GET /api/customers?…&newsletter=1`. Anonymise and export now cover web orders.
- ✅ **H&S follows opening days** *(migration 124)* — `tenants.hs_follow_opening_days`, one tenant-wide switch (H&S settings button on Food safety, Checklists, H&S Dashboard; owner/admin). When on, a venue's closed days (booking schedule via `services/openDays.js`, unscheduled venue = always open) expect no daily checklists and no fridge/hold/cooking checks: Checklists Today moves daily ones to a dimmed "Not needed today (closed)" group, the three pages show a closed-day banner, and the Overview H&S tiles show Closed. Weekly/monthly checklists stay due; out-of-range readings on a closed day still flag. Rota's `open_dates` now uses the same `openDatesOrNull()`.
- ✅ **Menu print designer** *(migration 121)* — `/menus/:id/design`: hand-place a menu's printed layout on snap-grid pages (header, section titles, whole sections, single dishes, text, images, lines, footer notes, allergen key, page numbers), drag to move, corner to resize, blocks can repeat on every page. Stays linked to the menu (blocks store a section/dish id + grid position; content read live; new dishes appear in the "Not placed yet" tray). Once saved, the print route (admin Print + website PDF link) renders the design; `?auto=1` shows the automatic layout. One renderer, `shared/menuLayout.js`, for the print page and the designer canvas. Needed the menu save to keep section/item ids (it re-issued new ones every save before). See Docs.jsx's Menus section. **Variant columns**: variant label + price pairs 1-3 to a row — `print_settings.variant_columns` (automatic layout) and `print_layout.variant_columns` with per-block `opts.variant_columns` (designer). **Fold**: `print_layout.fold` (`none | vertical | horizontal`) + `fold_gap_mm` + `fold_line` split each page's grid into two halves with a gap at the fold (`colStart`/`colEnd`/`rowStart`/`rowEnd` in `shared/menuLayout.js`); the designer maps pointer positions through `colAtMm`/`rowAtMm`. **Fonts**: `print_layout.font_body`/`font_heading`/`font_script` (default Inter/Fraunces/Caveat) and per-block `opts.font`, any font from the website builder's list (`shared/fonts.js`); Page setup's "Use website fonts" copies `tenant_site.theme.typography` heading/body (from `GET /:id/design`'s `site_fonts`); the print page loads only the fonts in use (`layoutFontsUrl()`).
- ✅ **Page builder previews every page in the site's template** — the canvas (`ThemeFrame`, the template label, boxed-step values) read `template_key`/`theme` off the record being edited, which only the tenant home (`tenant_site`) has, so extra pages, modals and location pages previewed as Classic with default colours. `PageBuilder.jsx`'s `brandConfig` now uses `tenant_site` for every page, with the venue's `BRAND_OVERRIDE_FIELDS` laid over it when `use_brand_override` is on (same rule as `mergeLocationConfig()`).
- ✅ **Menus: drag dishes to reorder** — dishes drag by their grip within their own section (one `DndContext` per `SectionEditor`, Pointer/Touch/Keyboard sensors, vertical + parent-element modifiers); ↑/↓ buttons kept. Sections themselves still reorder with ↑/↓ only.
- ✅ **Menu designer: footer notes pick** — callouts block `opts.callout_ids` (absent = all notes) via `pickedCallouts()` in `shared/menuLayout.js`; `menu_callouts` rows now keep their ids across menu saves (`upsertMenuTree()` inserts `c.id`, `ensureIds()` in `Menus.jsx` covers callouts), and `/duplicate` remaps the pick.
- ✅ **Several print designs per menu + section visibility** *(migrations 135, 136)* — `menu_print_designs` (name + layout, ordered by `created_at`) replaced `menus.print_layout`; `menus.print_design_id` picks the one the print route uses (NULL = automatic), `?design=<id>` prints any. Designer: `DesignBar` (pills, Printing badge, Copy, New design; locked while dirty), open design in `?design=`, Page panel "This design" (rename, use for printing / automatic, delete). `/duplicate` copies every design through `remapLayout()`. `menu_sections.visibility` (`show | website_hidden | hidden`): website_hidden is left out of the site's Menu (inline) block (`loadInlineMenus()`), the builder canvas, `GET /menus/public/:id` and online ordering (`loadOrderingMenu()`); hidden also doesn't print (print route filter + `buildContext()`/`placementSummary()`), and the designer says so on a placed block (`missingText()`). Edited under "Where it shows" in the Menus section panel.
- ✅ **Food safety setup moved to H&S settings + cooking sessions reworked** — H&S settings is now one window (left nav, venue picker): General, Fridges & freezers, Fridge check times, Hold stations, Hold check times, Cooking sessions (`components/hs/HsSetup.jsx`). The Food safety page's Equipment tab, the Holds tab's station/time lists and the cooking panel's gear are gone; empty check lists open settings on the right section. Cooking sessions: drag to reorder (`PATCH /cooking-sessions/reorder`, new ones go last); the session is picked in the temperature pop-up (`CookingEntryModal`, default last picked else `sessionForNow()`), `PATCH /cooking/:id` accepts `session_id` to move a reading; Today's checks is grouped by session with n/required per group. Deliveries have no setup (supplier typed per delivery).
- ✅ **Rota pay adjustments + one wages list** *(migration 137)* — `rota_pay_adjustments` (extra | deduction | advance | advance_repay, note, `source_id` repay → advance): an advance adds to this week's pay and creates an `advance_repay` in the next week (edited with it while unchanged, deleted with it). `carried_owed`: last week's Cash Recon wage rows with `0 < cash_amount < total` add the difference to this week's rota pay. `computeRotaWeek()` returns `base_pay`, the adjustments, `pay` (floored at 0) and `pay_shortfall`; fill-wages writes `pay` and lists adjustments in notes. UI: Adjustments column + `PayAdjustModal` in `rota.jsx`. `cash_wage_reports.status`/`submitted_at` dropped: wages aren't submitted. `WeekWagesEditor` (`components/cashRecon/WagesTable.jsx`) replaced WagesView's grid, the two wage widgets and MobileWages' list with one Name / Total / Paid / Paid on / Notes editor, explicit Save.
- ✅ **office / ops hosts + platform page with register interest** *(migration 138)* — admin at office.macaroonie.com, the phone app at ops.macaroonie.com (its own origin, so Android installs it separately; `/` → `/mobile/`, SPA sends other routes to `/mobile` when `IS_OPS_HOST`, `/ops.webmanifest` with scope `/`), macaroonie.com = platform page + register-interest form (`platform_interest`, global, no RLS; optional `INTEREST_NOTIFY_EMAIL`). `office`/`ops` added to `RESERVED_SUBDOMAINS`. The move itself is server-side (nginx scripts + Auth0 URLs), done by hand in the order in Docs.jsx. Also fixed `mobile.webmanifest`'s `start_url` (`/mobile` was outside its `/mobile/` scope).
- ✅ **Wage widget display options** — both Cash Dashboard wage widgets take `WAGES_OPTIONS` (`widgets.jsx`): `hide_manage`, `hide_bulk`, `paid_only`, passed to `WeekWagesEditor` as `hideManage` / `hideBulk` / `paidOnly`. No migration (widget `settings` jsonb).
- ✅ **Dish option prices no longer reset** — `ItemDrawer`'s `setOverride()` in `Menus.jsx` read `g.overrides`, but a group loaded from the server carries its overrides as `options[].overridden`, so editing one option dropped the others (back to the group default, often £0). `overridesOf()` reads either shape and is used by `setOverride()` and the save payload. Variant groups' option price boxes now use `PriceInput`.
- ✅ **Test data: past H&S logs and completed checklists** *(migration 139)* — the Test data page's "Generate past health & safety logs" fills a chosen past range (up to today, max 366 days) with fridge/freezer and hold readings inside each unit's limits, cooking checks (random published-menu dishes, each session's required count), past order sheets on template delivery days, delivery checks matched to placed order sheets (supplier, items, chilled/frozen temperature by category), and past checklists/audits completed with every task ticked. Optional share of out-of-range readings with corrective actions, skip closed days, pick which checklists. `is_generated` on every table it writes so "Clear generated" removes only its rows; `fs_delivery_checks.order_sheet_id` links a check to its order. See `services/hsTestDataSvc.js` and Docs.jsx → Test Data Tools.
- ✅ **Website blocks on extra pages and modals load their data** — `menu_inline`, `gallery` and `reviews_band` blocks were only hydrated from `home_blocks` and a location's `page_blocks`, so on a `/p/:slug` page or a modal a menu block showed the editor hint "Menu not configured — pick one in the inspector" on the public site even with a menu picked. The tenant and location bundles now pass every page's `blocks` to `loadInlineMenus()` / `loadGalleryItemsForBlocks()` / `loadReviewsForBlocks()`, and `menu_inline.eta` renders nothing when there is no menu. Builder hints say "block settings" instead of "inspector".
- ✅ **Staff paid by cash, bank transfer or a split** *(migration 140)* — `cash_staff.pay_method` (`cash` | `bank` | `split`) + `bank_amount` (split: that much by bank each week, the rest cash in hand), set on the Staff page ("Paid by"). `computeRotaWeek()` splits each person's `pay` into `bank_pay`/`cash_pay` (totals `pay_bank`/`pay_cash`) with `splitPay()` in `shared/payMethod.js`; RotaPayTable shows Bank and Cash columns when anyone has a bank part (Rota page) or when the Hours and pay widget's `show_bank` option is on. `cash_wage_entries.bank_amount`: fill-wages writes it; `WeekWagesEditor` has a Bank column (always on the Wages page and /mobile/wages, behind the `show_bank` option on both Cash Dashboard wage widgets), refills it from the person's method when a Total is typed, and Full pays only the cash part. Carry-over to next week's rota pay is now total − bank − cash for rows with some cash paid.
- ✅ **Tabs block + full-screen page builder** — `tabs` layout block: tabs (name, link anchor, drag order, up to 10) each holding other blocks; style underline/pills/boxed, alignment, active colour, phone tabs or drop-down sections; a hash matching a tab anchor or anything inside a tab opens it. Containers generalised via `SLOT_FIELD` in `blockTree.js`; no container inside a container. Page builder **Full screen** button hides the app and website menus (`maca_builder_fullscreen`).
- ✅ **Order sheets: Hide zero lines** *(superseded: order sheets were replaced by Purchasing, migration 153)* — `OrderDetail` (`OrderSheets.jsx`, also used by `/mobile/order-sheets`) has a Hide zero toggle beside the search box: only lines with a quantity show, plus `keepIds` (lines that had one when the toggle was turned on or the order loaded) so a line stepped to 0 doesn't vanish mid-edit; searching bypasses it. Remembered in `maca_order_sheet_hide_zero`. No API change.
- ✅ **Ops app tidy-up** — `MobileHSDashboard.jsx` is logging-only (layout editing removed; `DashboardTabs` framed strip with scroll arrows). `MobileShell` no longer mounts `MobileViewToggle target="standard"`. `WeekWagesEditor` gained `hidePaidOnForAll`; `MobileWages` passes `showBank={false}` + `hidePaidOnForAll`; cards layout has a name header strip; Pay everyone in full is a solid green button; the totals hide Bank when `showBank` is off. `DayView` (Cash Recon day, desktop and `/mobile/cash-up`) shows a read-only Wages paid today card and warns about cash wages with no Paid on day. `MobileHub.jsx` tiles are a two-column grid.
- ✅ **Mobile Allergens module** — `/mobile/allergens` (`MobileAllergens.jsx`, registry key `allergens`): the read-only `AllergenLookup` in its `phone` layout. Dish rows in every lookup now tap open to show the description and each tag by name. No API change.
- ✅ **Allergen matrix + allergen lookup** *(migration 141)* — Menus > Allergen matrix: menu picker, search, one column per dietary/allergen tag, tap to toggle, Save writes only `menu_item_dietary` for the changed dishes (`PUT /api/menus/:id/allergens`, `menus` manage). Read-only `AllergenLookup` as an H&S dashboard widget and an Overview tile (tap a tag once = contains, twice = free from). Migration 141 widens the widget and tile CHECKs and adds the nav link under Menus.
- ✅ **Allergens separate from dietary tags, four levels** *(migration 145)* — `menu_dietary_tags.kind` (dietary | allergen) + `standard_key` (one of the 14 UK allergens, unique per tenant); `menu_item_dietary.level` (contains | may_contain | removable; no row = doesn't have it; dietary tags always contains). The migration turns tags matching a standard allergen by code or exact label into it and adds the missing 14 for every tenant (new tenants: `ensureStandardAllergens()` from `POST /api/platform/tenants`). Dishes carry `dietary` (all codes) + `allergen_levels` (non-contains only) through `loadMenuFull()`, `loadInlineMenus()`, `loadOrderingMenu()`, the save payload and `/duplicate`. Menus show May contain like Contains and Can be removed with a small asterisk + "* Can be removed on request" note; printed keys list only used tags (now `allergenKey()`, see the allergen notice item). Page Menus > Allergens & dietary (two lists, drag order `PATCH /menus/dietary/reorder`, type, merge `POST /menus/dietary/:id/merge`, `POST /menus/dietary/standard-allergens`); dish panel `AllergenPicker` in `Menus.jsx`; matrix and lookup show levels apart. Seeds use `nuts` instead of the old `n` tag. **Hide a tag on menus** *(migration 146)*: `menu_dietary_tags.show_on_menu` (TagModal On menus Show / Hide); off = left out of every guest surface (filtered in `loadPrintMenu()`, `GET /menus/public/:id`, `loadInlineMenus()`, `loadOrderingMenu()`, and `MenuInlineCanvas` via `menuTags()`), still in the matrix, lookup and menu editor.
- ✅ **Checklists: Tick all, completion follows ticks; cooking items** *(migration 142)* — `PUT /checklists/instance` dropped `mark_complete`: an instance is `completed` when every active task is ticked (first who/when kept), `in_progress` otherwise. `ChecklistRunPanel` has Tick all; dashboard cards use `ChecklistHeaderAction` (Tick all, All done, confirmed Untick all). H&S settings Cooking items: `fs_cooking_settings.item_source` (menus | own | both, per venue) and `fs_cooking_items` (name, category tab, drag order); `fs_cooking_checks.cooking_item_id`. Picker and test data generator both use `loadCookingPicker()`.

- ✅ **AI assistant: bookings by chat and MCP** *(migration 148)* — one tool layer (`api/src/ai/tools`) for four channels: the staff assistant (Ask AI drawer, `/ai`, `/mobile/assistant`), a staff MCP connector per tenant (`/mcp/staff/:tenantSlug`, personal access tokens `mcp_...` made on `/ai` Connect), the website `ai_chat` block, and a platform-wide guest MCP connector (`/mcp`). Staff tools write through the admin booking routes in-process (`callApi()`), so RBAC is the admin's, plus a Bookings permission check per tool. Guest tools go through `/widget-api`; guest change/cancel needs an emailed 6-digit code (`ai/codes.js`). In chats, change tools become Confirm cards (`ai_pending_actions`); nothing changes until the person presses Confirm. Chat model: `AI_MODEL` (default in `config/env.js`) with `AI_EFFORT`, adaptive thinking, server-side fallbacks (`fallbacks: 'default'`), prompt caching, history stored unchanged and append-only. Usage per tenant per month per channel (`ai_usage`); chats stop at `ai_settings.monthly_token_limit` (Platform page). Off without `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (MCP still works). New module `ai_assistant` (bookings group). Booking moves for the guest manage page now go through the shared `services/guestBookingSvc.js`.

A new booking past the limits goes through `POST /bookings/admin-override` on `allocateBestFit({ ignoreMinCovers: true })`'s table (a combination sends its member tables), else the Unallocated row; a move uses `rescheduleBooking(..., { staff: true, overrideLimits: true })`. Times outside the booking times or blocked (cap 0) still fail. Also: `ensureUnallocatedTable()` in `occupancySvc.js` (admin-override made the row only in its displacement path and 422'd otherwise), admin-override checks the venue and tables are the caller's tenant's, and a move to a slot with no covers limit no longer fails. Activity marks bookings made past the limits.

Saved in `platform_secrets` (global, no RLS, value never returned, `hint` = last 4); a saved key wins over `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, removing it falls back to the env var. `PUT` checks the key with Anthropic first (`models.retrieve(AI_MODEL)`, free) and refuses a rejected one. `src/ai/apiKey.js` resolves the key (15 s cache per PM2 process); `chat.js` rebuilds its client when the key changes and turns an Anthropic authentication error into a 503 "key isn't working". - ✅ **AI assistant: attach photos, screenshots and files** *(migration 150)* — staff chats (Ask AI, `/ai`, `/mobile/assistant`) take photos and screenshots (JPEG/PNG/GIF/WebP, 5 MB), PDFs (10 MB) and text/CSV files (1 MB): paperclip, paste, drag and drop, and a camera button on touch devices. Files upload first (`POST /api/ai/attachments`, kind checked from the bytes, stored in `ai_attachments` with no conversation), then go with the message by id (`/chat` `attachments: []`, message may be empty), max 5 per message and 10 / 20 MB per chat. The stored message holds image / document blocks with an `_attachment` reference only; `chat.js` fills in the bytes for every model request, so the history stays small and replays unchanged. A 400/413 from Claude on the first call withdraws the message (`withdrawMessage()`): the files go back to unsent and the chat puts text and files back in the composer. Sent files show as thumbnails / chips, served to their uploader only (`GET /api/ai/attachments/:id`). The staff prompt says to report what it read and treat file text as information, not instructions. Tested against a local database and a fake Claude (blocks, replay, limits, other user 404, withdraw) and in a browser at 1015 px and 390 px.
- ✅ **Rota tips: half-point +/− on points, spare points, carry-over** *(migration 151)* — `rota_week_staff.points_nudge` (net per person per week). `POST .../points-nudge { staff_id, direction }` moves 0.5 (`POINTS_STEP`) between a person's points and the week's spare points (minus 422 under 0.5 points, plus 422 under 0.5 spare points), `POST .../reset-points-nudges`. `computeRotaWeek()` shares each points pot over people's points plus the spare points, so taking points off someone changes nobody else's share; the spare part is `pot.unallocated` / `totals.tips_unallocated_points`. Unallocated tips carry into the next week: `carriedIn()` in `rota.js` (earlier weeks' £ taps plus what their spare points were worth) → `computeRotaWeek({ carriedIn })` → `totals.tips_carried_in`, `tips_unallocated` (£ that + taps can hand out), `points_unallocated`, `tips_carry_out`. UI: `PointsCells` in `rota.jsx`, the Unallocated box (spare points, carried in, carry-out), the Changed column (`points_adjustment + points_nudge`), Undo point taps; the Cash Dashboard tips payout widget says what carries over. Tested against a local database (others' shares unchanged, + refused without spare points, next week sees the carry and can hand it out, earlier weeks unaffected) and in a browser.

- ✅ **Custom domains reach the site** — a tenant's verified domain showed nginx's bare 404: no server block named it, so nginx answered from its default port-80 server (certbot's `return 404` block for the apex). New `scripts/nginx-custom-domain.sh` (server block proxying everything to the API, certificate, redirect names, notes when the app doesn't know the domain). `routes/website.js`: `cleanCustomDomain()` (lower case, no scheme/path/port/trailing dot, hostname only, refuses our own root and another tenant's domain), verification cleared only when the domain changes (the Domain section sends it on every save, so switching Site is live used to take a verified domain offline), `platformIps()` (A records checked against what the site's own address resolves to, `APP_PUBLIC_IPS` optional), `server_status` on dns-setup and verify. Domain section: `DomainServerNotice` (with the command for platform admins), link only once https; guide step 5 no longer claims automatic certificates. Tested against a local nginx with the API behind it (35 checks) and the Domain section in a browser. No migration.
- ✅ **Custom domains switch on by themselves** — the domain sync (`scripts/custom-domains-sync.sh`, systemd timer every minute + a path unit on `/var/lib/macaroonie-domains/kick`, installed by every deploy through `scripts/install-domain-sync.sh`) runs the domain script for each verified domain without an https block: DNS must point only at the server (A records in what the root resolves to + `APP_PUBLIC_IPS`, any AAAA too) else `waiting_dns` with the reason; the www / bare twin goes with it when its DNS points here, or later; failed certificates back off 5 min / 15 min / 1 h / 6 h / daily (a kick retries after 2 min); blocks for domains no tenant has saved are removed with their certificate. State per domain in `<domain>.json`, heartbeat `last-run`, off switch `disabled`. The domain script keeps https for the main name when adding a twin fails, takes a lock, closes it for children. API: verify (when verified) and a domain change write `kick`; dns-setup / verify return `server_auto` + `server_state`. Domain section: progress notice (switching on / waiting for DNS / http only with next try), refetch every 10 s until https, manual command only when the sync isn't running. Tested against a local nginx, Postgres and the API with certbot stubbed (84 checks), the installer (systemd-analyze verify) and the Domain section in a browser (21 checks). No migration.
- ✅ **Till PINs work as soon as they are set + live updates across API processes** — a PIN set in Till setup didn't sign in on an open till: the till only knew the hashes from its last config, the `pos.config` push reached only sockets held by the same PM2 process (cluster, `instances: max`), and otherwise the till reloads its config every 10 minutes. `config/ws.js` now relays every `broadcast()` through Redis pub/sub (`macaroonie:ws`, origin-tagged so nothing arrives twice; local delivery still works with Redis down), which also fixes timeline / orders pushes. `staffForPin()` (`pos/src/lib/store.js`) fetches the config on a miss (online, at most every 5 s) and looks again. `hashPin()` throws "Open the till at https:// to sign in" without WebCrypto, `PinPad` shows errors instead of hanging on "Checking…", and `routes/posSite.js` redirects the pos host from http to https (X-Forwarded-Proto). Tested with two API processes and Redis (push across processes, Redis down and back, no doubles), the till in Chromium against the other process with pushes cut (new PIN signs in, wrong PIN, insecure origin message), the redirect, and `shared/pos` tests. No migration.

Our button id is `mcrn-gp-launch-…` so the search never matches it. Guestplan documents no open call and its script couldn't be fetched from the build environment, so this was tested only against stand-in scripts (plain, shadow DOM, opens itself, iframe, blocked); check it on the live site. No migration.

Screens keep in step through a server clock (`/screen-api/time`), clock-based rotation (`playlistPosition()`), the next board drawn 3 s early, transitions and animations timed from the shared clock and videos nudged every second (playbackRate or seek). Also: a new config re-fits what is on screen (turning a screen didn't apply until the board changed), a reopened socket fetches the config (changes made while a screen was offline waited for the minute poll), view-only people can play the playlist preview, Media upload accepts Screens manage, the designer shows videos still until Play. Dropping files (user report, 16 Oct 2026: "dragging dropping images doesn't work very well"): in the board and menu designers the browser's own picture drag cancelled the block move (reproduced: `dragstart` on IMG then `pointercancel`); fixed with `noImageDrag` + `user-select` / `-webkit-touch-callout: none`. - ✅ **Kitchen flow designer, runners, order ready boards per board** *(migration 169)* — Till setup → Kitchen screens → Kitchen flow: boxes (Orders in + stations of kind prep / expo / runner / board) and links with filters (dish, section, revenue centre, order type); from each point a dish takes the most specific links (ties all apply), its whole way worked out when sent (`item.route`), arrival = every node before it done. Expo / runner bumps finish dishes still upstream (`show_upcoming`), recall / undo refused once a later station is done (`moved_on`), remake at prep clears that station onwards, at expo / runner restarts the dish. Ready = every dish has reached its boards and runners; a runner at the end of every dish's way hands the order over (collected). `kds_routes` became `kds_flow_edges` (start links per station, every prep to every expo); kitchen orders cleared. Kitchen screen: From / Then / At labels, Also-on-this-order line, DONE at runners, Ready panel at expos and runners. Order status block: board picker (`opts.board` by name, `boards[name]` in the feed), stacked layout, ready first, preparing box colour, change animation (float / fade / pop / zoom) from the player's `orderChanges()`. Also fixed: the change animation was skipped when the draw fell in the same millisecond as the change (fractional clock offset). Tested: 23 kitchen + 18 screen layout unit tests, 77 API checks (incl. the brief end to end), 31 kitchen screen and 49 admin / player browser checks (1280 / 1015 / 390, view only).

Streams pick the order types their tills offer and per type ask for a customer name / tent number (off / ask / required, enforced at counter start, Send and Pay); kitchen orders carry type, type_name and tent; flow filters and the kitchen screen's tabs use the tenant's types; day figures by stream and type. The migration moved the dish / section eat-in and takeaway VAT into tax settings (takeaway % as an exception for Take out, Delivery, Collection), rewrote release snapshots (re-hashed at API start) and closed days' figures. Also fixed: the Menus section and dish panels never scrolled (a `<fieldset>` can't be a scroll container in Chromium); counter checks now show the customer name in the header; the kitchen screen's tabs no longer shorten names over 8 letters to initials; report rows keyed by label warned on duplicate names. - ✅ **Combos** *(migration 171)* — the user's design (16 Oct 2026): a combo is a menu item (`kind = 'combo'`) with its own price (can be £0) and steps (min / max with 0 = optional, the same choice more than once, pick itself with one choice, wait behind a button, show only after a pick in an earlier step), choices from dishes, whole sections or typed in (kitchen name), each with an extra charge; a picked dish keeps its own options (its base options never change the deal price, extras add). Answers: automatic combos group and reprice (items stay, saving as a discount, only when cheaper, re-checked on every change), on the till and online; a manual combo starts from its button or Make it a combo on a till line; choices can be items, sections or new. Online: one step per page, last page summary, basket parts, combo discount rows, `order_items.parts`, `order_promotions.combo_item_id`. Kitchen: parts under the combo, each routed by its own dish (typed-in parts like the combo). The migration turned the till's meal deal slots into steps in the menu tables and every release snapshot (deterministic choice ids, hashes recomputed at start). Repeated picks show as "2 x Cheese" / "2 x Ketchup pot" everywhere (`groupModifiers()`, `countParts()`, grouped online options). A kiosk is a till device (`kind = 'kiosk'`) whose orders are counter checks made of till events (`kioskOrder()`), so they reach the Counter list, the kitchen flow, Till reports (kiosk named) and Cash Recon unchanged; the kiosk rules allow only its own counter orders, menu dishes, automatic offers and `card_external` payments (test reader for now). - ✅ **Pepper: the staff assistant for every area** *(migration 173)* — the user's ask (17 Oct 2026): "pepper extended to mcp and chat tools across all sections and functions" (Pepper = the assistant of their COGS platform). The staff AI assistant is renamed Pepper (`ASSISTANT_NAME`, `shared/assistant.js`; nav link and module label, own module group `pepper` instead of Bookings) and gains 59 tools in six new files (`tools/rota.js` 16, `cash.js` 8, `orders.js` 10, `venue.js` 11, `site.js` 13, `general.js` 1) next to bookings, H&S, purchasing and menus, in the chat and the staff MCP connector. Every tool checks the page's own module (`requireStaffPermission()`, refusals worded per module), reads and writes through the admin routes with `callApi()`, and every change is a confirm card from one plan function shared by `describe()` and `run()`, audited with `audited()`. The staff chat uses tool search (`tool_search_tool_bm25_20251119`, `defer_loading` on all but `CORE_TOOLS`, search blocks replayed unchanged, `pause_turn` continued, `MAX_STEPS` 12); the guest chat and MCP send tools in full. `open_page` answers with an in-app link (ChatView renders only same-origin paths as `<Link>`). The older `find_customer` was folded into `find_customers` (adds the next booking). Also fixed (found while building): online orders — `loadAdminOrder()`, status, payment, refund and promised-time changes and the report didn't check the order / venue was the caller's tenant's (another tenant could read guest details and refund by id); schedules, venue settings and test data took any tenant's venue id (`requireOwnVenue()` in `middleware/venue.js` now on those plugins). Tested: 66 rota, 33 cash, 40 orders, 39 customers / venue, 31 website / support tool checks against Postgres with two tenants (permissions per role, cards, writes, other tenant refused), 25 chat-loop checks against a stand-in Claude (tool search blocks, deferred tools, pause_turn, a refusal as a tool error, the MCP tool list and a call), 21 browser checks (sidebar, drawer, links, cards, Pepper page at 1015, phone at 390). Cards pin what they were worked out with (`pin` from `describe()`, see Common mistakes): the cash, rota, online order and H&S change tools; 13 checks through the chat loop (a refund changed on the board in between is refused at Confirm).
- ✅ **Pepper: long jobs, one writer per chat, fair use, erasure; fresh-database builds** *(migration 174)* — the review's Now list (17 Oct 2026). **Timeout**: `scripts/ensure-nginx-ai-timeout.sh` (every deploy) gives `/api/ai/` 300 s in nginx (was the general 60 s: a 504 while the turn carried on); the chat loop starts no new step after `TURN_MS` (240 s) and replies "say carry on" (stop reason `time`), only between whole steps. **Batches**: `create_menu` / `add_dishes` take up to `BATCH_DISHES` (50) dishes, `import_price_list` 100 rows (a whole menu in one tool input was cut off at `MAX_TOKENS`); `add_dishes` can name a menu whose `create_menu` card still waits (`loadForAdd()` plans against that card's input, the add card says "(new menu)", its run refuses until the create card is confirmed); a cut-off tool call gets `CUT_OFF` telling the model to split it. **One writer per chat**: `holdConversation()` / `withHold()` in `chat.js` on `ai_conversations.running_until` (90 s lease, renewed every 30 s, lapses if the process dies) around `runTurn()`, `confirmAction()` and `cancelAction()` (the old in-memory Set only covered one PM2 process; a Confirm note between a tool call and its result broke the chat for good). **Usage in units**: `usageUnits()` (output 5x, cache write 1.25x, cache read 0.1x or less for the models in `CACHE_READ_WEIGHT`, by the model that answered); `ai_usage.units` (backfilled), `ai_usage_people` per person per day; `person_daily_pct` (default 25, null = none) checked before each staff turn and AI translation draft (429); Settings tab people card and Fair use and privacy card (daily share, `chat_retention_days` 30 / 90 / 180 / 365, explicit Save); Platform page in units. **Erasure and retention**: `ai/erase.js` — customer anonymise calls `eraseFromAi()` in its transaction with every name / email / phone on the customer, their bookings and orders: whole-word matches replaced with [removed] in messages, cards, titles and the audit (`ai_actions.redacted_at`), thinking blocks and files dropped, open cards cancelled, the chat closed (`closed_at`, `closed_reason` erased; read only, ChatView shows Start a new chat). `cleanUpAi()` hourly (`ai-cleanup` queue): idle chats past retention deleted, audit details after a year, unsent uploads / used codes after a day, per-person usage after 400 days. **Small fixes**: a submitted day's expenses can't be changed or deleted (`assertExpenseOpen()`); `setSoldOut()` only takes the tenant's own dishes (draft or a live release); 22P02 with a uuid = 404 (was 500), 4xx logged at warn; `place_order` pins `planned_delivery` and refuses if a cutoff moved the day; issue log `?scope=all` for platform admins (in a tenant's view they see that tenant's). **Fresh database**: 062 / 063 / 090 TRUNCATE with CASCADE in one statement, pg_cron optional (001 tries, 006 schedules only if present); `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (empty Postgres 16 migrate twice + API start + shared unit tests). Tested: 20 batch, 16 lock (two API processes), 22 usage, 28 erasure, 21 small-fix checks against Postgres with two tenants and a stand-in Claude; 24 browser checks (Settings at 1280 / 390, closed chat, issue log scope, Platform panel); every migration on an empty Postgres with and without pg_cron, twice; both nginx scripts against a real nginx (insert, rerun, both together in either order, old media block, bad config restored) and a 65 s stand-in turn through nginx; the earlier Pepper suites again.
- ✅ **Pepper streams its answers; everyday model with routing** *(migration 175)* — the review's Next list (17 Oct 2026), A1 and A5. **Streaming**: every model call is `callModel()` (`client.beta.messages.stream` + `finalMessage()`, `max_tokens` 32,000); with `Accept: text/event-stream`, `POST /api/ai/chat` and `/ai-api/chat` answer as server-sent events (`ai/sse.js`: start, text, activity, step, card, discard, done | error; opens on the first event, so refusals before the message is stored stay JSON; 15 s heartbeat; `X-Accel-Buffering: no`); a streamed turn may run 600 s (`STREAM_TURN_MS`), JSON 240 s. Admin `api.stream()` + `readEvents()` (45 s idle = lost), `ChatView.jsx` live items, activity line, cards held until the turn ends, `notice` in italics, `running` polling after a drop (GET conversation returns `running`); the website `ai_chat.eta` the same in plain script. Claude errors after retries = 503 "Claude is busy". **Routing**: `ai_conversations.model_tier` fast | main (NULL = main); new chats start on `AI_MODEL_FAST` and `upgradeChat()` moves them to `AI_MODEL` for good on a file, a `HEAVY_TOOLS` call (handed over "Not run yet", remade by the main model), a 60K-token request or a refusal; no `fallbacks` on the everyday model. `usageUnits()` prices per model (`PRICES`, $4 per million units). Also fixed: the stop notices (time, max_steps, refusal) never reached the screen; a refused message stayed in the history and got the next message refused too (now withdrawn when nothing of the reply was stored; a new chat refused outright is deleted, `conversation_id` null). Tested: 53 API checks against a streaming stand-in Claude (events, routing triggers, refusal paths, errors after start, withdrawal, website chat, real HTTP timing), the earlier Pepper suites (127 checks) with routing off, 29 browser checks (drawer, refused new chat, phone 390, website chat, a killed API mid-reply).

The sweep: every route with params as tenant B with A's ids and B's own mixed in (~53,000 calls; leaks by A's mark or ids, changes by an md5 per table, 500s), every write A made sent again as B (also with B's ids swapped into the address), no row of B's holding an id of A's (plus link tables without a tenant_id). Pepper's chat loop ported from the scratch suite (13 tests). **Fixed, found by it**: food-safety equipment / check times / hold stations / cooking sessions / deliveries, checklist templates and reviews took any venue id in the body (now `refuseOtherTenantsVenues` on every route); a table's section, a combination's tables, a staff role, a Cash Recon day's sources / channels / SC sources, a wage week's and wage default's staff (the default returned the other tenant's staff names and pay), online ordering's menus, a dish's option groups and options, an override's sittings (added under another tenant's override) and translation keys all took another tenant's ids; also the team routes' own-user lookups now filter the tenant. No migration. Tested: `npm test` (17 tests, about 90 s); the sweep reported the body venue ids and the mixed-id cases before their fixes (the wage default and translation keys were found by reading the routes it pointed at) and passes after.

Kitchen events (`kitchen.item_done`, `bumped`, `recalled`, `remake`, `priority`, `collected`; server-only `order_in`, `order_status`) are POS events through the same sequencer; `kds_orders` is their projection and `kds_log` the bump / remake log. - ✅ **Row-level security that holds for the app's own role** *(migration 176)* — the review's Next list (17 Oct 2026), C1. The app's database role owns the tables, so the RLS policies never applied to it: every query relied on its own tenant filter. Every table with a `tenant_id` now enables and forces RLS with one policy (the transaction's tenant, or system mode `app.bypass_rls = 'on'`); `table_combination_members` follows its combination, the feature board is readable by every tenant, unclaimed screen pairings by anyone with the code, `sweep_expired_holds()` switches system mode on for its DELETE, and the membership function became a system-mode query (`membershipSvc.js`). `config/db.js`: `systemSql` / `withSystem()` (second pool, connections start in system mode) for look-ups by token, slug, device or id, background jobs and the platform pages; `tenantSql()`; `withTx()` removed. Every query that ran on the bare pool moved to one or the other. Migrations run in system mode; 176 refuses to run (listing them, with the fix) when tenant tables belong to another role. Also fixed: the booking widget's venue endpoint read a deposit column that doesn't exist (every call was a 500). Tested: `api/test/system-paths.test.mjs` (every path that finds its tenant itself) and the existing suites as a role that isn't a superuser (CI does the same), the owner check on a scratch database (stray tables listed, superuser and fixed tables pass).

Ready / collected in the kitchen sets the board status (`onlineMoves()` + `afterKitchen()` in `posApi.js`, with `kds_online_ready`). Till setup → Kitchen screens → **Test the kitchen** opens a new window on the till app (`/?kitchen_test=<signed link>`, 8 hours, pos_setup view) with every kitchen screen of the venue (as set up, or one per station), each the real kitchen app in a scaled frame (tablet / TV / upright sizes, screens across, one bigger). - ✅ **Kiosk asked for a PIN after pairing; the till app updates itself** — the user's report (17 Oct 2026): "I tried to map kiosk using pos.macaroonie.com but it ask me for pin and throws undefined error". Cause: the browser was running the copy of the till app its service worker kept from before kiosks were deployed (#332, 06:33 that day); that copy saved the device as a kiosk but ran the till screens with the kiosk's config, which has no staff, so the PIN check failed with "Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'find')" (reproduced with the pre-kiosk build). A reload fixes such a device. Fixed for the future: `boot()` routes by kind through `openOtherApp()` (an unknown kind gets a Getting the latest app screen, never the till), the kind in the config reply (or a cached config) wins over the saved one (`relabel()`), `staffForPin()` reads `cfg.staff || []`; and `pos/src/lib/appUpdate.js` registers the service worker itself (`injectRegister: false`), checks for a new copy every 30 minutes and when the page becomes visible, and reloads after a `controllerchange` once the screen allows it (till: nobody signed in, 10 s idle; kiosk: start screen, empty basket, 10 s; kitchen screen: 30 s idle); kiosk previews and the kitchen test bench never register or reload. No migration. Tested: 16 checks of saved states in Chromium (kiosk saved as a till with and without a cached config, the old copy's state, an unknown kind, a real till still a till), the pre-kiosk build reproducing the error, 16 checks on a production build with its service worker (a new build: the till on its PIN screen reloads into it, a kiosk mid-order doesn't until it is back on its start screen, no second reload), the bench browser checks and the shared tests again.
- ✅ **Pepper knows today's date** — the user's report (17 Oct 2026): Pepper answered about Saturday 10 October and said "the app's clock for the venue says today is Saturday 10 October". Nothing gave the model the date unless it called `list_venues`; `day_overview` required a date, so it guessed one, then blamed the clock. `nowNote()` in `ai/chat.js` puts `[Now: <date> (<YYYY-MM-DD>), <HH:MM>.]` at the tenant's venues (one per time zone) before every staff chat message, joined with the page line by `contextNote()` into one hidden `_context` block (older messages keep `_page`); the staff prompt says to use it and never guess a date or blame the clock; `day_overview`'s date is optional (today at the venue). No migration. Tested: `api/test/pepper-help-briefing.test.mjs` (the line before every message, hidden from the chat and the title, not in the system prompt, `day_overview` today, venues in two time zones around midnight) and `ai-chat.test.mjs` (31 together).

Also colour pickers in screens and other builders, can you have the default theme colour swatches and add custom colour in addition". The grid was a 1px CSS gradient inside the scaled board (`transform: scale(0.24)` at the usual zoom), so the lines rendered at a quarter of a pixel and never showed; snapping itself worked. `GridLines` in `ScreenDesigner.jsx` now draws an SVG beside the board in screen pixels (a line every snap step, or every few so they stay 10 px apart; every fifth darker; z-index 3), the toggle is `aria-pressed` with a filled state and is remembered in `maca_screen_grid`. Colours: the shared `components/ColourPicker.jsx` (theme swatches from `/api/me` `site_theme`, then a custom colour) behind the screen designer's `ColourField`, the kiosk designer's colours (plus a Website colours set to start from), tag colours and a `ColourMenuButton` for the email editor's text colour; the website builder's `ThemeColourPicker` gained a custom (rainbow) swatch storing `#rrggbb` (`allowCustom`). Readers checked for codes: all already passed a code through except `footer.eta` and its canvas (fixed); the promo block now uses dark text on a light custom background (SSR and canvas); `resolveWidgetDefaults()` now resolves the tenant default confirmation buttons' roles (they reached the widget as `#primary`). No migration. Tested: 39 browser checks (grid on / off / zoom / remembered, swatches set and save board colours, custom and typed codes, Reset, 1015 px on one line, operator view only, kiosk Website colours, website custom colour saved and replaced, tag modal, email editor panel) and a staging render of the footer with a code and a role; the API test suite.
- ✅ **Sealed integration keys, connector tokens that expire, a connector list per person** *(migration 179)* — the review's Next list (17 Oct 2026), B2, B3, B4. **Keys**: the Anthropic key and the kiosk preview signing key (`platform_secrets.value`), Loyverse tokens, GloriaFood poll keys and email provider keys (`provider_api_key`, the SES secret now in its own `provider_secret` column instead of `provider_domain`, `smtp_pass`) are stored sealed with AES-256-GCM (`services/vault.js`, master key `SECRETS_KEY`); keys saved earlier are sealed at API start; production refuses to start without a valid key, and every deploy adds one to `api/.env` when missing (`scripts/ensure-secrets-key.sh`, also in `deploy.sh`).
