- ✅ **Each device on its own address; Disconnect on every device** — the user's asks (18 Oct 2026): "kiosk, kds, pos, screen need option in setting to disconnect", then "actually we need these devices with separate urls please". Tills stay at `pos.`, kitchen screens move to `kitchen.` (`kds.` forwards with a 301), kiosks to `kiosk.`, menu screens stay at `screens.`: the one `pos/` build is served on all three (`routes/posSite.js`, manifest named per host so each installs as its own app on Android) and opens the app for its address (`pos/src/lib/appHost.js`). `POST /pos-api/pair` takes `app` and refuses a code for another kind before using it (422 `wrong_address`, the Pair screen links to the right address with `#pair=` and it pairs by itself). Devices already paired on the till address get a Move bar (`MoveNotice`): `POST /pos-api/move` hands a one-time code (10 minutes) to the new address, which pairs as the same device; waiting events, taps and paid kiosk orders are sent first; the old address remembers where it went (`maca_moved_to`, `MovedAway`). Disconnect: till (Till tab, manager approval, refused with unsent events), kitchen screen (settings panel, unsent taps counted), kiosk (staff panel, waiting paid orders sent first, refused while any are left), menu screen (its own new settings panel: five taps or five OK presses, arrows, Escape); `POST /pos-api/disconnect` / `/screen-api/disconnect` clear the secret so Till setup / Screens show Not paired with the device's name and settings kept. Till setup shows each kind's address (`device_urls`, `deviceUrl()`); kiosk previews and the kitchen test bench open on their kind's address. Also fixed while testing: the player's fifth OK press opened its panel and pressed the panel's first button too. No migration. Tested: `api/test/device-addresses.test.mjs` (6 tests: addresses, wrong address refused and still usable, disconnect for a kitchen screen and a kiosk, move, a menu screen, hosts and manifests; two mutations caught), 45 browser checks on the real hosts mapped to the harness (pair per address, the kiosk button with the code, Disconnect on all four devices, Move from the till address and the old address forwarding, the player's settings by taps and by remote keys, Till setup's addresses), the full API suite.
- ✅ **Purchasing: orders that need approval, splitting orders for another day** *(migration 180)* — the user's ask (18 Oct 2026): "a user may be able to request only, second person amend and order. Also we can split requested or placed order into 2 separate orders for different days." New module `purchasing_approve` (Purchasing approval; owner / admin / operator manage by default, and the migration copies each role's own Purchasing level onto it, so nothing changes until a role is lowered). `supply_orders.status` gains `requested` (`requested_by` / `_at`, `requested_lines` = what was asked for, `supplier_options` = days and notes wanted, `return_note` / `returned_by` / `_at`, `split_from`); `purchase_orders.requested_by`, `amended_at` / `amended_by`, `split_from`. Routes: `POST /orders/:id/request` (Purchasing manage), `/return` (approval, reason required), `/withdraw` (who asked, or an approver), `/split` (a request; approval); `/submit` now needs approval and starts from the request's days; `POST /pos/:id/split` (approval: a second PO with the same prices, notes and delivery charge; a sent original goes back to To send as amended and both are emailed when the supplier takes email). PO quantities and cancel need approval; send / mark sent / date / receive take either module. `orderActions()` puts `can_edit`, `can_request`, `can_place`, `can_return`, `can_split`, `can_withdraw`, `can_delete` on every order and the screens show only those. Copies say Amended / Split off (`poChangeNotes()`). Admin: Waiting for approval cards and a count on the Order tab, the Send for approval window (days wanted, note for the manager), `RequestPanel`, Asked for N on changed lines, Split for another day (request and PO), Send back, Decline, Changed / Split chips, links between split POs. Pepper: `request_order`, `order_requests`, `send_back_order`, `split_order`; `place_order` / `update_order` / `order_form` take `order_id`; `place_order` refuses without approval (points at request_order); day briefing part `orders_to_approve`. Tested: `api/test/purchasing-approval.test.mjs` (9 tests incl. Pepper); the fixtures request, split, send back and split a PO, so the sweep covers the new routes (full suite); 42 browser checks (operator and owner at 1280, 1015 and 390, view only).
- ✅ **Staff connector: OAuth sign-in for claude.ai and ChatGPT** *(migration 181)* — the user's report (18 Oct 2026): "when i try to connect staff MCP, it prompts me for oAuth login with app key, rather then token generated by pepper connect section". Claude's custom connectors (and ChatGPT) can't send an Authorization header, so they always run OAuth; the connector pointed them at Auth0, which has no dynamic client registration, so they asked for a client ID and secret. Macaroonie is now its own authorization server (`routes/mcpOauth.js`): the protected-resource metadata names the connector's origin; authorization-server metadata (also openid-configuration); `POST /oauth/register` (public clients, https or localhost redirects); `GET /oauth/authorize` (unknown apps or addresses get a page, other problems go back to the app; S256 PKCE required; the tenant from `resource`) sends the person to the admin's Connect page (`/connect-ai`, `pages/ConnectAi.jsx`): which app, which restaurant group, Read only, Works for 30 / 90 / 180 days or a year, Allow or Cancel; someone from another group can only cancel, a person in another of their groups is offered the switch; Pepper off or no Pepper access is said there. `POST /oauth/token` swaps the code once (a second use revokes the token) for an ordinary access token named "<app> (connected app)", listed and revoked with the others on the Connect tab. `ai_oauth_clients` (global), `ai_oauth_grants` (forced RLS, found by id or code in system mode, deleted by the hourly clean-up a day after they expire), `ai_access_tokens.oauth_client_id` / `oauth_grant_id`. Also: a login that starts on a deep link now comes back to it (`appState.returnTo` + `onRedirectCallback` in `main.jsx`), and the Connect tab explains both ways to connect. Tested: `api/test/mcp-oauth.test.mjs` (5 tests: discovery from the 401, registration rules, the whole sign-in to a working connector token, wrong verifier / address, a code used twice, read only, Cancel, an old request, Pepper switched off, the other tenant), 22 browser checks (the Connect page at 1280 and 390, Allow and Cancel back to the app, the token on the connector, the Pepper page's list and wording, another group's owner, no request).
- ✅ **Take a station out of a kitchen flow** *(migration 182)* — the user's report (18 Oct 2026): "i have no way of removing a station from the flow ?! i can delete a station, but no way to take it out of the flow". Every flow drew every station of the venue, so the only way to stop dishes reaching one was deleting it (for every flow). `kds_flows.off_station_ids` (uuid[]) are the stations a flow leaves out: the designer (`KitchenFlow.jsx`) doesn't draw them; tapping a box offers Take out of this flow (asks first, naming how many links go; its links in this flow go with it), Not in this flow above the canvas lists them with Put back; `PUT /api/pos/kds-flows/:id` takes `off_station_ids` (no list = none out), refuses a link touching one and any id that isn't the venue's station, and works out warnings from the stations in the flow only. Routing is unchanged: with no link reaching it, `itemRoute()` never sends a dish there, and the kitchen screen's flow list (from the links) leaves it out. Copying a flow keeps the list. Tested: a test in `api/test/kitchen-flows.test.mjs` (saved and listed, other flows keep it, link refused, unknown / another venue's id refused, viewer and other tenant, copy, a dish sent while the flow in use leaves the wok out skips it, put back; two mutations caught), the full API suite, 26 browser checks (take out with Keep and Yes, links go, Put back, save, reload, another flow still draws it, 1015 px, view only).
- ✅ **Balance boxes: share tickets between two kitchen stations** *(migration 183)* — the user's ask (18 Oct 2026): "for the flows i need a balanced mode, a way to split tickets into 2 kds screens based on the volumes of specific items/variants ... ticket comes with 10 wings, goes to wingman 2, next ticket for 8 wings goes to wingman 1, any other items on these tickets go to fryer"; their choices: fewest pieces to cook, pieces set on the box, a big ticket stays on one station. A station of kind `balance` (Add to the flow → Balance box; `settings.pieces`, the On order counts member shape, edited with `MembersEditor` in `KitchenSection.jsx`) shares the dishes that reach it between the stations it links to. `shared/pos/kitchen.js`: `itemRoute()` unchanged (the designer's Check a dish shows both, "Wingman 1 or Wingman 2"); `resolveBalances()` / `collapseBalance()` take the box out of a dish's way keeping the picked station; `makeBalancePicker(cfg, at)` per event (a ticket stays together; replaced by `balanceTicket()`, next item): lowest `stationLoad()` (fired, not done, weighed by `memberCount()`, a dish not listed counts its quantity), a tie to the first after the last pick. Run in `lines.sent` (held lines keep the box until `course.fired`) and `kitchen.order_in`. The sequencer adds `cfg.balance = { open, last, picks }` for those events (store `loadOpenKitchenOrders()`, `loadBalanceTurns()`, `saveBalanceTurns()`; `kds_balance_turns`), the test bench keeps both in memory. `flowProblems()`: links out only to prep / expo / runner, no filters; `flowWarnings()`: fewer than two to share with. Boxes are never on kitchen screens (`BUMP_KINDS` / `isBumpKind()` wherever stations are listed for screens and the bench), refuse bumps, and pass dishes straight through like boards (`noBump()`). Making a station a box drops its links to non-bumping stations and its links' filters (`tidyBalanceBox()`); pieces must name the tenant's dishes (`checkPieces()`). Tested: 4 shared tests + 1 bench test (route collapse, fewest pieces, turns, a ticket together, held course decided when fired, online orders, flow checks; two mutations caught), an API test through the real sequencer (pieces refused for another tenant's dish, filters refused, alternating picks and `kds_balance_turns`, no bump, not on a screen, kind change tidying; a mutation caught), the full API suite (the sweep replays the box with the other tenant's dish), 18 browser checks (add, pieces, link in and out, Shared, Check a dish, save, panel, station row, screen picks, 1015 px, view only).
- ✅ **Balance boxes: a choice for big tickets** — the user's ask (18 Oct 2026): "Re big tickets - can you make it balancer box option how to handle". A box's `settings.split_above` (null = keep each ticket on one station, the default; N = share a ticket when more than N of its pieces reach the box), `StationSettings` in `posSetup.js` (0-999, nullable), **Big tickets** in `StationModal` (`KitchenSection.jsx`: Keep each ticket on one station / Share big tickets + a number, empty saves as 20), the station row and the flow designer's box note say which. `shared/pos/kitchen.js`: `balanceTicket(items, cfg, at)` replaced the per-event picker: called with the dishes `lines.sent` sends (not held ones), `course.fired` fires and `kitchen.order_in` brings, it decides every box (`flowBoxes()`: upstream first) on their ways in place; units are dish lines or meal deal parts (`unitPieces()`: a deal's own options count with its first part at the box); keep mode sends them all to the lowest `stationLoad()`, share mode sends each, biggest first, to the lowest load plus what this ticket already put there, ties taking turns; the last pick goes to `cfg.balance.picks` as before. `itemFromLine()` / `itemFromOnline()` no longer decide boxes. No migration. Tested: 2 new shared tests (keep, at the limit, shared with load, one line never split, turns, meal deal parts split, online order shared; five mutations caught), the API test through the sequencer (validation, a three-line order shared 2/1 by load, the last pick, back to keep), 21 browser checks (default, the choice, number, save, row and panel wording, emptied number, back to keep, pieces kept, 1015 px, view only), the full API suite.
- ✅ **Installer app: link tills, kitchen screens, kiosks and menu screens from a phone** *(migration 184)* — the user's ask (18 Oct 2026): "build another pwa on installer subdomain where user can link screens, kds, kiosks, pos,etc. Mobile first design, user can also add new POS or Screen." Devices now show their own code: a till, kitchen screen or kiosk that isn't set up asks `POST /pos-api/pairings` for a six-letter code (`pos_pairings`, 15 minutes, `app` = the address's kind so the code can only become that kind) and shows it with a QR code of `linkUrl(code)` (`installer.<domain>/link/<code>`, drawn by the new dependency-free `shared/qr.js`), polling `GET /pos-api/pairings/:id` every 4 s; the menu screen player draws the QR code beside its code. Someone signed in links it: `POST /api/installer/lookup` says what the code is, then `POST /api/pos/venues/:id/link` (new device, or `device_id` = a new device for one already there: it takes the code's secret, the old device stops) or the screens claim. The installer app is the admin build on `installer.<domain>` (`routes/installerSite.js`, own manifest, `IS_INSTALLER_HOST` sends the other trees to `/install`): home (venue, Link, Add, the four lists with state from `GET /api/installer/venues/:id`, every 10 s), Link (code box, an in-app scanner where `BarcodeDetector` exists, new or one already here, name and settings), Add (kind, address with a QR code, or a Till setup code made here), a device page (Save sends the whole settings, Identify / Reload, move to a new device, remove asking twice); phone first, 48 px targets. Till setup gets **Link a code shown on the till / kitchen screen / kiosk** and a line pointing at the installer; typing a Till setup code into a device stays ("Have a code from Till setup?"). Rate limits on both pairing routes raised (a venue's devices share one address). Tested: 4 API tests (`api/test/installer.test.mjs`: link a kitchen screen and sign in with its secret, wrong kind, a code used twice, a new device for a till with the old secret refused, another tenant's device, a test address's code as a kiosk, view-only and viewer refusals, another tenant can't see a linked code or link into the venue, run-out codes, a menu screen's code; the installer address serves the build with its manifest, http redirected; two mutations caught), the system-paths test, the full API suite (101), the sweep (fixtures link a kitchen screen), 3 QR unit tests (capacities, patterns and format bits, a known code; checked with a QR reader at every version and level and under damage), 38 browser checks (QR codes read back from screenshots of the kitchen screen and menu screen; the phone links a kitchen screen, a menu screen with a board and a till by a code made in the installer; a new device for a till; remove; a kiosk in portrait; operator and viewer; Till setup's link button; 390 and 1015 px with no sideways scroll).
- ✅ **Kiosk design layout options, hexagons, section tiles, split photo** *(migration 185)* — the user's ask (19 Oct 2026): "restart button in the bottom bar, also have an option to show back button and restart as icons only. Design option to hide the left menu, design option to hide header bar, option to change product images to round or oval (no text bg in this layout), also option to present tiles as interlocking hexagons (whole design hexagonal styled), also option add random spin option to wheel selector. split image ..."; their answers: no side menu = a custom section tiles grid, the split photo shows on the dish page from the picks, from groups and steps you switch on. **Start again** (`RestartButton` in `Menu.jsx`, its confirm sheet) moved from the top bar to the bottom bars after Back (menu, basket, name / table number, pay; not the card reader or the builder). Design `layout`: `tiles` (cards | round | oval | hexagon), `header` (the top bar; off: no `TopBar` on the menu, basket, builder or allergens, `LanguageButton` in the bottom bar, a combo shows its name and price in the page), `icon_buttons` (`.k-btn-label` hidden by `.k-icons`, `aria-label` kept). `menu.rail` `none` + `menu.section_tiles` [{ section_id, image_url, title, size }]: `startOrder()` opens section `'tiles'`, `SectionTiles` shows the home page above a tile per section (the design's order first), a section opened from it gets Back in the order bar. Round / oval: CSS only (`.k-tiles-round` / `.k-tiles-oval` on `.k-root`: transparent cards, photos as circles / ellipses, the picked ring on the photo). Hexagon: `HexGrid` (measured width, rows of n and n - 1 on 2n half-columns, cells w x w/0.866 with -h/4 margin) + `HexTile` for dishes, grid choices and section tiles; hexagon ends on buttons, chips, cards, keys; flat hexagons for round buttons and badges; a masked honeycomb `::before`. Wheel: `wheel_spin` (Spin shown, default on), `wheel_spin_picks` (a spin turns to a random choice that can be picked and calls `onTap` when it stops; the card under the pointer is hidden while it turns, Pick disabled). Split photo: `menu_variant_groups.kiosk_split` (live, in `kioskConfig()`'s groups) and a combo step's `split` (stored only when true, published with the menu); `splitImages()` decides; `SplitPhoto` clips each full-size picture to its part with seeded jagged tears and a paper band; on the dish page, the combo's photo and the order line. - ✅ **Kiosk: dish and choice pictures set apart; choice hexagons lock together** — the user's ask (19 Oct 2026): "isolate variants design and item design with separate control for images, also in hex flow show variants tiles interlocked as well". Design `layout.option_tiles` (cards | round | oval | hexagon, defaults to `layout.tiles` when not set, so designs keep their look) beside `layout.tiles`; the root gets `.k-opts-<style>` beside `.k-tiles-<style>`, and kiosk.css keys every choice rule (`.k-opt`, `.k-opt-img`, `.k-opt-thumb` (new: list and wheel thumbs, which used `.k-thumb`), `.k-opt-tick` / `.k-opt-less`, `.k-slide-focus`) on `.k-opts-*` and every dish rule on `.k-tiles-*`; the kiosk-wide hexagon look (buttons, chips, cards, honeycomb background) stays with hexagon dishes. `OptionGroup.jsx` and `Builder.jsx` pass `option_tiles` to the displays. Choice honeycombs: `hexLayout(total, max, compact)` in `shared/kiosk.js` (moved out of the HexGrid so it is unit-tested) gives `HexGrid` its positions; `compact` (GridDisplay) makes two rows from three hexagons on while they fit (3 = 2 + 1, 4 = 2 + 2, 5 = 3 + 2, else rows of max and max - 1 with the last centred), fixed columns with cells capped at `maxCell` (1.3 x target), so a few choices no longer sit in one straight row; dishes still fill the width. Tested: 2 new shared tests (picture styles; honeycomb shapes, interlock and width for 0-20 hexagons at three widths), `api/test/kiosk-design.test.mjs` (option_tiles round trip and in the preview config), 52 kiosk browser checks (portrait and landscape: round dishes with hexagon choices and no hexagon buttons, 4 flavours as 2 + 2 measured, the dish photo following the dishes, list and wheel thumbs following the choices, hexagon dishes with card choices, oval choices, basket thumb, combo pages), 12 shape checks (3, 4 and 7 choices at portrait, landscape and a 1080 x 1920 totem, every row interlocked), the previous release's 136 kiosk checks, 20 designer checks (panels, preview following each control, save, dishes changed alone, reload, 1015 / 390, view only).
- ✅ **New location with Pepper** *(migration 186)* — the user's ask (19 Oct 2026): "AI new store deployment with all bells and whistles, AI prompts based on the initial input, scopes at high level, deep dive where required, produce a short brief summary of deployment and creates a new location within a tenant". Venues → New location with Pepper (`/new-location`, nav link after Venues): the person describes the store; Pepper (`askPepper()`, structured output, the tenant's venues and a summary of what each has from `venueSummary()`) returns a plan with a mode per area (copy from a chosen location / new / skip), up to three rounds of questions (choice, yes / no, several, date, time, number, text; leave to Pepper; skip), a closer look per area (`deep_dive`) and a brief (headline, summary, highlights, assumptions, risks, follow-ups); changes by request (`change`) or by hand (PATCH overrides: name, web address, time zone, currency, address, opening date, copy from, each area's mode). Every plan goes through `normalisePlan()` (also the problems list and the preview of what each area makes). Create (`createStore()`, one transaction): the venue, then per area in order: opening hours + sittings + caps + overrides, sections / tables / combinations / disallowed pairs, booking rules, website page (contact from the plan, pages without a system key), booking emails, online ordering (off until switched on), Deliveroo links, the venue's own tax rates, till (new PIN salt, the receipt address from the plan, revenue streams, floor, printers and routes), kitchen stations and flows (the active flow remapped), devices (unpaired copies; screens with new codes), Cash Recon setup (owner / admin), food safety setup, checklists, dashboards, purchasing (supplier venue settings without account numbers, order lists, pars scaled, venue prices), staff named in the plan (roles matched by name). Copies get new ids and every id pointing at the template's own rows is moved to the copies or dropped (`copyTable()`), never left pointing back. A later opening date adds a "Not open yet" closure until then. Areas the person can't set up are left out with a follow-up; the follow-up list (`buildFollowups()`: link devices, printers, website, ordering, bookings, suppliers, team, GloriaFood / Loyverse links, areas left for later, Pepper's own) is ticked on the page. Pepper in chat and MCP: five tools in `ai/tools/locations.js`, Create as a confirm card pinned to its opening. Usage counts towards the allowance and the person's daily share; one step at a time per opening (`busy_until`, 409). Tested: 10 API tests (`api/test/store-openings.test.mjs`: scope, answers, overrides, closer look, change, other tenant and viewer refused, busy, copy create scanned for any id of the template's, new create, areas without access, a failed scope kept as a draft, round limit, the chat tools with the confirm card), the engine against Postgres (copy-all and new-all, rolled back), 49 browser checks against a stand-in Claude (1280, 1015, 390, view only).

Share in the kiosk design editor (`ShareDialog`): venue (whose menu and prices), revenue stream, an optional last day, Make a link; the link with Copy, Open and a QR code, views and last opened, New link (the old one stops, views reset) and Stop sharing with inline confirms. The link (`kiosk.<domain>/?kiosk_share=<token>`, 24 random bytes) opens `SharePage`: the design's name, the group and venue, Upright / Wide, Start again, Full screen, the kiosk framed at 1080 x 1920 or 1920 x 1080 scaled to fit. The kiosk inside (`&frame=1`) loads `GET /pos-api/kiosk-share` (system mode by token, rate limited; 404 unknown / venue gone, 410 past its date), shows the saved design (posted designs are ignored) with the venue's live menu, never orders or pays (preview mode), never registers a service worker. Till setup's Kiosks tab marks shared designs. Design `layout.option_pictures` (default on) and `layout.option_align` (`left` | `center`, `OPTION_ALIGNS` in `shared/kiosk.js`), set in the designer's Choices panel (Words only beside Choice pictures, whose shape descriptions then talk about words; Line up the choices). `choiceLook(design)` in `pos/src/kiosk/displays.jsx` gives every display `tiles`, `pictures` and `center`; `OptionGroup.jsx` and `Builder.jsx` spread it and centre their heading / step rule. Words only: a card is a `Face` (name, level marks, description) above the price; round, oval and hexagon cards draw the face in that shape (`.k-opt-face`); a hexagon grid uses `HexTile words` (`.k-hex-words`, the name in the middle, the honeycomb unchanged); the list, slider and wheel leave their thumbnails and photos out; `splitImages()` still uses the pictures. - ✅ **Kiosks: every picture kept on the kiosk; a lost connection noticed in seconds** — the user's ask (19 Oct 2026), after asking whether the kiosk works offline: "1. go ahead [keep every menu photo], 2. on dropped live link do 2 more quick attempts before indicating offline, afterwards check every few seconds for restored connection". Pictures: `pos/src/kiosk/photos.js` `savePhotos()` loads, as `<img>` (so the service worker's picture rule sees them), three at a time, every picture from `configImages()` (`shared/kiosk.js`: logo, start pictures (not videos), every `image_url` / `logo_url` in the config, own origin only, each once, design first, at most `MAX_SAVED_PICTURES` 1800) that the `kiosk-images` cache doesn't have yet, after each config change, at boot and when the link comes back; the cache went to 2000 entries and a year (`pos/vite.config.js`). Connection (`kiosk/store.js`): `state.link` up | checking | down (`online` = not down); `linkTrouble()` on a closed or failed socket, a heartbeat with no pong (ping every 15 s, 6 s to answer, only once the server's `connected` message has `heartbeat: true`), a request with no reply (`fetchConfig()`: 5xx or none = no answer) or the browser's offline event: two quick tries (1 s, then 2 s, 4 s timeout each), then down and `keepChecking()` every 5 s; `linkOk()` on any answer (a link back from down reconnects the socket at once, sends the outbox, saves pictures); the online event checks at once. `config/ws.js` answers `{"type":"ping"}` with `{"type":"pong"}` on device sockets. Staff panel: Connection (Online / Checking / Offline) and Pictures on this kiosk. No migration. Tested: a shared test (`configImages`), the kiosk / device / installer API tests, 31 browser checks on a production build with its service worker and the API's WebSockets (29 of 29 pictures saved and counted, no second download, pictures never shown loading with the server unreachable and a never-saved one not, the menu's 20 dish pictures offline, an offline reload showing paused with the logo, the online event reopening in under 3 s, Checking at once and paused after about 3 s when the API stops, a try every 5 s, back within one try, pings answered, a frozen API (SIGSTOP) paused after about 30 s by the heartbeat and reopened after SIGCONT), the earlier 67 kiosk checks.
- ✅ **Kiosk preview: the menu with unpublished changes** *(migration 188)* — the user's report (19 Oct 2026): "i added images to menu, but cant see them in preview kiosk, do i need to publish menu changes to see in the preview, if yes, add option to see all changes as option". The preview (like the kiosks) reads the published menu versions, so dish and section photos, prices and new dishes only showed once published (option group pictures are tenant-wide and showed at once). The design editor's preview toolbar has **Published menu** / **Menu with unpublished changes** (`kd-menu-version`, remembered in `maca_kiosk_preview_menu`), a note naming the menus with changes not published (`menus_changed` from `menuChanges()` in `services/kioskSvc.js`, comparing `contentKey()` of each published and draft menu the preview can show) with Show the changes / Show the published menu, and **Reload** (a new preview link, after editing a menu in another tab). The choice is signed into the preview link (`m: 'draft'`); `kioskConfig()` honours it only for previews and returns `menu_version`; `tillMenus()` / `tillMenuData()` in `services/posSvc.js` take the version. - ✅ **Card machines: Dojo, Stripe Terminal and SumUp on the till and kiosks** *(migration 193)* — the user's ask (20 Oct 2026): "go ahead with card machines", the second of three steps (locations, card machines, the offline hub; devices are Android, cloud APIs). Till setup → Card machines: accounts per provider (secret key checked with the provider, sealed, `hint` only), machines per location (picked from the account, paired by code for Stripe and SumUp, or a Test card machine), recent payments with approved ones not on a bill marked. A till or kiosk picks its machine (`settings.card_reader`). The server drives every payment: `pos_card_payments` is written first, the provider called outside any transaction, the device polls; the minute sweep checks attempts nobody watches. Till Pay → Card machine (amount + tip, Cancel, declined with Try again, Dojo signature check, resume after a reload, Add it to the bill); taking a card machine payment off a bill and refunds of closed checks go to the card first. Kiosk: card step on the real machine (pays before its order exists: the attempt carries the payment id), declined offers counter; previews keep the pretend card. Rules: `card_integrated` payments, refunds and voids must name an approved attempt, matched by venue, check, payment, amount and tip, used once, checked by the sequencer; the hub's store refuses them. Migration 193 adds `card_integrated` beside `card_external` in Cash Recon takings maps and gives kiosks on the old test reader a Test card machine. Provider calls from the SDK types (Stripe 23.0.0, @sumup/sdk 0.2.0); Dojo from its documented shapes (its docs weren't reachable). Also: new locations don't copy a device's card machine (follow-up to add them), `httpError()` passes `code` and ids into the reply. Tested: 5 shared tests (shapes, voids, kiosk, every refusal, the sequencer using an attempt once and refusing a second check and a wrong amount; three mutations caught), 8 API tests (`card-machines.test.mjs` against stand-in Stripe, SumUp and Dojo servers: accounts and keys, readers and pairing, a Stripe till sale on the check once for the right amount, refusals and the refund permission, voiding through a card refund, SumUp and Dojo sales with signature, kiosk sales, the sweep, another tenant and roles; a mutation caught), the full API suite (151, sweep with a test machine on the fixtures' till), 37 browser checks (Till setup tab: an account, a machine, its list of payments, an operator reading payments only; till Pay: approved, declined then tried again, a payment picked up on reopening Pay, taking a payment off and refunding a closed check to the card with manager approval; kiosk: a declined card, then approved with an order number).

For receiving, if product prices have been amended, prompt user if the price quotes in the system should be updated, same for new products, for new products allow user to map them against existing ones (for example substition). we do also need a feature allowing a price variation for products, so we have inventory name, but price quote may have a slightly different name like a specific brand." **Product names**: `inv_quotes.product_name` (Inventory page quote form, import column, Pepper's price tools), copied onto PO lines, shown on the order form, the supplier switch, the PO page and the supplier's copy (`productOf()`). **Invoices**: Book in delivery → photos or a PDF → `POST /api/ai/invoices/read` (the order's lines as L refs, the supplier's other quotes as C refs; stored in `purchase_invoices` with the files) → the form fills: received quantities, Short / Substituted, invoice price per pack beside the ordered price (price viewers, editable), lines not on the invoice flagged, Not this line; invoice lines not ordered: one of the supplier's products added, others Add it (item + quote or pack as invoiced, new inventory item with setup), Came instead, Leave out (Save waits for a decision). The receive saves invoice_* on lines, adds lines ordered 0 (`added_on_receipt`), never changes ordered prices or totals; the supplier's copy leaves added lines out. **Price list**: with Purchasing setup manage, saving opens Update the price list from the invoice (`price_list` rows: price changed → everywhere / this venue / keep; new product → add with name, code, pack, optionally the usual one at the venue or everywhere; substitute → just this time / add as another product), `POST /pos/:id/price-list`, history source invoice with po_id; the same button stays on the PO page while anything differs. **Receipts**: Photograph the receipt in the expense forms (desktop day view and the phone) → `POST /api/ai/receipts/read` → description (shop: what), amount, VAT only when printed, card / cash, the venue's category by name; the photo becomes the receipt; on the phone a receipt from another day can go on that day. Price access: invoice prices are read and stored for everyone who books in, shown only to price viewers (`invoiceView()`, the receive takes `invoice_price` only from them). Also: the order form's last ordered ignores lines added on receipt; the catalog search takes `?all=1`. - ✅ **CI: the API test that failed one run in three; menu positions** — the user's question (24 Oct 2026): "Looks like last deployment failed?" The deploys had succeeded; the CI "API tests" job had failed four times since 23 Oct (#62, #65, #73, #74). Cause: `ItemBody` / `SectionBody` / `VariantBody` / `ItemGroupAttach` / `CalloutBody` in `routes/menus.js` defaulted `sort_order` to 0, so `upsertMenuTree()`'s `?? index` fallback never ran and a dish sent without a position tied with the first dish; `loadMenuFull()`'s `json_agg(DISTINCT ...)` returns tied dishes ordered by the jsonb (so by random id), and `kiosk-preview-menu.test.mjs`'s last test read `sections[0].items[0]` (the new unpriced dish about one run in three). Fixed: the five schemas take `sort_order` as optional (left out = place in the list), `loadMenuFull()` breaks ties by name then id, the test picks its dish by id, new `api/test/menu-order.test.mjs` (sections, dishes, options, groups, notes and the published copy keep the order sent; ties by name; both mutations caught). CI: `npm test` uses `--test-reporter=spec` (failing tests listed with their errors at the end) and the job sets the Postgres service's `log_min_messages` to fatal, because GitHub prints the service log after the tests and the sweep's expected errors buried the results (and the job log download is blocked from the build environment: only its tail can be read). No migration. Tested: the new test file, the kiosk preview test, the full API suite.

API (`routes/posSetup.js`): `FlowLink` schema, saved false when targets is empty, carried by `loadFlowLinks()` / `loadFlow()` / the setup page / copies both ways; `dropFlowTarget()` turns an Except link left with nothing into every dish instead of deleting it; `tidyBalanceBox()` clears it. - ✅ **Splitter: Smart sharing by speed, and a busy order type to its own station** — the user's ask (24 Oct 2026): "Splitter box - add few modes - smart splitter mode where traffic is adjusted based on time tickets on the next station take to bump, if one line falls behind, more tickets are sent to the other. the other mode is to switch to split by order type at specified treshold count of orders in specific type". A Splitter's settings gain `share_mode` (`SHARE_MODES`: pieces | speed) and a busy order type rule (`rush_type_id`, `rush_count` 1-99, `rush_station_id`). `balanceTicket(items, cfg, at, ko)` (the kitchen order passed at all three call sites) picks the output with the lowest `(load + pieces added) x pace`, a tie to the first after the last pick: pace 1 for pieces (the old rule), `stationPace()` for Smart (seconds per piece over a station's last `PACE_TICKETS` 8 bumps in the last `PACE_MINUTES` 30, each from the later of when it reached the station and the bump before it, `piecesAt()` counts, never under 1 s, unknown = the others' average by `fillPaces()`). `busyTypeStations()`: with the box linked to the rule's station and `typeWaiting()` (open orders of the type with a live dish to do at the box's outputs, not the order itself, plus one when the order is of the type) at `rush_count` or more, that type's tickets go only to its station and every other ticket to the others. The sequencer adds `cfg.balance.recent` only for a Smart box (`loadRecentKitchenOrders(PACE_MINUTES * 2)` in `services/posSvc.js`: kds_orders changed in that many minutes, bounded by created_at for the index); the bench passes its orders. API: `StationSettings` (type and station together), `checkSplitterRule()` (the tenant's order type; a bumping station of the venue, not the box; other kinds keep no Splitter settings), `dropBusyStation()` on delete or a kind that stops bumping; `flowWarnings()` warns when the rule's station isn't linked (the flow PUT loads station settings). Admin: `StationModal` How it shares + When one order type gets busy (`BusyTypeRule`, `linkedFrom()` lists linked stations first), the station row, `balanceRule()` / `busyRule()` in `KitchenFlow.jsx` (box note, link panel, Check a dish). No migration. Tested: 5 shared tests (pace, Smart picks 3 of 4 to the faster wingman, unknown pace, big tickets by speed, the busy rule on, off and across a second send and online orders, the warning, the sequencer reading recent orders only for Smart; six mutations caught), an API test through the sequencer and Postgres (validation incl. another tenant's type and station, a board, the box itself; Smart picks from past bumps stored in kds_orders; the busy rule on, off and on again; the warning; the rule cleared when its station becomes a board or is deleted; viewer 403, other tenant 404; four mutations caught), the full API suite, 31 browser checks (station window at 1280 / 1015 / 390: modes, rule, linked stations first, the unlinked note, save and read back, the list, the box note, the link panel, Check a dish, the warning, off again; view only).

Each dish or section choice in a combo step has `groups` (in the combo jsonb, stored only when set, never on a typed-in choice): per option group key (a library group id, or `variants` for the dish's one-off options) `use` all | off | only (`option_ids`) | swap (`group_id`, a library group asked instead), with optional `min` / `max`. Saving: `cleanDealGroups()` keeps only groups the choice's dishes have (a section: any of its dishes), only option ids they offer, and a swap group in the tenant's library; a group taken off the dish drops out on the next save. Admin: Options in this deal under each choice in Edit steps (`DealOptionsButton` with a summary), the window (`DealOptionsModal`) with a row per group (As on the dish / Leave out / Only some with option chips, the last one locked / Another group with Make a copy through `POST /menus/variant-groups/:id/copy`), How many, On the dish / In the deal lines, View users read only. - ✅ **Purchasing: add items to an order that hasn't been sent** — the user's ask (25 Oct 2026): "purchasing, for orders that are not sent, please allow user to add items". Adding follows the rule for changing a PO's lines: Purchasing approval (`purchasing_approve` manage), while the PO is `to_send`. API (`PUT /pos/:id/lines` `add`, which existed with no screen): quotes come from `loadItemQuotes()` (active quotes of active suppliers) and must be the PO's supplier's (else 422), a switched-off item is refused, and a product already on the order (same `quote_id`, not `added_on_receipt`) tops its line up to `fitQty(existing + added)` instead of making a second line (also for the same product twice in one request, and for a line taken off then added back, which gets a new line); new lines copy pack, price, VAT, code and product name as at ordering. `GET /venues/:id/catalog?supplier_id=` narrows the items to that supplier's (`all=1` ignored with it, 400 for a bad id); each item's `quotes` still holds every supplier's, so callers filter by `supplier_id`. Admin (`PoDetail` in `ordering.jsx`): **Add items** (`po-add-items`) beside Change quantities, both through `startEdit()`; `AddPoItemsModal` (search with a 250 ms pause, one row per pack of the supplier with code, product name, pack and the venue's price for price viewers, On the order rows disabled, tap to add / take out, Done (n to add), first 40 with a note), new lines (`po-new-line`, `NewLineBadge`, stepper in the quote's `order_multiple` steps, `qtyProblem()` hint, X to drop) below the existing ones, draft totals from `orderTotals()` with Before these changes (`po-total-before`), the action row (Email, Mark as sent, Split, Cancel) hidden while editing; Save sends `lines` + `add`. Pepper's `change_purchase_order` (`poChangePlan()`) searches `catalog?supplier_id=` (it used to search every supplier's items and filter after the 40 limit, so a product could be missed) and its card says when a product tops up a line already on the order. Fixtures add an item to the split PO, so the sweep replays the add as the other tenant. No migration. Tested: `api/test/po-add-items.test.mjs` (6 tests: the catalogue per supplier, the copied fields and totals, top-ups, refusals for another supplier's, another tenant's, unknown and switched-off products, 0, a viewer, ordering without approval, another tenant and a sent PO, approval without prices storing the price, Pepper's cards; four mutations caught), the full API suite (217), 51 browser checks (owner at 1280: the picker's rows and search, add and take out, minimum and steps, line prices, totals before and after, Mark as sent hidden, X, a line at 0, save, reopen; Cancel; a sent order; ordering without approval, approval without prices, a viewer; phone 390 with the picker as a sheet; 1015), five UI mutations caught.
- ✅ **Kiosk designs: bottom bar and side menu colours** — the user's ask (25 Oct 2026): "i have no control over bottom bar bg colour, need it in kioks theme setting, also need separate left menu bg colour control" (their dark design showed a light grey bar: the bar was always drawn in the Text colour with Page words, the side menu in the Cards colour). `brand.colors` gains `bar`, `bar_text`, `rail`, `rail_text` (`PART_COLORS` in `shared/kiosk.js`; a colour code or null = not set, never See-through; `COLOR_LABELS` Bottom bar, Bottom bar text, Side menu, Side menu text; `PART_FOLLOWS`). `partColours(colors)` is the one rule: each as set, else the bar follows Text and the side menu Cards (See-through with see-through cards), words not set take whichever of Page and Text has the higher contrast on their part (`contrast()` from `shared/themeKit.js`, now imported by `shared/kiosk.js`; a tie keeps the old Page on the bar / Text on the side menu), so nothing set = exactly the old look. `KioskApp.jsx` sets `--k-bar`, `--k-bar-text`, `--k-rail`, `--k-rail-text`; kiosk.css: `.k-bar` (every bottom bar: menu, order, details, paying, allergens), `.k-bar .k-btn-back` (solid in the words colour, the bar colour for its words), `.k-bar .k-btn-onbar` and the hexagon bar tint from the words colour, `.k-rail` (class on `Menu.jsx`'s nav, was `bg-[var(--k-surface)]`), `.k-rail-on` with `--k-rail-on` (12% primary on the side menu's colour, was `--k-soft`, which mixes into Cards). A dish's own page (`AddBar`, the combo bar) and the top bar stay in Cards. Designer (`BrandPanel`): a Bottom bar and side menu card under the colours (`ColourPicker optional`, placeholder Same as Text / Same as Cards / Automatic, new `fallback` prop = the colour in use in the colour box, Reset), `PART_PAIRS` warnings only once a pair is set (the side menu only when the design has one), a note when the design has no side menu, Brand summary own bottom bar / own side menu. Colour sets and `kioskBrandFromKit()` (theme maker, Use the brand) don't carry them, so they follow the new colours. No migration. Tested: a shared test (the old look for two designs, set / dropped values, the better of the two words colours over six bars, set words kept, see-through cards; three mutations caught) and the theme kit test (a kit starts them over), `api/test/kiosk-design.test.mjs` (saved in lower case, bad words dropped, a copy and the kiosk config get them, cleared), 63 kiosk browser checks (upright and wide: the old look for two designs, a bar colour with automatic words and its buttons, the order and allergens bars, the dish page keeping Cards, a yellow bar's dark words, the side menu and its picked tint, set words, section tiles Back and the hexagon tint, a standard design's Back as before; five mutations caught), 42 designer checks (placeholders and colour boxes, the preview following, automatic words, warning and Reset, summary, save and reload, Reset and a colour set starting over, no side menu note, 1015 / 390, View only; three mutations caught).
- ✅ **Kitchen screens: ticket colours, dish names by section, a packing check at the pass** *(migration 207)* — the user's ask (25 Oct 2026): "kds improvements - i want to be able to give items font colours by category, also be able to set bg colour per status (like now when i tick individual items on prep screen, but able to choose colour myself), also on expo screen i want to be able to tick off individual items and dont bump off ticket on ticking off all (like a packing check) - (add setting to enable bump off on all checked if user chooses so)". **Colours**: `pos_settings.kds_colours` `{ states, sections: [{ name, colour }] }`, one per venue, set on Store tech → Kitchen screens → Ticket colours (`TicketColoursCard`: a `ColourMenuButton` chip per state, Standard colour to clear, a preview ticket drawn with the same `ticketColours()` as the screen, sections picked from the venue's menu section names, one Save). Twelve states (`KITCHEN_COLOURS`): dish rows todo / ticked / upcoming / held / voided, ticket tops head / head_new / head_rush / head_prepared / head_checked, timer_warn / timer_late; a state not set keeps the old look exactly; a set colour gets black or white words (`inkOn()`, luminance 0.6). Sections match by name (`sectionKey()`: case and spaces ignored) and reach the screen as section ids (`sectionColoursById()` in `kdsConfig()`, over the draft tables and the live versions, so a section renamed before publishing keeps its colour by either name); kitchen items and meal deal parts now carry `section_id` (a typed-in part takes the combo's). The kitchen screen draws rows, tops, the RUSH chip and the timer with inline styles (`rowLook()`; head: rush > new > checked > prepared), dish and part names in their section colour except voided rows; info lines keep their tints on dark rows and take the row's words on light ones. **Packing check**: at an expo or runner a tap sends the new device event `kitchen.item_checked` (`item.checked[station]`), which never moves the dish: the ticket shows N ticked, then CHECKED with the head_checked top, and waits for READY / DONE. Ticking a dish still upcoming finishes it before (`finishBefore()`), as the pass's bump would. Station setting `bump_when_checked` (Edit station → Ticking off dishes, expo / runner): the tick that leaves every bumpable dish ticked runs `bumpStation()` (the old `kitchen.bumped` body, now shared) with `by_items: true`. Ticks go with a remake and when the dish is sent back upstream (an undo or a recalled bump at an earlier station: `untickAfter()`); READY then Prev at the pass keeps them. Prep stations still mark dishes done; older app copies sending `item_done` at the pass keep working. The bench shows ticked off at the pass in its Orders panel. A new location copies the colours. Tested: 4 new and one extended shared kitchen tests + 4 colour tests, `api/test/kitchen-colours.test.mjs` (2 tests through the sequencer and Postgres: save, stored cleaned, 400s, viewer, other tenant, config by section id incl. a draft rename, ticks without a bump, prep refused, the setting's validation, the last tick bumping with its kds_log row) and the store-openings copy; 18 shared / API mutations caught, the full API suite (220); browser: the test bench in three modes (12 standard, 34 colours incl. the amber timer after a minute, 22 last-tick bump) and 45 admin checks (card, preview, sections, Undo, save, reload, station choice, 1280 / 1015 / 390, View only), 5 UI mutations caught.
- ✅ **Kiosk designs: fit each picture to its space** — the user's ask (26 Oct 2026, with a screenshot of a logo banner cut short at the bottom): "need conntrols to fit image". Banners were always `object-cover` in a 16:7 box, so a logo or poster lost its edges. Every home page banner, featured tile, start screen picture or video and reach picture now carries `fit` `{ mode, focus, size, bg }` (`pictureFit()` in `shared/kiosk.js`): `auto` (the default: `contain` for a see-through picture, `cover` for anything else, so saved designs keep their look, see-through featured pictures included), `cover` (fill, cropped, `focus` kept in view) or `contain` (the whole picture, `size` 30-100% of the space, placed at `focus`, on `bg` none | surface | primary | accent | text). `fitLayout(fit, clear)` gives the styles and `FitPicture` (`pos/src/kiosk/parts.jsx`) draws them with `useClear()` for Automatic and the design colour vars behind; banners and featured tiles (`Menu.jsx`), start screen media and the reach band (`Media` in `Start.jsx`) use it. `menu.banner_shape` wide (16:7, as before) | short (3:1) | tall (16:9) | picture (each banner the ratio of its own picture, measured on load); the banner keeps its 36vh cap, so on a wide screen the cap usually sets the height. Designer: `PictureFit` under each banner, featured tile with a picture, start screen and reach item (a Fit summary opening Automatic / Fill / Whole picture, a 3 x 3 position grid, Size and Behind it with the design's colours as swatches), the Banner shape row in the Menu panel once there is a banner, the shape in the Menu summary. No migration. So need the kiosk to render not mobile or zoomed out", and "visual fixes to the combo render - the first variant on the left, the second on the right, third left most, 4th right most, and so on. Main item always centre. Of course do not apply to variant rendering over the item image". **Zoom**: kiosk.css sizes follow vmin with finger-sized minimums, which take over on a phone. `kioskScale(w, h)` in `shared/kiosk.js` (`KIOSK_MIN_SHORT` 800: 1 at or above it, else short / 800 rounded down to 3 decimals, never under 0.1) and `useKioskViewport()` in `pos/src/kiosk/viewport.js` (called first in `KioskApp`, a layout effect, top window only): the viewport tag gets `width=device-width, initial-scale=k, minimum-scale=k, maximum-scale=k, user-scalable=no, viewport-fit=cover`, so the browser lays the page out at the visible size / k (a 412 x 915 phone: 800 x ~1553 upright, ~1553 wide on its side with the same k, the screen's sides swapping), re-read on resize; every way out of the kiosk (Disconnect, a removed device, a move) reloads or leaves the page, which brings back `pos/index.html`'s tag, and the effect's cleanup puts it back should the kiosk app close within the page. Tablets, kiosk screens, desktop browsers (which ignore the tag), frames (designer preview, shared page's kiosk), the share page, the pairing screen, the till and the kitchen screen are unchanged. The staff panel gained a Screen line (`k-staff-screen`: size and zoom) and its Card machine line (`k-staff-card`) now names `config.card_machine` (it read the device's old `card_reader === 'test'` and said None for every machine since migration 193). **Meal picture**: `bundleLayout()` puts sides in the order picked left, right, further left, further right (was right first), and centres the main(s) across (no main: the whole), sized from the wider half so one side leaves the main in the middle at the size it would have with a side each way; top to bottom the whole is still centred. Dips in front (smalls, in the order picked) and picks drawn over a dish's own photo (`PickPhoto`, `CornerPicks`) are unchanged. No migration. Tested: 2 shared tests (the small-screen scale; the meal picture test rewritten: left then right, the main in the middle with one, two, three and four sides and with two mains, still inside every box shape), 38 browser checks in Chromium's mobile mode (phone upright: tag, 800 wide at 0.515, text 16.4px, the bottom bar's children side by side with the total clear of View order; turned with the screen's sides swapped: same scale, 1553 wide; opened on its side; a 810 x 1440 tablet and a desktop browser unchanged; inside a frame the kiosk's own tag and the page around it unchanged; a shared page and its framed kiosk unchanged; the pairing screen unchanged, a kiosk linked from it zoomed out with the staff panel's Screen line and its test card machine named, back to the pairing screen with the tag as it was after Disconnect; the hook mounted and unmounted on its own putting the tag back; the meal picture on the phone, upright and wide: the main alone in the middle, one sauce on the left with the main still in the middle, the second on the right), 14 mutations caught (6 shared, 8 kiosk), and the Help and Docs text rendered (7 checks).

It posts `{ name, settings: the normalised draft }` to the existing `POST /api/pos/kiosk-designs`, so `ownDesign()` and the 50-design limit apply and their refusal shows in the window. `savedAs()` puts the new row into the `['kiosk-designs']` query (share null) so the editor never shows "doesn't exist" while the list reloads, invalidates it and the setup, resets the save mutation and the leave confirm, and navigates to the new design; the editor stays mounted, and its load effect keys on the row id (`loadedFor`), so the draft, the saved copy and the dirty check move to the new design, and the preview link is made again (its query key has the id). The original keeps its saved settings and its share link. Also fixed in Docs: the designer's address (`/pos/kiosks/designs/:id` since migration 202). No API change, no migration. - ✅ **Kiosk analytics: every guest visit recorded, summed up and played back on the kiosk** *(migration 208)* — the user's ask (26 Oct 2026): "For the kiosk, build analytics suite where capture not only transactional data but also behavioral data. We need a stream capturing interaction with different elements, dwell time, cancellations, etc. Basically all info needed to recreate a step by step journey using kiosk mock up." **Recording** (`pos/src/kiosk/journey.js`): after every store change the kiosk works out a flat view of what it shows (`viewOf()`: screen, section, order type, language, reach, allergen picks, basket, the dish being built, the name and tent as lengths only, the paying method, the result, errors, the link state, the basket value, and `ui:<key>` for screens' own state through `useJourneyState()`: a dish's picks and quantity, a combo's picks, page and opened steps, option groups, the Start again, language and allergens sheets, the idle warning, the card step) and keeps the difference as a `v` event; touches (`tap`: place, the element's words after the change, test id, item / section / option id, hit 1 / 0 nothing answers / 2 disabled), drags (`swipe`), scrolling (`scroll`, the box by its child-index path from `.k-root`), the viewport (`vp`) and the end (`end`: done, guest, timeout, left after 45 s untouched on the start screen, interrupted after a reload, kept in `kiosk_journey_open`). `restart(why)` sets `ended`. Nothing typed: keyboard and number pad touches (`data-jprivate`) at the keyboard's middle, a sheet's backdrop as outside the window. Finished visits queue in localStorage `kiosk_journeys` (40, 2.5M characters) and go in batches of 5 to `POST /pos-api/kiosk/journeys`, idempotent by the visit's ULID; a 400 / 413 drops the batch. Previews, share links and replays never record; a design can switch it off (`behaviour.record_journeys`). **Storing**: `kiosk_sessions` (forced RLS; summary columns plus summary / names / viewport / events jsonb); `cleanVisit()` checks the shape and clamps times into order, `saveVisits()` works the summary out itself (`summariseJourney()`) and keeps a design or stream id only when it is the tenant's (the stream also the venue's); events cleared after 90 days, rows after 400 (hourly). **Report** (`/api/pos/kiosk-analytics`, Till reports view): overview (`aggregateJourneys()`: tiles, the funnel through `STAGES`, outcomes, where guests gave up, time per screen with a dish being built as its own screen, changes of mind, dishes with views / view time / adds / removals / left, by hour, by kiosk; up to 92 days, 20,000 visits), visits newest first (total from `count(*) OVER ()`), one visit with a replay link (a kiosk preview link with `&replay=1` for its design at its venue and stream, the kiosk's design now when that was deleted; notes when the design changed or the events are cleared). **Playback**: the kiosk app in replay mode (`bootReplay()`, `listenForReplay()`) takes `kiosk.replay { view, scroll }` from the admin player, shows the view (the name as dots), hands screens' own state back through `useJourneyState()`, puts scroll boxes back, freezes the order number countdown and the idle warning (`s.replay`), covers itself against touches, and answers which dishes are no longer on the menu. The admin page (`pages/KioskAnalytics.jsx`) scales the frame to the visit's viewport, draws touches (red for nothing that answers), Play / Pause, the slider, step back / forward, 1x-8x, Skip long pauses, the steps in words with their dwell times (`journeySteps()`: a touch folds into the change it caused within 700 ms, a dead touch never does; typing and scrolling fold into one step; going back to the start screen isn't counted as removals), This visit. Nav link Kiosk analytics after Till reports (migration 208 for existing menus). A void order can't ask (422); a direct send on a paid check clears any address still waiting. Kiosk `Done`: `receipt.sent` `waiting` with `receipt_waiting` ("We'll email your receipt to s***@gmail.com once you've paid at the counter"), the sheet's `receipt_email_hint_counter`, both in nine languages; the preview's example answers by `state.method`.
